# 2.1 水体污染与监测

# Water pollution and monitoring

# 2.1.1 水体及水污染

# Water and water contamination

- 水体是地表水、地下水及其包含的底质、水中生物等的总称
- Water body is a general term for surface water, groundwater, and the sediments and aquatic organisms they contain.
- 水资源短缺，可利用的淡水资源仅江河、淡水湖及地下水的一部分
- Water is scarce and only part of freshwater in rivers, lakes and groundwater is available.

| Seawater | Available   |
| -------- | ----------- |
| 海 水,     | Freshwater  |
| 97.30%   | 淡 水         |
| 1%       | unavailable |
| 2.70%    | 不可利用        |
|          | 99%         |

# 水质监测的目的

# Purposes of water quality monitoring

# (1) 地表水——经常性监测

# Surface water - regular monitoring

对江、河、水库、湖泊、海洋等地表水和地下水中的污染因子进行经常性的监测，以掌握水质现状及其变化趋势。

"Regular monitoring of pollution factors in surface water and groundwater, such as rivers, lakes, reservoirs, oceans, etc., to grasp the current state of water quality and its trends.

# (2) 生产和生活过程——监视性监测

# Production and life processes - surveillance monitoring

对生产、生活等废（污）水排放源排放的废（污）水进行监视性监测，掌握废（污）水排放量及其污染物浓度和排放总量，评价是否符合排放标准，为污染源管理提供依据。

Surveillance monitoring of wastewater from production and domestic discharge sources, grasp the volume of wastewater discharge and the concentration and total discharge of pollutants, assess compliance with discharge standards, and provide a basis for pollution source management.

# (3) 事故监测——应急监测

# Accident monitoring - emergency monitoring

对水环境污染事故进行应急监测，为分析判断事故原因、危害及制订对策提供依据。

Emergency monitoring of water environmental pollution incidents to provide a basis for analyzing and judging the causes and harms of the incidents and for formulating countermeasures.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 14】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 14】

# 水质监测的目的

# (4) 环境管理——提供数据和资料

为国家政府部门制定水环境保护标准、法规和规划提供有关数据和资料。

Provide relevant data and materials for national government departments to formulate water environmental protection standards, regulations, and plans.

# (5) 环境科学研究——提供数据和资料

为开展水环境质量评价和预测预报及进行环境科学研究提供基础数据和技术手段。

Provide data and methodologies for water environmental quality assessment, and conducting environmental research.

# (6) 环境污染纠纷——仲裁监测

为判断纠纷原因提供科学依据

Provide scientific evidences for judgement.

# 2.1.2 监测项目（主要项目）

# Monitoring items

# （一）地表水监测项目 Surface water

基本项目：水温、pH值、溶解氧、高锰酸盐指数、化学需氧量、五日生化需氧量、氨氮、总氮（湖、库）、总磷、铜、锌、硒、砷、汞、镉、铅、铬（六价）、氟化物、氰化物、硫化物、挥发酚、石油类、阴离子表面活性剂、粪大肠菌群。

Basic items: water temperature, pH, dissolved oxygen (DO), permanganate index (CODMn), chemical oxygen demand (COD), biochemical oxygen demand (BOD₅), ammonia nitrogen, total nitrogen (lake, reservoir), total phosphorus, copper, zinc, selenium, arsenic, mercury, cadmium, lead, chromium (hexavalent), fluoride, cyanide, sulfide, volatile phenol, petroleum, anionic surfactant, fecal coliform.

# Groundwater

# 地下水环境监测技术规范

# Technical specifications for environmental monitoring of groundwater

标准号：HJ164-2020代替HJ/T164-2004

2021-03-01实施

字号：[大][中][小] [打印]

为贯彻《中华人民共和国环境保护法》和《中华人民共和国水污染防治法》，保护地下水环境，防止地下水污染，保障人体健康，制定本标准。本标准规定了地下水环境监测点布设、环境监测井建设与管理、样品采集与保存、监测项目和分析方法、监测数据处理、质量保证和质量控制以及资料整编等方面的要求。

# 地下水环境监测技术规范（HJ164-2020代替HJ/T164-2004）

# 2.1.3 废(污)水监测项目 Wastewater

# 第一类 Class I：

在车间或车间处理设施排放口采样测定的污染物，包括总汞、烷基汞、总镉、总铬、六价铬、总砷、总铅、总镍、苯并（a）芘、总铍、总银、总α放射性、总β放射性。

Contaminants measured at the discharge point of the treatment facilities or the workshop, including total mercury, alkyl mercury, total cadmium, total chromium, hexavalent chromium, total arsenic, total lead, total nickel, benzo(a)pyrene, total beryllium, total silver, total alpha radioactivity, total beta radioactivity.

# 第二类 Class II：

在排污单位排放口采样测定的污染物，包括pH、色度、悬浮物、生化需氧量、化学需氧量、石油类、动植物油、挥发性酚、总氰化物、硫化物、氨氮、氟化物、磷酸盐、甲醛、苯胺类、硝基苯类、阴离子表面活性剂、总铜、总锌、总锰。

Contaminants measured at the point of wastewater discharging, including pH, color, suspended solids, biochemical oxygen demand, chemical oxygen demand, petroleum, animal and vegetable oil, volatile phenol, total cyanide, sulfide, ammonia nitrogen, fluorine Compounds, phosphates, formaldehyde, anilines, nitrobenzenes, anionic surfactants, total copper, total zinc, total manganese.

# 2.1.4 水质监测分析方法

# Analytical methods for water quality monitoring

选择分析方法遵循的原则：

- 灵敏度能满足定量要求
- 方法成熟、准确
- 操作简单，易于普及
- 抗干扰能力好

The principles for analytical methods:

- sensitivity meets the quantitative requirements;
- mature and accurate;
- simple to operate;
- easy to popularize;
- good ability;

水体的不同 + 污染物不同 经多个单位实验证验证，但欠成熟

# 2.1.4 水质监测分析方法

# Analytical methods for water quality monitoring

# ❖监测分析方法的分类 Classification

1. # 用于测定无机污染物的方法 Methods for inorganic pollutants

- 化学分析方法：重量法、容量法等

Chemical: gravimetric, volumetric, etc.
- 原子吸收光谱法：冷原子吸收光谱法、火焰原子吸收光谱法、石墨炉原子吸收光谱法，测多种微量、痕量金属元素

Atomic absorption spectrometry: cold atomic absorption spectrometry, flame atomic absorption spectrometry, graphite furnace atomic absorption spectrometry, measurement of trace metals
- 分光光度法：紫外、可见和红外分光光度法，测定多种金属、非金属离子或化合物，常规监测

Spectrophotometry: UV/Visible/Infra-red, metals, non-metal ions or compounds, regular monitoring
- 等离子发射光谱（ICP-OES）法 ：多种元素同时测定

Plasma emission spectroscopy (ICP-OES) method: simultaneous determination of multiple elements

# 2.1.4 水质监测分析方法

# Analytical methods for water quality monitoring

# ❖监测分析方法的分类 Classification

1. # 用于测定无机污染物的方法 Methods for inorganic pollutants

- 电化学法：电位分析法、近代的极谱分析法、库仑滴定法，常规监测

Electrochemical methods: Potentiometry, polarography, coulometric titration, routine monitoring
- 离子色谱法：连续测定多种离子

Ion chromatography: continuous determination of multiple ions
- 其他方法：原子荧光光谱法、电感耦合等离子发射光谱-质谱（ICP-MS）法、气相分子吸收光谱法等。

Others: atomic fluorescence spectrometry (AFS), inductively coupled plasma emission spectroscopy-mass spectrometry (ICP-MS), gas-phase molecular absorption spectroscopy (GMA).

# 2.1.5 污染物形态分析

# Speciation analysis of pollutants

# 1.污染物形态

污染物形态是指污染物在环境中呈现的化学状态、价态和异构状态。一定形态的污染物在环境中有其发生和演变过程，并且在不同的条件下可转变为其他形态，具有不同强度的毒性。

The speciation of pollutants refers to the chemical state, valence state and isoform of the pollutants in the environment. Pollutants with one type of speciation may occur, and/or change to another forms. The toxicity is different for pollutants with different speciation.

# 2.了解污染物形态的意义

- ⮚深入认识其环境行为 understanding the environmental behavior
- ⮚正确评价对环境的影响 accurate assessment of the environmental impact
- ⮚设计污染物分析监测方案和治理方法 monitoring plan and treatment method

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 30】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 30】

# 2.1.5 污染物形态分析

# Speciation analysis of pollutants

# 3. 污染物形态分析常用的方法

# Methods for speciation analysis

- ▪直接测定法 Direct analysis

专一性的化学方法或物理化学方法，测定样品中污染物的各种形态，如用离子选择电极法测定离子态元素。

Specific chemical or physicochemical methods: ion-selective electrode methods for the determination of ionic elements.
- ▪分离测定法 Separation assay

是将样品中不同形态的待测组分用物理法（离心、超滤、渗析等）或物理化学法（萃取、层析、离子交换等）先进行分离，然后逐一测定。

Using physical methods (centrifugation, ultrafiltration, dialysis, etc.) or physic-chemical methods (extraction, chromatography, ion exchange, etc.) for separation.
- ▪干法 Dry method

用电子探针、X线衍射仪、核磁共振波谱仪等对颗粒状样品或生物样品进行非破坏性的形态分析。

Non-destructive morphological analysis of granular or biological samples by electron probes, X-ray diffractometers (XRD), nuclear magnetic resonance spectrometers (NMR).
- ▪理论计算法 Theoretical calculation

是利用被研究体系有关热力学数据进行计算，确定其形态的方法。

thermodynamic data

# 监测方案制订要求

# Requirements for the formulation of monitoring program

- (1) 明确地，具体地规定 监测目的; Specify the purpose of monitoring
- (2) 确定监测介质和监测项目，以此选择分析方法，前后统一，使监测数据具有可比性; Ascertain the medium and items for monitoring, then select the analysis methods which needs to be unified to make the monitoring data comparable.
- (3) 规定采样地点、方法，时间和频次，并具体到责任人; Specify the sampling location, method, time and frequency, and the responsible person.
- (4) 明确排放特点、自然环境条件、居民分布情况等，据此确定采样设备、交通公具及运行路线; Be clear of discharge characteristics, natural environmental conditions, and distribution of residents, etc. Then determine right sampling equipment and routes.
- (5) 对监测结果尽可能提出定量要求，如监测项目结果的表示方法、有效数字的位数及可疑数据的取舍等; Provide requirements for monitoring data, i.e.: representations of results, significant digit, and the deletion of suspicious data.

# 2.2.1 地表水水质监测方案的制订

# Formulation of monitoring program for surface water quality

- 定义：流过或汇集在地球表面上的水，如海洋、河流、湖泊、水库、沟渠中的水统称为地表水。
- Definition: water on the surface of the Earth, such as oceans, rivers, lakes, reservoirs, and ditch.

# 步骤 steps：

1. (1) 基础资料的收集 Collection of background information
2. (2) 监测断面和采样点的布设 Determination of monitoring sections and sampling points
3. (3) 采样时间和采样频率的确定 Determination of sampling time and frequency
4. (4) 采样及监测技术的选择 Selection of sampling and monitoring technologies
5. (5) 结果表达、质量保证及实施计划 Results expression, quality assurance and implementation plan

# 2.2.1 地表水水质监测方案的制订

# Formulation of monitoring program for surface water quality

- 基础资料的收集
Background information
1. 水体的水文、气候、地质和地貌资料。如水位、水量、流速及流向的变化；降
雨量、蒸发量及历史上的水情；河流的宽度、深度、河床结构及地质状况；湖
泊沉积物的特性、间温层分布、等深线等。

Hydrological, climatic, geological and geomorphological information: changes in water level and quantity,
changes in flow rate and direction; rainfall, evaporation, water regime, river width and depth, riverbed structure and geology; characteristics of lake sediments, inter-temperature layer distribution, isobath, etc.
2. 水体沿岸城市分布、工业布局、污染源及其排污情况、城市给排水情况等。

Distribution of cities, industries, pollution sources, sewage discharge, urban water supply and drainage
3. 水体沿岸的资源现状和水资源的用途；饮用水源分布和重点水源保护区；水体
流域土地功能及近期使用计划等。

Usage and status of water resources; distribution of drinking water sources and key water source protection areas; land usage of watershed.
4. 历年水质监测资料。

water quality monitoring data previously

# 2.2.1 地表水水质监测方案的制订

# Formulation of monitoring program for surface water quality

# 监测断面和采样点的布设

# Determination of monitoring section and sampling point

1. # 监测断面布设原则

# Criteria for monitoring:

1. 应在水质、水量发生变化及水体不同用途的功能区处设置监测断面

places where water quality, quantity, and usage change
2. 大量废水排入河流的居民区、工业区上下游；

upstream and downstream of residential and industrial areas around the discharging point
3. 湖泊、水库的主要出入口；

entrance and exit of lakes and reservoirs
4. 饮用水源区、水资源区域等功能区；

Areas like drinking water source and water resources
5. 入海河流的河口处、较大支流汇合口上游和汇合后与干流混合处；

The estuary of the river into the sea, rivers confluence
6. 国际河流出入国际线的出入口处；

Entrance and exit for International rivers
7. 尽可能与水文测量断面重合

consistent with the hydrographic survey section.

# 2.2.1 地表水水质监测方案的制订

# Formulation of monitoring program for surface water quality

# 监测断面和采样点的布设

# Determination of monitoring section and sampling point

# 2) 河流监测断面和采样点的布设

# monitoring section and sampling point for rivers

为评价完整江河水系的水质，需要设置背景断面、对照断面、控制断面和削减断面；对于某一河段，只需设置对照、控制和削减三种断面。

To evaluate the water quality of a river system, it is necessary to set the background section, the check section, the control section and the cutting down section.

# 背景断面：

设在基本上未受人类活动影响的河段，用于评价一完整水系污染程度。

Background section: a section of the river that is unaffected by human activities. It was used to evaluate the degree of pollution of a complete water system.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 38】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 38】

# 监测断面和采样点的布设

# 1) 河流监测断面和采样点的布设

对照断面：为了解流入监测河段前的水体水质状况而设置。一个河段一般只设一个对照断面。

Check section: To understand the quality of the water before flowing into the monitoring section. Generally one check section for a river.

控制断面：控制断面的数目应根据城市的工业布局和排污口分布情况而定，设在排污区（口）下游，污水与河水基本混匀处。流经特殊要求地区（饮用水源地、风景游览区、严重水土流失区及地球化学异常区）的河段上也应该设置。

Control section: Determined according to the distribution of industries and discharging points. Located at downstream of the discharging point area where the wastewater and river water are completely mixed or specific areas like drinking water sources, scenic area, etc.

削减断面：是指河流受纳废水和污水后，经稀释扩散和自净作用，使污染物浓度显著降低的断面，通常设在城市或工业区最后一个排污口下游1500m以外的河段上。

Cutting down section: located at 1500m downstream of the last discharging point of the city or industrial zone.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 39】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 39】

# G-G’削减断面 cutting down section

# B-B’、C-C’、D-D’、F-F’控制断面 control section

| -- | ------ | ---------------- | ----------------- | -------------- | -- | ----- | -- |

# 监测断面和采样点的布设

# Determination of monitoring section and sampling point

# 1) 湖泊、水库监测垂线（或断面）和采样点的布设

Vertical lines (or sections) and sampling points for lakes and reservoirs

湖泊、水库通常只设监测垂线，当水体复杂时，可参照河流的有关规定设置监测断面。

Only vertical lines for monitoring of Lakes and reservoirs. When the water body is complex, the monitoring section can be set up based on river.

# (1)

在湖（库）的不同水域，按照水体类别和功能设置监测垂线

The vertical lines should be set in areas with different water types and functions.

# (2)

如无明显功能区别，网格法均匀设置监测垂线

The vertical lines should be set evenly if there is no obvious differences in the water functions.

# (3)

受污染影响较大的重要湖泊、水库，在污染物主要输送路线上设置控制断面

Control sections should be set across the route for pollutants transportation when the lakes and reservoirs are heavily polluted.

# 4) 海洋

污染物在较大海域分布不均匀; Contaminants are unevenly distributed in large sea areas

局部海域相对均匀; Partially uniform.

污染区、过渡区、对照区; Contaminated area, transition area, contrast area

监测断面、监测垂线; Monitoring section, monitoring vertical line

# 监测断面和采样点的布设

# Determination of monitoring section and sampling point

设置监测断面后，应根据水面的宽度确定断面上的采样垂线，再根据采样垂线处水深确定采样点的数目和位置。

After setting the monitoring section, the sampling vertical line on the section should be determined according to the width of the water surface, and then the number and position of the sampling points should be determined according to the water depth at the sampling vertical line.

# 采样时间和采样频率的确定

# Determination of sampling time and frequency

1. 饮用水源地每月至少采样监测1次，采样时间根据具体情况选。

The source of drinking water should be sampled and monitored at least once a month. The sampling time should be selected according to the specific situation.
2. 对于较大水系干流和中、小河流，单月采样监测1次，全年采样监测次数不少于6次。采样时间为丰水期、枯水期和平水期，每期采样2次。流经城市或工业区，污染较重的河流，酌情增加。底质每年枯水期采样监测1次。

For larger water systems, the sampling of main stream, medium and small rivers should be monitored once a month. The number of sampling and monitoring in the whole year should be not less than 6 times. The sampling time should be twice in each season: wet, dry and the flat water period. But frequency should be increased for rivers flowing through urban or industrial areas or heavily polluted. The sediment should be sampled once a year during the dry season.
3. 受潮汐影响的监测断面，分别在大潮期、小潮期进行采样监测，每次应采集当天涨、退潮水样分别测定。

The monitoring sections affected by the tides should be sampled and monitored during the spring tide period and the neap tide period, in the same day.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 45】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 45】

# 采样时间和采样频率的确定

# Determination of sampling time and frequency

1. 国家监控断面或垂线：每月采样监测1次，在每月5-10日进行。

National monitoring section or vertical line: sampling and monitoring once per month, date 5-10 per month.
2. 背景断面：每年采样监测1次，在污染可能较重的季节进行。

Background section: Sampling and monitoring once a year, in the season when pollution is likely to be serious.
3. 海水水质常规监测：每年按丰水期、平水期、枯水期或季节采样监测2-4次

Routine monitoring of seawater quality: 2-4 times in a year in wet season, flat water period, dry season.

# 采样及监测技术的选择

# Selection of sampling and monitoring technology

根据监测对象的性质、含量范围及测定要求等因素选择适宜的采样、监测方法和技术。

Methods and techniques for sampling and monitoring should be based on the properties of the monitored objectives, the content, and the measurement requirements.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 46】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 46】

# 结果表达、质量保证及实施计划

# 1. 结果表达 Expression of results

水质监测所测得的众多化学、物理以及生物学的监测数据，是描述和评价水环境质量，进行环境管理的基本依据，必须进行科学地计算和处理，并按照要求形式在监测报告中表达出来。The data are the basis for environmental management.

# 2. 质量保证 Quality assurance

质量保证概括了保证水质监测数据正确可靠的全部活动和措施。质量保证贯穿监测工作的全过程。Quality assurance is very important.

# 3. 实施计划 Implementation plan

实施计划是实施监测方案的具体安排，要切实可行，使各个环节工作有序、协调地进行。The implementation plan should be practicable.

# 2.2 水质监测方案制订

# 2.2.2 地下水监测方案制订

地下水：存在于土壤和岩石空隙(孔隙、裂隙、溶隙)中
的水统称为地下水

Groundwater: The water that exists in soil and rock voids
(pores, fissures, and pores) is called groundwater.

地下水埋藏在地层的不同深度，目前主要监测浅层地下
水(潜水)，根据需要，也可监测深层地下水(承压水)。

Groundwater is buried at different depths in the strata. Currently,
shallow groundwater (unconfined water) is mainly monitored, and
deep groundwater (confined water) can be monitored as needed.

地下水的流动性和水质参数变化比较缓慢

Groundwater mobility and water quality parameters change
slowly.

Technical Specifications for Groundwater Environmental
Monitoring (HJ/T164-2020)

# 2.2.2 地下水监测方案制订

# Formulation of monitoring program for groundwater quality

- 调查和收集资料
Investigation and collection of information

- 收集、汇总监测区域的水文、地质、气象等方面的有关资料和以往的监测资料。
Collect and summarize relevant information on hydrology, geology, meteorology, etc. in the monitoring area and previous monitoring data.
- 例如，地质图、剖面图、测绘图、水井的成套参数、含水层、地下水补给、径流和流向 ， 以及温度 、 湿度 、 降水量等。
For example, geological maps, profiles, surveys, sets of parameters for wells, aquifers, groundwater recharge, runoff and flow, and temperature, humidity, precipitation, etc.
- 调查监测区域内城市发展、工业分布、资源开发和土地利用情况，尤其是地下工程规模、应用等；了解化肥和农药的施用面积和施用量；查清污水灌溉、排污、纳污和地面水污染现状。
Investigate and monitor urban development, industrial distribution, resource development and land use in the monitoring area, especially the scale and application of underground projects; understand the application area and application rate of chemical fertilizers and pesticides; and identify sewage irrigation, sewage, pollution and the status quo of surface water pollution.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 50】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 50】

# 2.2.2 地下水监测方案制订

# Formulation of monitoring program for groundwater quality

- # 调查和收集资料

# Investigation and collection of information

- 测量或查知水位、水深，以确定采水器和泵的类型，所需费用和采样程序。

Measure or check the water level and water depth to determine the type of water collector and pump, the cost and sampling procedure.
- 在完成以上调查的基础上，确定主要污染源和污染物，并根据地区特点与地下水的主要类型把地下水分成若干个水文地质单元。

On the basis of completing the above investigations, identify the main pollution sources and pollutants, and divide the groundwater into several hydrogeological units according to the characteristics of the area and the main types of groundwater.

# 2.2.2 地下水监测方案制订

# Formulation of monitoring program for groundwater quality

# ❖ 采样点布设 Sampling point layout

❑ 地下水一般呈分层流动，进入地下水的污染物、渗滤液等可沿垂直方向运动，也可沿水平方向运动，各层之间会发生串流

Groundwater generally flows in layers, and pollutants, leachate, etc. entering groundwater can move in the vertical direction or in the horizontal direction, and cross-flow occurs between the layers.

❑ 不仅要掌握污染源分布、类型和污染物扩散条件，还要弄清地下水分层和流向

It is necessary not only to grasp the distribution, type and diffusion conditions of pollution sources, but also to clarify groundwater stratification and flow direction.

❑ 两类采样点：背景值监测井、污染控制监测井

Two types of sampling points: background value monitoring well, pollution control monitoring well

❑ 背景值监测井：布设在监测区域未受污染的地段，地下水水流上方，垂直于水流方向

Background value monitoring well: laid in the uncontaminated area of the monitoring area, above the groundwater flow, perpendicular to the direction of the water flow

❑ 污染控制监测井：布设在污染源周围不同位置，特别是下游方向

Pollution control monitoring wells: deployed at different locations around the source of pollution, especially downstream

# ❖ 采样时间和采样频率的确定 Determination of sampling time and sampling frequency

# 2.2.3 水污染源监测方案制订

# Formulation of monitoring program for water pollution sources

污染源分类：Pollution sources :

- 按成因：自然污染源、工业废水源、生物污水源、医院污水源等
- According to the causes, it includes natural pollution sources, industrial pollution sources, domestic sewage sources, and hospital sewage sources.
- 按污染物：化学性、生物性、物理性等
- Divided by pollutants: chemical, biological, physical.
- 按排放形式：点源、非点源
- Divided by emission: point source, non-point source.

查清用水情况、废水或污水的类型

主要污染物及排污去向和排放量

综合分析确定

车间、工厂或地区排污

- □数量及位置
- 监测项目、监测点
- 废水处理情况
- 采样时间和频率
- 是否排入江河湖海，流经区域是否有渗坑等

采样监测方法技术

制订质量保证程序、措施和实施计划

# 2.2.3 水污染源监测方案制订

# Formulation of monitoring program for water pollution sources

- # 采样点的设置

# Sample point
- 水污染源一般经管道或渠、沟排放，截面积比较小，不需设置断面，而直接确定采样点位。

The source of water pollution is generally discharged through pipelines or channels and trenches, and the cross-sectional area is relatively small. It is not necessary to set the section, while the sampling point need to be set directly determined.
- # 工业废水

# Industrial wastewater

- a.在车间或车间设备出口处应布点采样测定一类污染物。

At the exit of the workshop or workshop equipment, sampling points should be taken to determine the first type of pollutants.
- b.在工厂污水总排污口布设采样点测定二类污染物。

Measure the second type of pollutants by sampling points at the total sewage outlet of the factory.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 54】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 54】

# 2.2.3 水污染源监测方案制订

# Formulation of monitoring program for water pollution sources

# ❖ 采样点的设置

# Sample point

❖ c.有处理设施的工厂应在处理设施的排出口布设采样点。为了解对废水的处理效果，可在进水口和出水口同时布点采样。

Factories with processing facilities should set sampling points at the discharge port of the treatment facility. In order to understand the treatment effect on wastewater, sampling can be carried out at the inlet and outlet simultaneously.

❖ d.在排污渠道上，采样点应设在渠道较直、水量稳定、上游没有污水汇入处。

On the sewage channel, the sampling point should be set at the place where the channel is straight, the water volume is stable, and there is no sewage inlet at the upstream.

54

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 55】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 2 · Slide 55】

# 2.2.3 水污染源监测方案制订

# Formulation of monitoring program for water pollution sources

# ❖ 采样点的设置 Sample point

# 生活污水 Domestic wastewater

# (1) 城市管网污水 Urban pipe network wastewater

采样点设在非居民生活排水支管接入城市污水干管的检查井；城市污水干管的不同位置；污水进入水体的排放口等。

The sampling point is set at the inspection well of the non-resident living drainage branch pipe connected to the urban sewage main pipe; the different positions of the urban sewage main pipe; the sewage entering the discharge port of the water body.

# (2) 城市污水处理厂 Urban sewage treatment plant

采样点设在污水进口和处理后的总排放口 。对污水处理厂，应在进、出口分别设采样点。另外，还需设污泥采样点。

The sampling point is set at the total discharge port after the sewage inlet and treatment. For sewage treatment plants, sampling points should be set at the inlet and outlet respectively. In addition, sludge sampling points are required.

# ❖ 采样时间和采样频率 Sampling time and frequency

工业废水和城市污水的排放量和污染物浓度随工厂生产及居民生活情况常发生变化，采样时间和频率应根据实际情况确定。

Discharges and pollutant concentrations of industrial wastewater and municipal wastewater often change with factory production and residents' living conditions. The sampling time and frequency should be determined according to actual conditions.

55

# 2.3.1 水样的类型 Types of water samples

# ❑ 瞬时水样 Instantaneous water sample

在某一时间和地点从水体中随机采集的分散单一水样。

Water sample randomly collected from a water body at a certain time and place

- 水质稳定，组分时空变化不大，代表性

stable water quality, consistent in components with time and space, representative
- 变化较大时，隔时、多点采集

When change is large, collect samples at interval time and multisite

# ❑ 混合水样 Composite water sample

- 等时混合水样：某一时段内，同一采样点按等时间间隔采集的等体积瞬时水样混合后的水样：某一时段平均浓度

Composite water sample with the same time interval. At a fixed sampling point, water was collected with equal volume in a time range with the same intervals. It refers to the average concentration in a certain period of time.
- 等比例混合水样：某一时段内，同一采样点所采水样量随时间或流量成比例变化的混合水样，即在不同时间依照流量大小按比例采集的混合水样：流量和污染物浓度不稳定

Proportionally composite water sample: At a fixed sampling point, water sample was collected proportionally to the flow rate at different time within a certain period. It is suitable when the flow rate and pollutants concentration are unstable.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 6】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 6】

# 2.3.1 水样的类型 Types of water samples

| 水样名称                                                                                                        | 采样时间    | 采样地点   | 混合情况              |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------ | ----------------- |
| 瞬时水样                                                                                                        | 规定时间内随机 | 规定地点   | 一次性采样             |
| 综合水样                                                                                                        | 同时      | 不同点    | 均匀混合 或 按流量比例混合    |
| 混合水样                                                                                                        | 不同时     | 同点     | 均匀混合 或 按流量比例混合    |
| 平均污水样                                                                                                       | 周期性     | 同点或不同点 | 混合后一次测定 或 单测后求平均值 |
| 把不同采样点同时采集的各个瞬时水样混合后所得到的样品称综合水样 Mixed instantaneous water samples collected from different sampling points. |         |        |                   |
| 平均比例                                                                                                        | a.不同时   | a.同点   | 按比例混合             |
|                                                                                                             | b.同时    | b.不同点  |                   |
| 定时水样                                                                                                        | 在各周期内定时 | 定点     | 各水样单独测定           |

# 水样名称

# 适用情况（举例）

- 瞬时水样
1. 废水量与水质均较稳定的水体
2. 随时间变化的水体，在适当时间间隔采瞬时水样
3. 随空间变化的水体，在相应部位采瞬时水样

The water quality parameters of integrated water samples are required when building a comprehensive treatment plant for a plenty of sewage rivers and canals.

- 综合水样
- 为几条排污河渠建设综合处理厂
- 混合水样
- 废水流量恒定，但水质有变化的污染源
- （时间混合样）
- 平均污水样
- 废水流量及水质均有变化的污染源，生活污水宜采集此样
- 平均比例
- 污水排放量不稳定的情况下，一个排污口不同时间的平均
- 混合污水样
- 比例混合或几个排污口的瞬时综合排污浓度
- 定时水样
- 调查任何污染源在某段时间内污染物的排放情况

# 2.3.2 地表水样的采集 surface water sampling

# 1 采样方法 Sampling method

# ①船只采样

适用于一般河流和水库的采样，但不容易固定采样地点，往往数据不具有可比性。

Sampling on boats: suitable for general sampling of rivers and reservoirs, but it is not easy to fix the sampling location, often making the data not comparable.

# ②桥梁采样

安全、可靠、方便，不受天气和洪水的影响，适合于频繁采样，并能在横向和纵向准确控制采样点位置。

Sampling on bridge: safe, reliable and convenient, not affected by weather and floods It is suitable for frequent sampling and can accurately control the sampling point position in both horizontal and vertical directions.

# ③涉水采样

较浅的小河和靠近岸边浅的采样点可涉水采样，但要避免搅动沉积物而使水样受污染。

Sampling by wading into the water: shallow creeks and sampling points near the shore, but should avoid mixing the sediment and contaminating the water sample.

# ④索道采样

在地形复杂、险要，地处偏僻处的小河流，可架索道采样。

Sampling by cableway: Small river located at places with complex and dangerous terrain.

# 2.3.6 流量的测量 Flow Measurement

- 为计算地表水污染负荷是否超过环境容量和评价污染控制效果，掌握废(污)水排放源污染物排放总量和排水总量，采样时需要同步测量废(污)水的流量
- To calculate whether the pollution load for surface water exceeds the environmental capacity and to evaluate the effect of pollution control, know the total amount of pollutants discharged from the wastewater sources and the total amount of drainage, the flow of wastewater should be measured simultaneously.
- 对于较大河流，水利部门都设有水位测量断面，尽可能利用此断面
- For larger rivers, special sections are always set by the government, use the section if possible.
- 若监测河段无水文测量断面，应选择一个水文参数比较稳定、流量有代表性的断面作为测量断面
- If there is no such section, a section with relatively stable hydrological parameters and representative flow rate should be selected as the measurement section.

# 2.3.6 流量的测量 Flow Measurement

Surface water

Select a straight river range.

flow velocity-area

Put the float at the start point.

Then measure the time needed for the float flowing from the start to the final point.

Divide the section float into many small blocks.

是一种粗略测量小型河、渠中水流速的简易方法。测量时，选三个过水横断面面积，求出平均横断面面积。在上游投入浮标，

Then measure the area and flow velocity of each block.

Calculate the flow of each block.

Add all flow value together.

qν = K · ν · A

# 2.3.6 流量的测量 Flow Measurement

Surface water

Select a straight river range. Measure the area of the flow velocity-area sections at the start point, middle point and final point.

Put the float at the start point. Then measure the time needed for the float flowing from the start to the final point.

float 是一种粗略测量小型河、渠中水流速的简易方法。测量时，选择一平直河段，测量该河段不小于10m间距内起点、中点和终点三个过水横断面面积，求出平均横断面面积。在上游投入浮标，测量浮标流经确定测量间距河段（L）所需时间，重复测量几次，求出所需时间的平均值（t），即可计算出流速（L/t）。

qν = K · ν · A

# 2.3.6 流量的测量 Flow Measurement

# 流量转换器

# 废(污)水流量测量 Wastewater flow measurement

1. # 流量计法 Flow meter method

自由水面的敞开水路用流量计：堰式流量计、水槽流量计

Flow meter for free water surface with open waterway: weir flow meter, water tank flow meter

充满水的管道用流量计：电磁流量计、压差式流量计

Flow meter for pipes filled with water: electromagnetic flowmeter, differential pressure flowmeter
2. # 容积法 Volumetric method

将污水导入已知容积的容器或污水池中，测量流满容器或污水池的时间，然后用其除受纳容器或池的容积，即可求知流量。该方法简单易行，适用于测量污水流量较小的连续或间歇排放的污水。

Introducing sewage into a container with given volume, measuring the time to fill the, and then the flow rate can be calculated. Simple and easy, and is suitable for wastewater continuous or intermittent discharge of sewage with small flow rate.
3. # 溢流堰法 Overflow weir method

适用于不规则的污水沟、污水渠中流量的测量。该方法是用三角形或矩形、梯形堰板拦住水流，形成溢流堰，测量堰板前后水头和水位，计算流量。如果安装液位计，可连续自动测量液位。

Applicable for irregular sewer. Using a triangular or rectangular, trapezoidal raft to stop the water flow and forming an overflow weir, then measuring the water level before and after the raft, and then calculating the flow.

# 2.3.7 水样运输与保存

# Transportation and storage of water samples

# 水样的运输

# Transportation of water samples

- 为避免水样在运输过程中震动、碰撞导致损失或沾污，将其装箱，并用泡沫塑料或纸条挤紧，在箱顶贴上标记。同一采样点的样品瓶尽量装在同一箱中；应有交接手续。
- To prevent sample contamination during transportation, the samples should be carefully packaged.
- Marked on the top of the box
- Samples at the same sampling point should be placed in the same box
- There should be handover procedures.

- 需冷藏的样品，应采取致冷保存措施；冬季应采取保温措施，以免冻裂样品瓶。
- Refrigeration should be conducted for samples needing to be refrigerated
- Avoid freezing of the sample bottles.

# 2.3.7 水样运输与保存

# Transportation and storage of water samples

# 水样的保存方法

# Method for water storage

- 加入的保存剂不能干扰以后的测定
- 保存剂的纯度最好是优级纯
- 做空白试验，对测定结果进行矫正

# 1. 冷藏或冷冻法

# refrigerated or frozen

- 冷藏或冷冻的作用是抑制微生物活动，减缓物理挥发和化学反应速度。
- To inhibit microbial activity, slow down physical volatilization and chemical reaction.
- 冷藏： 温度一般2-5°C，该法不能长期保存水样
- Refrigeration: 2-5 °C, cannot store water samples for a long time.
- 冷冻：温度一般为-20°C，不能将水样充满整个容器
- Freezing: -20 °C, cannot fill the container

# 2. 加入化学试剂保存法

# adding chemical reagents

1. 加入生物抑制剂
2. biological inhibitors

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 26】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 26】

# 2.3.7 水样运输与保存

# Transportation and storage of water samples

# 水样的保存方法

# Method for preserving water samples

- 2. 加入化学试剂保存法
- adding chemical reagents

# (2) 调节pH值

# Adjusting the pH

如测定金属离子的水样常用HNO₃酸化至pH为1～2，既可防止重金属离子水解沉淀，又可避免金属被器壁吸附；测定氰化物或挥发性酚的水样加入NaOH调至pH为12时，使之生成稳定的酚盐等。

- For metal ions, acidified to pH 1-2 by HNO₃ to prevent the hydrolysis and precipitation as well as the metal adsorption by the container wall
- For cyanide or volatile phenol, the pH should be adjusted to 12 by adding NaOH to form a stable phenolate.

# (3) 加入氧化剂或还原剂

# Adding oxidant or reducing agent

如测定汞的水样需加入HNO₃(至pH＜1)和K₂Cr₂O₇(0.05%)，使汞保持高价态；测定硫化物的水样，加入抗坏血酸，可以防止被氧化；测定溶解氧的水样则需加入少量硫酸锰和碘化钾固定溶解氧（还原）等。

- For mercury, adding HNO₃ (to pH&#x3C;1) and K₂Cr₂O₇ (0.05%) to maintain high valence of mercury
- For sulfides, adding ascorbic acid added to prevent oxidation
- For DO, adding a small amount of manganese sulfate and potassium iodide should be to fix DO

| ----------- | ---- | ----- | -- | ------------ | ---------------- | ----------------- |
| 酸度          | 100  | +     | +  | 4            | 24h              |                   |
| 碱度          | 100  | +     | +  | 4            | 24h              |                   |
| pH值         | 50   | +     | +  | 4            | 6h               | 最好现场测定            |
| BOD ₅       | 1000 | +     | +  | 4            | 6h               |                   |
| COD         | 50   | +     | +  |              | 加H2SO4至pH<2      | 7d                |
| DO(碘量法)     | 300  |       | +  |              | 加1mL硫酸锰和2mL碱性碘化钾 | 4\~8h 现场测定        |
| 正磷酸盐（总溶解性磷） | 50   | +     | +  | 4            | 24h              | 长时间保存时，加40mLHgCl2 |
| 氰化物         | 500  | +     | +  | 4            | 加NaOH至pH=13      | 24h 现场测定          |
| 氨氮          | 400  | +     | +  | 4            | 加H2SO4至pH<2      | 24h               |
| 硝酸盐         | 100  | +     | +  | 4            | 加H2SO4至pH<2      | 24h               |
| 硫化物         | 250  | +     | +  |              | 2mL醋酸锌溶液         | 24h 现场测定          |
| 六价铬         |      | +     |    | 加NaOH至pH8\~9 | 当天测定             | 用新的硬质玻璃           |

# 2.3.7 水样运输与保存

# Transportation and storage of water samples

# 水样的过滤或离心分离

# Separation by filtration or centrifugation

- 如欲测水样中某组分的含量，采样后立即加入保存剂，分析测定时充分摇匀后再取样。
- For determining the content of a component in water sample, add the preservative immediately after sampling, and shake thoroughly before sampling for measurement.
- 如果测定可滤（溶解）态组分含量，所采水样应用0.45μm微孔滤膜过滤，除去藻类和细菌，提高水样的稳定性，有利于保存。
- For determining the dissolved component, the water sample should be filtered using a 0.45μm membrane to remove algae and bacteria, which improves the stability of the water sample for better storage.
- 如果测定不可过滤的金属时，应保留过滤水样用的滤膜备用。对于泥沙型水样，可用离心方法处理。对含有机质多的水样，可用滤纸或砂芯漏斗过滤。用自然沉降后取上清液测定可滤态组分是不恰当的。
- For determining the non-filterable metal, the membrane after filtration should be kept. Sediment-containing water can be treated by centrifugation. For water samples containing high organic matter, use filter paper or sand core funnel.
- It is not a proper way measure filterable components by using the supernatant.

28

# 2.4 水样的预处理

# Pretreatment

# 2.4.1 水样的消解

# Digestion

# 2.4.2 富集与分离

# Concentration and separation

# 为什么要进行预处理

# Reasons for pretreatment

- 环境水样所含组分复杂，并且多数污染组分含量低，存在形态各异，所以在分析测定之前，往往需要进行预处理，以得到欲测组分适合测定方法要求的形态、浓度和消除共存组分干扰的试样体系。
- Environmental water samples contain complex components, and most of the components are with very low content and always exist in different speciation. Therefore, pretreatment is required to remove interferences and obtain the proper speciation and compositions for the measured components.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 31】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 31】

# 2.4.1 水样的消解

# Digestion of water samples

当测定含有机物的水样中的无机元素时，需要进行消解处理

Digestion is required to determine inorganic elements in the water samples containing organic matters.

- 破坏有机物 Destruction of organic matter
- 溶解悬浮性固体 Dissolving suspended solids
- 将各种价态的欲测元素氧化成单一高价态或转变成易于分离的无机化合 Oxidize elements with various valences to high valence state or an easily separable inorganic compound
- 消解后样品应透明、澄清、无沉淀；不引入待测组分和干扰组分；不损失待测组分 After digestion, the sample should be transparent, clear, and free of precipitation; no components to be tested and interference components are introduced; no loss of components to be tested

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 33】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 33】

# 2.4.1 水样的消解

# Digestion of water samples

# 湿式消解法

# Wet digestion

1. # 硝酸消解法

# nitric acid digestion

- 对于较清洁的水样，可用硝酸消解。
- Cleaner water samples can be digested by nitric acid.
2. # 硝酸-高氯酸消解法

# nitric and perchloric acid digestion

- 两种酸都是强氧化性酸，联合使用可消解含难氧化有机物的水样。
- Nitric and perchloric acid are strong oxidizing acids.
- For samples containing hardly oxidizable organic compounds
3. # 硝酸-硫酸消解法

# nitric and sulfuric acid digestion

- 两种酸都有较强的氧化能力，其中硝酸沸点低，而硫酸沸点高，二者结合使用，可提高消解温度和消解效果。常用的硝酸与硫酸的比例为5∶2。
- Both acids are very strong
- A combination of both acids can increase the digestion temperature and the digestion effect.
- A commonly used ratio of nitric acid to sulfuric acid is 5:2.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 34】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 34】

# 2.4.1 水样的消解 Digestion of water samples

# 4. 硫酸-磷酸消解法 Sulfuric and phosphoric acid digestion

- 两种酸的沸点都比较高，其中硫酸氧化性较强，磷酸能与一些金属离子如Fe³⁺等络合，故二者结合消解水样，有利于测定时消除Fe³⁺等离子的干扰。
- The boiling points of both acids are relatively high.
- The oxidation ability of sulfuric acid is strong, and the phosphoric acid can be complexed with some metal ions such as Fe³⁺. Therefore water digestion by a combination of both acids is beneficial to eliminate the interference of Fe³⁺.

# 5. 硫酸-高锰酸钾消解法 Sulfuric and potassium permanganate digestion

- 该方法常用于消解测定汞的水样。高锰酸钾是强氧化剂，在中性、碱性、酸性条件下都可以氧化有机物，其氧化产物多为草酸根，但在酸性介质中还可继续氧化。
- Digest water samples for mercury determination.
- Potassium permanganate is a strong oxidant, which can oxidize organic matter in neutral, alkaline and acidic conditions. The oxidation product is mostly oxalate which can be oxidized further in acidic medium.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 35】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 35】

# 2.4.1 水样的消解 Digestion of water samples

# 6. 多元消解法 multivariate digestion

为提高消解效果，在某些情况下需要采用三元以上酸或氧化剂消解体系。例如，处理测总铬的水样时，用硫酸、磷酸和高锰酸钾消解。

To improve the digestion effect, in some cases it is necessary to use a ternary acid or oxidant digestion system. For example, when measure the total chromium in the water sample, the sample can be digested with sulfuric acid, phosphoric acid, and potassium permanganate.

# 7. 碱分解法 alkali digestion

当用酸体系消解水样造成易挥发组分损失时，可改用碱分解法，即在水样中加入氢氧化钠和过氧化氢溶液，或者氨水和过氧化氢溶液，加热煮沸至近干，用水或稀碱溶液温热溶解。

When there is a loss of volatile components using acid, the alkali digestion can be used, by adding sodium hydroxide and hydrogen peroxide solution, or ammonia water and hydrogen peroxide solution. The water mixture is heated and boiled to near dryness, and then was dissolved with water or a dilute alkali solution.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 36】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 36】

# 2.4.1 水样的消解

# 干灰化法

又称干式分解法或高温分解法。其处理过程是：取适量水样于白瓷或石英蒸发皿中，置于水浴上或用红外灯蒸干，移入马弗炉内，于450～550℃灼烧到残渣呈灰白色，使有机物完全分解除去。取出蒸发皿，冷却，用适量2%HNO₃（或HCl）溶解样品灰分，过滤，滤液定容后供测定。

Dry decomposition or high-temperature decomposition. The treatment process is as follows: adding an appropriate amount of water into a white porcelain or quartz evaporating dish, placing it on a water bath or evaporating it with an infrared lamp, transferring it into a muffle furnace, and burning it at 450-550°C until the residue is grayish white, so that the organic matter is completely decomposed and removed. The evaporating dish was taken out, cooled, and the sample ash was dissolved with an appropriate amount of 2% HNO₃ (or HCl), filtered, and the filtrate was made into a volume for measurement.

本方法不适用于处理测定易挥发组分（如砷、汞、镉、硒、锡等）的水样。

This method is not suitable for measuring volatile components such as arsenic, mercury, cadmium, selenium, tin, etc.

# 2.4.2 富集与分离 Concentration and separation

# concentration

富集 提高待测组分的浓度

目的 Separate 组件 to be tested

分离 消除共存干扰组分

或掩蔽 Eliminate coexisting interference components

# Method

方法 过滤与挥发、蒸馏、溶剂萃取、共沉淀

离子交换、吸附、层析、低温浓缩..

Filtration and volatilization, distillation, solvent extraction, co-precipitation, ion exchange, adsorption, chromatography, low temperature concentration

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 38】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 38】

# 2.4.2 富集与分离 Concentration and separation

# ■ 富集 Concentration

- 富集是分离的一种，即从大量试样中搜集欲测定的少量物质至一较小体积中，从而提高其浓度至其测定下限之上。
- A type of separation, a small amount of substances to be measured is collected from a large number of samples into a small volume, thereby increasing its concentration above the detection limit.

# ■ 分离 Separation

- 分离是将欲测组分从试样中单独析出，或将几个组分一个一个地分开，或者根据各组分的共同性质分成若干组。
- Separation is to separate the components to be tested from the sample, or to separate several components one by one, or to divide into several groups according to the common properties of the components.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 39】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 39】

# 2.4.2 富集与分离 Concentration and separation

# 气提、顶空和蒸馏法 Air stripping, headspace and distillation

# 气提法 Air stripping

- 该方法基于把惰性气体通入调制好的水样中，将欲测组分吹出，直接送入仪器测定，或导入吸收液吸收富集后再测定。
- Introducing an inert gas into the prepared water sample
- Blowing the component out for measurement or bringing the component into the equipment directly
- Using liquid absorbents to enrich them before measurement

汞、硫化物 mercury, sulfide

$N_{2}$

# 2.4.2 富集与分离 Concentration and separation

# 气提、顶空和蒸馏法 Air stripping, headspace and distillation

# 顶空法 Headspace method

- 该方法常用于测定挥发性有机物（VOCs）水样的预处理。例如，测定水样中的挥发性有机物（VOCs）或挥发性无机物（VICs）时，先在密闭的容器中装入水样，容器上部留存一定空间，再将容器置于恒温水浴中，经一定时间，容器内的气液两相达到平衡。
- Used to pretreat water samples for volatile organic compounds (VOCs) measurement
- When measuring VOCs or volatile inorganic substances (VICs), the water sample is placed in a closed container, with a certain space in the upper part of the container. Then the container is placed in a water bath with constant temperature. For a certain period of time, the gas-liquid two phases in the vessel reach equilibrium.

欲测物气相中的平衡浓度[X]G和水样中原始浓度[X]L⁰之间的关系：

The relationship between the equilibrium concentration [X]G in the gas phase of the sample and the original concentration [X]L⁰ in the water sample

Separating components based on different boiling points, including atmospheric distillation, vacuum distillation, steam distillation, fractionation, etc.

# 2.4.2 富集与分离 Concentration and separation

# 萃取法 Extraction

# 固相萃取法 Solid phase extraction (SPE)

• 萃取剂是固体
• 其工作原理基于：水样中欲测组分与共存干扰组分在固相萃取剂上作用力强弱不同，使它们彼此分离。
• 固相萃取剂是含C₁₈或C₈、腈基、氨基等基团的特殊填料。
• Extractant is solid
• Principle: different interactions between the components to be tested or the interferences with the solid extractant.
• The solid phase extractant is a special filler containing C₁₈, C₈, a nitrile group or an amino group.

1.水样；2.抽气；3.萃取膜片

Schematic diagram of a solid phase extraction device

# 2.4.2 富集与分离 Concentration and separation

# 吸附法 Adsorption

吸附法是利用多孔性的固体吸附剂将水样中一种或数种组分吸附于表面，再用适宜溶剂、加热或吹气等方法将欲测组分解吸，达到分离和富集的目的.

Using a porous solid adsorbent to adsorb components on its surface, and then separating or concentrating the components by further desorption using suitable solvent, heating or blowing.

| Physical adsorption: | Van der Waals force  |                   |
| -------------------- | -------------------- | ----------------- |
| Adsorption           | Chemical adsorption: | chemical reaction |

| Activated carbon | Metal ion      | Porous polymer  | Organic matter |
| ---------------- | -------------- | --------------- | -------------- |
| Aluminum oxide   | Organic matter | Mercapto Cotton | Alkyl mercury  |

# 2.4.2 富集与分离 Concentration and separation

# 离子交换法 Ion exchange

- 该方法是利用离子交换剂与溶液中的离子发生交换反应进行分离的方法。离子交换剂分为无机
- 离子交换剂和有机离子交换剂两大类，广泛应用的是有机离子交换剂，即离子交换树脂
- Separating ions using ion-exchange materials, including organic and inorganic exchangers. Ion exchange resin is a type of organic exchanger, and is most commonly used.

# 交换柱的制备：选择交换树脂，浸泡，装柱

| 操作程序               | Procedure                                                              |
| ------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 交换：由上到下            | exchange : from top to bottom                                          |
| 洗脱：盐酸、氯化钠溶液、氢氧化钠溶液 | Elution: hydrochloric acid, sodium chloride solution, sodium hydroxide |

# 强酸型、弱酸型阳离子交换树脂

| RSO3H | RSO3- + H+ |
| ----- | ---------- |
| RCOOH | RCOO + H   |

# 强碱型性阴离子交换树脂

| R—N(CH3)3OH | → R—N+（CH3)₃3+OH |
| ----------- | ---------------- |
| Cl⁻, NO₃⁻等  |                  |

# 共沉淀法 Co-precipitation

Principle: Some trace components will co-precipitate with some non-dissoluble compounds.

原理: 将共存的某些痕量组分一起载带沉淀出来

# Type:

- • surface adsorption
- • mixed crystal co-precipitation
- • solid solution or trapped
- • interactions among colloids
- • with different charges
- co-precipitation with organic compounds

# 应用

有机共沉淀剂

# 浊度 Turbidity

浊度是反映水中的不溶解物质对光线透过时阻碍程度的指标，通常仅用于天然水和饮用水，而污水和废水中不溶物质含量高，一般要求测定悬浮物。测定浊度的方法有目视比浊法、分光光度法、浊度计法等。

Turbidity is an indicator for the degree of hindrance to light transmission by insoluble matters in water. It is usually used only for natural water and drinking water. In sewage and wastewater, the content of insoluble matters is very high. It is generally required to measure SS. Methods for measuring turbidity include visual nephelometry, spectrophotometry, and turbidity method.

# 测定方法

| 目视比浊法 | visible turbidimetric method |
| ----- | ---------------------------- |
| 分光光度法 | spectrophotometric method    |
| 浊度仪法  | turbidity meter method       |

0  50  100             500   3000 (NTU)

# 透明度

| 铅字法  | Type transparency meter method |
| ---- | ------------------------------ |
| 塞氏盘法 | Plug's plate method            |

# 固体物Solid

水中的固体物分为总固体物、溶解固体物和悬浮物三种。它们是表征水中溶解性物质、不溶解性物质含量的指标。

| total solids (TS) | total dissolved solids (TDS) | total suspended solids (TSS) |

# 矿化度Salinity

矿化度是水化学成分测定的重要指标，用于评价水中总含盐量，是农田灌溉用水适用性评价的主要指标之一。该指标一般只用于天然水。

An important index for chemical compositions in water. It is used to evaluate the total salt content in water. A key index to evaluate the applicability of the irrigation water. This index is generally used for natural water.

矿化度的测定方法有重量法、电导法、阴、阳离子加和法、离子交换法、比重计法等。重量法含意明确，是较简单、通用的方法。

Measurement methods: gravimetric method, conductivity method, anion-cation addition method, on exchange method, hydrometer method. The gravimetric method is simple and most commonly used.

基态→第一激发态，吸收一定频率的辐射能量。

产生共振吸收线（简称共振线）

# 吸收光谱

- Absorption spectrum refers to the spectrum produced by a substance absorbing photons and transitioning from low energy levels to high energy levels.
- The absorption spectrum can be linear or absorption band.
- Studying absorption spectra can reveal the structure and motion of atoms, molecules, and many other substances, as well as their interactions with electromagnetic fields or particles.

光谱法 Spectrometry

# 发射光谱    吸收光谱   荧光光谱

# Emission  Adsorption  Fluorescence

第一激发态→基态        发射出同样频率的辐射。

产生共振发射线（也简称共振线）       发射光谱

- Use of atomic or ion emission characteristic spectra of each element under thermal or electrical excitation to determine the composition of a substance, and to conduct qualitative and quantitative analysis of elements.
- This method can be effectively used to measure high, medium, and low content elements.

分光光度法、双硫腙分光光度法

Spectrophotometry and spectrophotometric method with dithizone

冷原子吸收光谱法

• Fluorescence spectrum: Ground state atoms (usually in the vapor state) absorb radiation at a specific frequency and are excited to high energy states. During the excitation process, they emit fluorescence of characteristic wavelengths in the form of light radiation.

Flame/graphite furnace atomic absorption spectrometer

光谱法是基于物质与辐射能作用时，测量由物质内部发生量子化的能级之间的跃迁而产生的发射、吸收或散射辐射的波长和强度进行分析的方法。

# Cold atomic absorption spectrometry

Spectrometry is a method for analyzing the wavelength and intensity of emitted, absorbed, or scattered radiation generated by the transitions between quantized energy levels inside a substance when it interacts with radiation energy.

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 62】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 3 · Slide 62】

# 光谱法 Spectrometry

# 金属测定中常用仪器分析方法 techniques

- 原子发射光谱法是研究待测元素激发的辐射强度；

Atomic emission spectrometry is the study of the radiation intensity excited by the element being measured;
- 原子吸收光谱法是研究待测原子蒸气对光源共振线的吸收强度，是属吸收光谱；

Atomic absorption spectrometry is the study of the absorption intensity of the measured atomic vapor on the resonance line of a light source, which belongs to absorption spectroscopy;
- 原子荧光光谱法是研究待测元素受激发跃迁所发射的荧光强度，虽然激发主式与发射光谱法不同，但仍然是属发射光谱。

Atomic fluorescence spectrometry is the study of the fluorescence intensity emitted by excited transitions of the tested element. Although the excitation formula is different from that of emission spectroscopy, it still belongs to emission spectroscopy.
- 而原子荧光光谱法既具有发射光谱分析的特点，又与原子吸收法有许多相似之处，因此，介于两者之间，在某些方面兼具两者的优点。

Atomic fluorescence spectrometry has the characteristics of emission spectroscopy analysis and also shares many similarities with atomic absorption spectrometry. Therefore, it combines the advantages of both.

# 2.6 金属化合物的测定 Measurement of metal compounds

# 一、铝 Aluminum

- 铝是自然界中的常量元素，毒性不大，但过量摄入人体，能干扰磷的代谢，对胃蛋白酶的活性有抑制作用。
- Al is a common element in nature with low toxicity. However, the excessive intake of Al may interfere the metabolism of phosphorus and inhibit the activity of pepsin.
- 我国饮用水限值为0.2 mg/L。The concentration of Al in the drinking water should not exceed 0.2 mg/L.

# 测定方法 Measurement：

1. 电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法（ICP-OES）
Inductive coupled plasma-optical emission spectrometer
2. 间接火焰原子吸收法
Indirect flame atomic absorption spectrometer
3. 分光光度法
Spectrophotometry

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 4 · Slide 8】 -->

# 2.6  金属化合物的测定 Measurement of metal compounds

有11%的网友常吃永和大王的油条，而且永和大王自称为“非矾油条”，肯德基自称为“安心油条”。在本次检测中，永和大王油条铝含量为13.51mg/kg，肯德基油条的铝含量为12.30mg/kg，虽未超过国家标准的100mg/kg，但两者均不是完全“无铝”。

对此，中国农业大学范志红博士表示：油条中不能做到“零铝含量”，是因为铝总会微量存在于食品和环境当中。只要不是人工添加含铝物质，也没有含铝容器和含铝包装污染的问题，其含量通常足够低，到了不产生健康威胁的程度，就无需担心了。

根据调查显示，街边的油条含铝量普遍远远超过WHO/FAO健康指导值。按照WHO/FAO的标准，一个人每周每公斤体重最多摄入2毫克的铝。

对于一个60公斤体重的成年人，街边的油条吃一根就已经至少摄入了32mg的铝。即每天平均摄入量不超过17mg。儿童摄入过量铝会导致智力发育障碍，老人易发痴呆。

范志红博士认为，不吃或少吃油条、油饼、麻花、子、虾片等等所有质地蓬松或脆爽的油炸食品，是一个减少铝摄入量的明智做法。

http://health.sohu.com/s2012/youtiao/

# (一) 电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法（ICP-OES）

# 原理 Principle：

电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法(ICP-OES)

- 电感耦合等离子体焰炬温度可达6000-8000K
- Inductively coupled plasma-optical emission spectrometer
- 当将试样由进样器引入雾化器，并被氩载气带入焰炬时，则试样中组分被原子化、电离、激发，以光的形式发射出能量
- 不同元素的原子在激发或电离时，发射不同波长的特征光谱，故据特征光的波长可进行定性分析；元素的含量不同时，发射特征光的强弱也不同，据此可进行定量分析.
- The temperature of ICP torch can reach 6000-8000K.
- When the sample is introduced into the atomizer from the injector, and brought into the torch by the argon carrier gas, the components in the sample will be atomized, ionized and excited, which emits energy in the form of light.
- Cold atomic absorption spectrometry
- The atoms of different elements emit characteristic spectra with different wavelengths, which enables the qualitative analysis according to the wavelength of the characteristic light.
- When the content of the elements is different, the intensity of the emitted characteristic light is also different, according to which the quantitative analysis can be performed.

# 阳极溶出伏安法

Anodic stripping voltammetry

原子发射光谱 Atomic emission spectrometry

1）当处于基态的气态原子或离子吸收了一定的外界能量时，其核外电子就从一种能量状态（基态）跃迁到另一能量状态（激发态）；When a gaseous atom or ion in the ground state absorbs a certain amount of external energy, its extranuclear electrons transition from one energy state (ground state) to another energy state (excited state).

2）处于激发态的原子或离子很不稳定，经约10-8秒便跃迁返回到基态，并将激发所吸收的能量以一定的电磁波辐射出来；Atoms or ions in excited states are very unstable, and after about 10-8 seconds, they transition back to the ground state and emit the energy absorbed by the excitation as a certain electromagnetic wave.

3）将这些电磁波按一定波长顺序排列即为原子光谱（线状光谱）；Arranging these electromagnetic waves in a certain wavelength order is called atomic spectrum (linear spectrum).

4）由于原子或离子的能级很多并且不同元素的结构是不同的，因此，对特定元素的原子或离子可产生一系列不同波长的特征光谱，通过识别待测元素的特征谱线存在与否进行定性分析。Due to many energy levels of atoms or ions and different structures of different elements, a series of characteristic spectra with different wavelengths can be generated for specific atoms or ions. Qualitative analysis can be conducted by identifying the presence or absence of characteristic spectral lines of the tested element.

| intensity | C.u327305nm |
| --------- | ----------- |
| 9000      |             |
| 10111     |             |
| 0.0       | ：395nm      |

光学系统＆检测系统 Optical system &#x26; detection system

仪表

- 等离子体发射出的光，经过光学准直镜变成平行光
- The light emitted by the plasma is converted into parallel light through an optical collimating mirror.

放大器

- 通过光栅和棱镜的色散，得到不同波长和谱级的光
- Obtaining light of different wavelengths and spectral levels through the dispersion of gratings and prisms

光电倍增管

- 再经过光电倍增管的加强作用，在检测系统中被转化成数据
- Converting into data in the detection system through the strengthening effect of the photomultiplier tube

# (一) 电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法（ICP-OES）

# 原理 Principle：

定量关系可用下式表示

Quantitatively expressed as:

I = aCb

式中：

- I——发射特征谱线的强度；
- C——被测元素的浓度；
- a——与试样组成、形态及测定条件等有关的系数；
- b——自吸系数，b≤1°

I: Intensity of spectrum

C: Concentration of target element

a: coefficient related to sample composition, speciation and conditions

b: self-absorption coefficient

# Quantitation in Atomic Emission

• Concentration proportional to intensity emitted at the analyte wavelength.

# (一) 电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法（ICP-OES）

# 测定要点 key points

# 2-4 一些元素的测定波长及检出限

# 1) 水样预处理 Pretreatment

如测定溶解态元素，采样后立即用0.45mm滤膜过滤，取所需体积滤液 ，加浓硝酸消解；如测定元素总量, 用浓硝酸消解后，定容，并使溶液保持硝酸质量分数为5%的酸度。

• For dissolved elements, samples should be filtered by 0.45 mm membrane immediately after sampling, and then digested with nitric acid.

• For the total amount of elements, the samples should be digested with nitric acid, and then keep the solution with 5% nitric acid at a fixed volume.

# 一些元素的测定波长及检出限

| 测定元素 | 波长/nm  | 检出限/(mg · L−1) |
| ---- | ------ | -------------- |
| Al   | 308.21 | 0.1            |
| Fe   | 396.15 | 0.09           |
| As   | 193.69 | 0.1            |
| K    | 766.49 | 0.5            |
| Ba   | 233.53 | 0.004          |
| Mg   | 279.55 | 0.002          |
| Be   | 313.04 | 0.0003         |
| Mn   | 257.61 | 0.001          |
| Ca   | 234.86 | 0.005          |
| Na   | 589.59 | 0.2            |
| Ni   | 231.60 | 0.01           |
| Cd   | 214.44 | 0.003          |
| Pb   | 220.35 | 0.05           |
| Sr   | 407.77 | 0.001          |
| Co   | 238.89 | 0.005          |

| Cr   | 205.55 | 0.01           |
| V    | 311.07 | 0.01           |
| Zn   | 213.86 | 0.006          |
| u    | 324.75 | 0.01           |

# 2) 配置标准溶液和试剂空白溶液

Standard solution and blank

# 3) 测定 Measurement

调节仪器工作参数，选取两个标准溶液进行两点校正后，一次将试剂空白溶液，水样喷入电感耦合等离子体焰炬测定，扣除空白值后的元素测定值即为水样中该元素的浓度。

• Adjust working parameters of the instrument, select two standard solutions for two-point calibration, and then measure the blank.

• Then the water sample can be measured.

# 二、镉 Cadmium

- 镉属剧毒金属，可在人体的肝、肾等组织中蓄积，造成脏器组织损伤，尤以对肾脏损害最为明显。还会导致骨质疏松，诱发癌症。
- Cadmium is highly toxic, which can accumulate in the liver, kidney and other tissues of the human body, causing damage to organs and tissues, especially for kidney. It can also cause osteoporosis and induce cancer.
- 我国生活饮用水卫生标准规定镉的浓度不能超过0.005mg/L。
- The concentration of cadmium should not exceed 0.005mg / L in the drinking water in China.

# 2.6 金属化合物的测定 Measurement of metal compounds

# 原子吸收法 Atomic absorption spectroscopy

- 原子蒸气对其原子共振辐射吸收的现象 The phenomenon of atomic vapor absorbing its atomic resonance radiation.

# 感应耦合等离子体光谱法 Inductively coupled plasma-optical emission spectrometer

- 基于物质所产生的原子蒸气对特征谱线（通常是待测元素的特征谱线）的吸收作用来进行定量分析的一种方法 A quantitative analysis method based on the absorption of characteristic spectral lines (the element being tested) by atomic vapor generated by a substance.
- 将含待测元素的样品溶液在火焰/石墨炉中解离成基态原子。Transform the sample solution containing the element to be tested into ground state atoms in a flame/graphite furnace.
- 当空心阴极灯（光源）辐射出待测元素的特征光通过火焰/石墨炉时，因被火焰中待测元素的基态原子吸收而减弱。When the characteristic spectrometry of the element is emitted by a hollow cathode lamp (light source) through a flame/graphite furnace, it weakens due to absorption by the ground state atoms of the element in the flame.

# 冷原子荧光光谱法

- 在一定实验条件下，特征光强的变化与火焰中待测元素基态原子的浓度有定量关系，故只要测得吸光度，就可以求出样品溶液中待测元素的浓度。There is a quantitative relationship between the variation intensity and the concentration of the ground state atoms of the tested element in the flame. Therefore, as long as the absorbance is measured, the concentration of the tested element in the sample solution can be calculated.

# 阳极剥离伏安法 Anodic stripping voltammetry

# 2.6 金属化合物的测定 Measurement of metal compounds

# 铅和汞 Lead and mercury

# 日本水俣病 Japan Minamata disease

- 因为这两种东西在剂量小的情况下确实有让人容颜改善的情况，在现代劣质美容院里，往往还有用铅来增白皮肤的效果。在古人看来，这就是返老还童长生不老的显现。
- Can make the skin look better when using a small dosage.
- Lead is usually used in some beauty salons to whiten the skin.
- 值得一提的是，古罗马就是将铅作为美容粉和自来水管道，这也成为古罗马历史研究中认为古罗马灭亡的原因之一。
- Ancient Rome always use lead as a beauty powder and also for water pipe, which has been recognized as one reason for the destruction of ancient Rome.
- 当时的技术水平，铅和汞提纯很简单，熔点低。
- At that time, the purification of lead and mercury was simple due to the low melting point.
- 丹砂：是汞的硫化物，还是中药材，具镇静、安神和杀菌等功效。中国古代用它作为炼丹的重要原料。
- Cinnabar: HgS. Chinese herbal medicine, effects of calming, soothe the nerves and sterilizing.
- An important raw material for alchemy in ancient China.

# 三、汞 Mercury

电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法(ICP-OES)

汞及其化合物属于剧毒物质，主要来源于金属冶炼、仪器仪表制造、颜料、塑料、食盐电解及军工等废水。天然水中汞含量一般不超过0.1ug/L；我国饮用水标准限值为0.001mg/L

- Mercury and its compounds are highly toxic, mainly from metal smelting, instrument manufacturing, pigments, plastics, salt electrolysis and military wastewater.
- The mercury content in natural water generally does not exceed 0.1 g/L.
- For drinking water in China, the up limit is 0.001mg/L.

# （一）双硫腙分光光度法 Dithizone spectrophotometry

# 金属测定中常用仪器分析方法 techniques

# 1、分光光度法简介 Introduction

• 分光光度法是建立在分子吸收光谱基础上的分析方法。分子吸收光谱上的吸收峰值波长、吸收峰数目及形状与物质的分子结构（如价电子结构、键型、官能团等）紧密相关，这是进行定性分析的基础；吸收峰峰值波长处的吸光度与被测物质的浓度之间的关系符合朗伯比尔定律，即在一定的实验条件下二者呈线性关系，这是定量分析的基础。

Flame/graphite furnace atomic absorption spectrometer

• An analytical method based on molecular absorption spectroscopy.

• The wavelength of the absorption peak, the number and shape of absorption peaks are closely related to the molecular structure of the substance (such as valence electron structure, bond type, functional group, etc.), which is the basis for qualitative analysis.

# 冷原子荧光光谱法

• The absorbance at the wavelength of the peak and the concentration of the measured substance are in accordance with Lambert Beer‘s law, that is, a linear relationship under certain experimental conditions, and this is the basis of quantitative analysis.

# 阳极溶出伏安法

Anodic stripping voltammetry

# （一）双硫腙分光光度法 Dithizone spectrophotometry

# 朗伯比尔定律 Lambert Beer's Law

电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法(ICP-OES)

为吸光度 为透射比 A=lg(1/T)=Kbc

A; T, 即透射光强度与入射光强度之比; c为吸光物质的浓度,单位 mol/L; b为吸收层厚度, 单位cm

A is the absorbance; T is the transmittance, that is, the ratio of the transmitted light intensity to the incident light intensity; c is the concentration of the light absorbing substance, mol/L; b is the thickness of the absorbing layer, cm)

# Flame/graphite furnace atomic absorption spectrometer

# 物理意义 Physical meaning

- 当一束平行单色光垂直通过某一均匀非散射的吸光物质时, 其吸光度A与吸光物质的浓度c及吸收层厚度b成正比
- When a bundle of parallel monochromatic light passes vertically through a uniform non-scattering light absorbing material, the absorbance A is proportional to the concentration c of the light absorbing material and the thickness b of the absorbing layer.

# 冷原子荧光光谱法

# 波长范围 Range of wavelength：

1. 10～400 nm的紫外光区 ultraviolet light
2. 400～780 nm 的可见光区 visible light
3. 780nm～300 μm (按波数计为 4000cm⁻¹～400cm⁻¹) 的红外光区 infrared light

# 阳极溶出伏安法

Anodic stripping voltammetry

# （一）双硫腙分光光度法 Dithizone spectrophotometry

# 表2-5 物质颜色与吸收光颜色的关系

| 物质颜色 | 吸收光颜色 | 波长/nm     |
| ---- | ----- | --------- |
| 黄绿   | 紫     | 400\~450  |
| 黄    | 蓝     | 450 \~480 |
| 橙    | 绿蓝    | 480 \~490 |
| 红    | 蓝绿    | 490\~500  |
| 紫红   | 绿     | 500\~560  |
| 紫    | 黄绿    | 560\~580  |
| 蓝    | 黄     | 580\~600  |
| 绿蓝   | 橙     | 600 \~650 |
| 蓝绿   | 红     | 650\~780  |

在利用分光光度法监测项目中，大多数是将被测物质转变成有色物质，在可见光区通过测其对特征波长的吸光度，并与标准溶液的吸光度相比较实现定量测定。

# 分光光度计基本组成部分

1. 光源
2. 分光系统
3. 比色皿架
4. 光电检测及放大装置
5. 指示、记录仪表

双硫腙分光光度法 Dithizone spectrophotometry

# （一）双硫腙分光光度法 Dithizone spectrophotometry

- 试剂纯度要求高：提高汞螯合物的稳定性和测定准确度
- High demand for chemical purity: to improve the stability of mercury chelate and the accuracy of measurement
- 有色螯合物对光敏感，避光操作
- The colored chelate is sensitive to light, away from light
- 消除铜离子等共存金属离子干扰
- Eliminate the interference from other metal ions (Cu²⁺) coexisting in the solution
- 将汞沉淀回收或进行其他处理，避免污染
- The precipitation should be recycled or treated specially to avoid pollution

中华人民共和国国家标准 G8746D-87

水质 总汞的测定 高锰酸钾-过硫酸钾消解法 双硫踪分光光度法

国家环境保护局 1J03-14X 1085-08-01买

# （二）冷原子吸收法 Cold (vapor) atomic absorption spectroscopy

# 1. 方法原理 Principle

水样经消解后，将各种形态的汞转变成二价汞，再用氯化亚锡将二价汞还原为单质汞。利用汞易挥发的特点，在室温下通入空气或氮气流将其气化，载入冷原子吸收测汞仪，测量对特征波长(253.7nm)光的吸光度，与汞标准溶液的吸光度进行比较，定量，在一定浓度范围内，吸光度与浓度成正比。

# Workflow

图2.6.3 冷原子吸收汞仪工作流程 of cold atomic fluorescence mercury analyzer

- After digesting the water samples, various forms of mercury will be converted into divalent mercury, which is reduced to elemental mercury by stannous chloride (SnCl₂).
- As mercury is volatile, it will be vaporized by air or nitrogen flow at room temperature, and then will be loaded into a cold atomic absorption mercury analyzer.
- The absorbance of light at a characteristic wavelength (253.7 nm) will be measured, and then concentration will be quantified in comparison with the standard solution.
- In a certain concentration range, the absorbance is proportional to the concentration.

| 1.汞还原瓶 | 2.硅胶管 | 3.三通阀 | 4.吸收池 | 5.流量计 | 6、14.汞吸收瓶 | 7.缓冲瓶 | 8.抽气泵 | 9.汞灯 | 10.光电倍增管 | 11.放大器 | 12.指示表 | 13.记录仪 | 15.水蒸气吸收瓶 |
| ------ | ----- | ----- | ----- | ----- | --------- | ----- | ----- | ---- | -------- | ------ | ------ | ------ | --------- |

# （二）冷原子吸收法 Cold (vapor) atomic absorption spectroscopy

# 2. 测定要点 key points

# 电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法(ICP-OES)

# 1) 水样保存及预处理 Preservation and pretreatment

在硫酸硝酸介质中，加入高锰酸钾和过硫酸钾溶液，于近沸或煮沸状态下消解水样。煮沸法对含有机物、悬浮物较多，组成复杂的废水消解效果较近沸法好。对于清洁地面水、地下水及含有机物较少的污水，可以用溴酸钾溴化钾混合液在酸性介质中于室温(20℃以上)消解水样。过剩的氧化剂在临测前用盐酸羟胺溶液还原。

- Add potassium permanganate and potassium persulfate into water with sulfuric acid and nitric acid as medium, then digest the water sample in a near boiling or boiling state. Boiling method is preferred for water samples containing large amounts of organic matter and suspended solids.
- But for clean surface water, groundwater and sewage containing less organic matter, the solution mixture of potassium bromoacetate and potassium bromide can be used to digest the water sample in an acidic medium at room temperature (above 20 qC).
- Excess oxidant should be reduced by hydroxylamine hydrochloride solution before test.

# 2) 空白样品制备 Preparation of blank sample

用无汞蒸馏水代替水样，按照水样制备步骤制备空白试样 Replace the water sample with mercury-free distilled water, and prepare blank samples as procedures above.

# （二）冷原子吸收法 Cold (vapor) atomic absorption spectroscopy

# 2. 测定要点 key points

1. # 绘制标准曲线 Draw a calibration curve

- 按照水样介质条件，配制系列汞标准溶液。分别吸取适量注入还原瓶内，加入氯化亚锡溶液，迅速通入载气，记录指示表最高读数或记录仪记录的峰高。用同法测定空白试样。以扣除空白后的各测量值为纵坐标，相应标准溶液的浓度为横坐标，绘制出标准曲线。
- A series of mercury standard solutions are prepared according to the conditions of the water samples.
- 火焰原子吸收光谱法、石墨炉原子吸收光谱法
- Pipette the appropriate amount into the reducing bottle, then add the stannous chloride (SnCl₂) solution, quickly inject the carrier gas, record the highest number of the indicator or the peak height.
- 分光光度法、双硫腙分光光度法
- The blank sample was measured by the same method.
- The measured values after removing the blank are as ordinate, and the concentration of the corresponding standard solution are as abscissa, and draw the calibration.
2. # 水样的测定 Determination of water samples

- 取适量处理好的水样于还原瓶中，按照测定标准溶液的方法测其最高读数或峰高，从标准曲线上查得汞的浓度，再乘以水样稀释倍数，即得水样汞的浓度。
- Take an appropriate amount of treated water sample in a reducing bottle, measure the highest reading or peak height accordingly.
- 电位分析法
- Get the concentration of mercury from the calibration curve, and multiply the dilution factor of the water sample to obtain the mercury concentration in the sample.

Anodic stripping voltammetry

# 2.6 金属化合物的测定 Measurement of metal compounds

# 四、铅 Lead

电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法(ICP-OES)

- 铅是可在人体和动植物中蓄积的有毒金属，其主要毒性效应是导致贫血、神经机能失调和肾损伤等。
- 铅对水生生物的安全浓度为0.16mg/L。
- Lead is a toxic metal that can accumulate in humans, animals and plants. Its main toxic effects are anemia, neurological dysfunction and kidney damage.
- The safe concentration of lead for aquatic organisms is under 0.16 mg/L.

# 2.6 金属化合物的测定 Measurement of metal compounds

# 五、铜 Copper

- 铜是人体所必需的微量元素，缺铜会发生贫血、腹泄等病症，但过量摄入铜亦会产生危害。
- 铜对水生生物的危害较大，有人认为铜对鱼类的毒性浓度始于0.002mg/L，但一般认为水体含铜0.01 mg/L对鱼类是安全的。
- Copper is an essential trace element in the human body. Anemia, diarrhea and other diseases may occur in the absence of copper, but excessive intake of copper may also cause toxicity.
- Copper is more harmful to aquatic organisms. Some people think that the toxicity concentration of copper on fish starts at 0.002mg/L, but it is generally believed that water containing under 0.01 mg/L of copper is safe for fish.

# 六、锌 Zinc

电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法(ICP-OES)

锌也是人体必不可少的有益元素，每升水含数毫克锌对人体和温血动物无害，但对鱼类和其他水生生物影响较大。锌对鱼类的安全浓度约为0.1mg/L。

Zinc is also an indispensable element for human body. A few milligrams of zinc per liter in water is harmless to humans and warm-blooded animals, but has a worse impact on fish and other aquatic organisms. The safe concentration of zinc on fish could not exceed approximately 0.1 mg/L.

# 七、铬 Chromium

- 铬是生物体所必需的微量元素之一。铬的毒性与其存在价态有关，六价铬具有强毒性，为致癌物质，并易被人体吸收而在体内蓄积。
- Chromium is one of the trace elements necessary for living organisms. The toxicity of chromium is related to its valence state. Hexavalent chromium is highly toxic, carcinogenic, and easily absorbed and accumulated in human body.
- 水体、pH、温度、氧化还原性物质、有机物等影响，三价铬和六价铬之间相互转化
- Water properties, pH, temperature, redox species, organic matter, etc.
- The transformation between trivalent chromium and hexavalent chromium

# 八、砷 Arsenic

单质砷毒性极低，而砷的化合物均有剧毒，三价砷化合物比其他砷化物毒性更强。砷化物容易在人体内积累，造成急性或慢性中毒。

The toxicity of As(0) is very low, but the arsenic compounds are all highly toxic, among which the trivalent arsenic compounds are even more toxic than other arsenic compounds. Arsenide is easily accumulated in the human body, causing acute or chronic poisoning.

新银盐分光光度法

# New silver salt spectrophotometry

硼氢化钾（或硼氢化钠）在酸性溶液中产生新生态的氢，将试料中砷转变为砷代氢、用硝酸-硝酸银-聚乙烯醇-乙醇溶液为吸收液，将其中银离子还原成单质银，使溶液呈黄色，在400nm处测量吸光度。

化学反应式如下：

BH + H+ + 3H2O —→ 8[H] + H3BO3

2As3+ + 6[H] —→ 2AsH3↑

6Ag + AsH3 + 3H2O —→ 6Ag + H3AsO3 + 6H+

# 注：

1. 反应管 reaction tube
2. U形管 U-shaped tube
3. 脱胺管 Deamination tube
4. 吸收管 Absorption tube

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 4 · Slide 41】 -->

# 氢化物发生-原子吸收法

# 原理

硼氢化钾或硼氢化钠在酸性溶液中，产生氢，将水样中无机砷还原成砷化氢气体，将其用N₂气载入石英管中，以电加热方式使石英管升温至900~1000℃。砷化氢在此温度下被分解形成砷原子蒸汽，对来自砷光源的特征电磁辐射产生吸收，将测得水样中砷的吸光值和标准吸光值进行比较，确定水样中砷的含量。

Potassium borohydride or sodium borohydride generates hydrogen in acidic solutions, reducing inorganic arsenic to arsine gas. It is then loaded into a quartz tube with N₂ gas and heated by electric heating to 900~1000 ℃. Hydrogen arsenide is decomposed into arsenic vapor at this temperature, which absorbs characteristic electromagnetic radiation from an arsenic light source. The measured absorbance value of arsenic in the water sample is compared with the standard absorbance value to determine the arsenic content in the water sample.

| 1.反应瓶 | 2.水样瓶   | 3.定量加样器 | 4.KBH₁溶液瓶 | 5.流量计 |
| ----- | ------- | ------- | --------- | ----- |
| 6.砷光源 | 7.电热石英管 | 8.分光器   | 9.检测系统    |       |

# 2.6 金属化合物的测定 Measurement of metal compounds

# 一、酸度和碱度 Acidity and Alkalinity

# 酸度 Acidity

- 水中所含能与强碱发生中和作用的物质的总量，包括无机酸、有机酸、强酸弱碱盐等
- The total amount of substances in the water which can neutralize the strong base, including inorganic acids, organic acids, strong acid and weakly acidic salt, etc.
- 酸碱指示剂法 Indicator method：用标准NaOH溶液滴定水样至一定pH，根据其所消耗的NaOH溶液量计算酸度（mg/L）。酚酞指示剂：pH8.3，总酸度（酚酞酸度）；甲基橙指示剂：pH3.7，强酸酸度（甲基橙酸度）
- The water sample is titrated to a certain pH with a standard NaOH solution, and the acidity (mg/L) is calculated according to the amount of the NaOH solution consumed. Phenolphthalein indicator: pH 8.3, total acidity (phenolic acidity); methyl orange indicator: pH 3.7, strong acidity (methyl orange acidity)
- 电位滴定法 Potentiometric titration：用NaOH标准液滴至pH计显示3.7和8.3，计算两种酸度
- Calculate the two types of acidity by using NaOH standard droplets to pH meter showing 3.7 and 8.3
- 阳极溶出伏安法 Anodic stripping voltammetry

# 2.6 金属化合物的测定 Measurement of metal compounds

# 一、酸度和碱度 Acidity and Alkalinity

# 电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法(ICP-OES)

# 碱度 Alkalinity

Inductively coupled plasma-optical emission spectrometer

- 水中所含能与强酸发生中和作用的物质的总量，包括强碱、弱碱、强碱弱酸盐等 The total amount of substances in the water that can neutralize with strong acids, including strong alkaline, weak alkaline, weakly alkaline salt, etc.
- 天然水中碱度由重碳酸盐、碳酸盐和氢氧化物造成 Alkalinity in natural water is due to the existence of bicarbonate, carbonate and hydroxide
- 酸碱指示剂滴定法 Indicator titration：
- 酚酞碱度：标准酸溶液滴定至酚酞指示剂由红色变为无色（pH8.3），此时OH⁻已被中和，CO₃²⁻被中和为HCO₃⁻；
- 甲基橙碱度：继续滴定至甲基橙指示剂由橘黄色变为橘红色（pH4.4），HCO₃⁻已被中和完全，也成为总碱度

# 一、酸度和碱度 Acidity and Alkalinity

水样以酚酞为指示剂滴定消耗强酸量为P，继续以甲基橙为指示剂滴定消耗强酸量为M，二者之和为T。

The consumed amount of strong acid is denoted as “P” when the water sample is titrated with phenolphthalein as indicator. Then the amount of strong acid is denoted as “M” when the water titration is continued by using methyl orange as indicator. The sum of the above is “T”.

# 测定水的总碱度时，可能出现下列5种情况：

Five cases may occur when determining the total alkalinity:

- 1.M=0（P=T）；
- 2.P>M（或P>0.5T）；
- 3.P=M；
- 4.P<m（或p<0 class="5t）；<" li="">
</m（或p<0>
- 5.P=0（或M=T）。

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 4 · Slide 48】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 4 · Slide 48】

# 一、酸度和碱度 Acidity and Alkalinity

水样以酚酞为指示剂滴定消耗强酸量为P，继续以甲基橙为指示剂滴定消耗强酸量为M，二者之和为T。

The water sample is titrated with phenolphthalein as the indicator, consuming a strong acid amount of P, and continuing to titrate with methyl orange as the indicator, consuming a strong acid amount of M. The sum of the two is T.

# 1. M=0（P=T）

水样对酚酞显红色，呈碱性反应。加入强酸使酚酞变为无色后，再加入甲基橙即成橘红色，故可推断水样中只含有氢氧化物。

Red with phenolphthalein as indicator, add acid until no color. Then add methyl orange, solution is orange red, indicating only hydroxides contained in water.

# 2. P>M（或P>0.5T）

水样对酚酞显红色，呈碱性。加入强酸至酚酞变为无色后，加入甲基橙显橘黄色，继续加酸变为橘红色，但消耗量较用酚酞滴定时少，说明水样中有氢氧化物和碳酸盐共存。

Red with phenolphthalein as indicator, add acid until no color. Then add methyl orange, solution is orange. Continue adding acid until the solution changes to orange red. However, at this time, P is higher than M, indicating the coexistence of hydroxides and carbonate.

# 二、pH

# pH和酸度、碱度的区别和联系

• pH表示水的酸碱性的强弱

• 酸度或碱度是水中所含酸或碱物质的含量

• 同样酸度的溶液，如0.1 mol盐酸和0.1 mol乙酸，二者的酸度都是100 mmol/L，但其pH却大不相同.

# 测定水的pH的方法

| method  | pH指示表                  | 阻抗转换 | 甘本 |
| ------- | ---------------------- | ---- | -- |
| • 比色法   | Colorimetric analysis  |      |    |
| • 玻璃电极法 | Glass electrode method |      |    |

# 三、溶解氧 Dissolved oxygen （DO）

# 电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱法(ICP-OES)

Inductively coupled plasma-optical emission spectrometer

- 溶解氧：溶解于水中的分子态氧称为溶解氧。
- Dissolved oxygen: the amount of molecular oxygen dissolved in water.

# 火焰原子吸收光谱法、石墨炉原子吸收光谱法

- 大气压力下降、水温升高、含盐量增加，都会导致溶解氧含量降低。
- DO value will decrease as a result of decreased atmospheric pressure, increased water temperature and salt content.

# 测定水中溶解氧的方法 Method for DO measurement

- 1. 碘量法 Iodometry
- 2. 修正的碘量法 Revised iodometry
- 3. 氧电极法 Clark electrode method
- • 电位分析法 Potentiometry
- 阳极溶出伏安法 Anodic stripping voltammetry

# 1、碘量法 Iodometry (DO)

# 原理 Principle：

| 碱性介质   | 酸性介质          | Na₂S₂O₃     |
| ------ | ------------- | ----------- |
| Mn²⁺   | alkali medium | Mn⁴⁺ acidic |
| ᵐᵉᵈⁱᵘᵐ | I             | 滴定titration |
| DO →   | I-            | → 2         |

MnSO4 + 2NaOH = Na₂SO₄ + Mn(OH)₂↓

2Mn(OH)₂ + O₂ = 2MnO(OH)₂ (棕色沉淀)

MnO(OH)₂ + 2H₂SO₄ = Mn(SO₄)₂ + 3H₂O

Mn(SO₄)₂ + 2KI = MnSO₄ + K₂SO₄ + I₂

2Na₂S₂O₃ + I₂ = Na₂S₄O₆ + 2NaI

# 2.6 金属化合物的测定 Measurement of metal compounds

# 四、氰化物 Cyanide

- 氰化物包括简单氰化物、络合氰化物和有机氰化物（腈）。简单氰化物易溶于水、毒性大；络合氰化物在水体中受pH值、水温和光照等影响离解为毒性强的简单氰化物。
- Cyanide includes simple, complex and organic cyanide (nitrile). Simple cyanide is highly soluble in water and is highly toxic; Complex cyanide in water can be dissociated to simple cyanide if influenced by pH, water temperature and light.

# 测定方法 method

- 硝酸银滴定法 Silver nitrate titration
- 适用于高浓度水样 Suitable for water samples with high concentration of cyanide
- 分光光度法 Spectrophotometry
- 分为异烟酸-吡唑啉酮分光光度法和异烟酸-巴比妥酸分光光度法，灵敏度高，是广泛应用的方法
- Divided into isonicotinic acid-pyrazolone spectrophotometry and isonicotinic acid-barbituric acid spectrophotometry. High sensitivity, widely used
- 催化快速比色法 Fast colorimetry with catalyst
- 适用于环境污染事故应急监测的快读定性和半定量方法
- Fast-reading qualitative and semi-quantitative methods are suitable for emergency monitoring of environmental pollution accidents
- 离子选择电极法 Ion selective electrode method
- 不稳定 Unstable
- Anodic stripping voltammetry

色谱 Chromatography
# 离子色谱的检测方式

# Detection Methods of IC

色谱又称色层法或层析法，是一种物理化学分析方法，它利用不同溶质（样品）与固定相和流动相之间的作用力（分配、吸附、离子交换等）的差别，当两相做相对移动时，各溶质在两相间进行多次平衡，使各溶质达到相互分离。

Chromatography, also known as chromatography or chromatography, is a physical and chemical analysis method that utilizes the differences in forces (distribution, adsorption, ion exchange, etc.) between different solutes (samples) and the stationary and mobile phases. When the two phases move relative to each other, each solute undergoes multiple equilibrations between the two phases to achieve mutual separation.

紫外直接吸收或可见光光度法测定选择性强，可进行柱后衍生。

离子色谱的优点

# Advantages

- 1 快速、方便（quick, convenient）；
- 6 对常规的7种阴离子(F⁻、Cl⁻、Br⁻、NO²⁻、NO³⁻、SO⁴²⁻、PO⁴³⁻)，种常见的阳离子(Li⁺、Na⁺、NH₄⁺、K⁺、Mg²⁺、Ca²⁺)分析时间小于10min。
- 2 灵敏度高(high sensitivity)；分析浓度为ug/L(ppb)～mg/L(ppm)，最低可达10⁻¹²g/L。
- 3 选择性好(good selectivity)；
- 4 可同时分析多种离子化合物(analysze multiple compounds the same time)；
- 5 分离柱的稳定性好，容量高(separation column is stable and has high capacity)。苯乙烯/二乙烯苯填料高pH及有机溶剂稳定。

# 离子选择电极法：

Ion selective electrode method

- 氟离子选择电极是一种以氟化镧(LaF₃)单晶片为敏感膜的传感器
- Fluoride ion selective electrode is a sensor with a lanthanum fluoride (LaF3) single wafer as a sensitive film
- 由于单晶结构对能进入晶格交换的离子有严格的限制，所以有良好的选择性。
- Since the single crystal structure has strict limits on the ions that can enter the lattice, it has good selectivity.

1. 试液
2. 1.5mol/I.HNO
3. 切换阀
4. 蠕动泵
5. 树脂柱
6. 火焰原子吸收分光光度计
7. 记录仪

# 三、溶解氧 Dissolved oxygen （DO）

- 溶解氧：溶解于水中的分子态氧称为溶解氧。
- Dissolved oxygen: the amount of molecular oxygen dissolved in water.
- 大气压力下降、水温升高、含盐量增加，都会导致溶解氧含量降低。
- DO value will decrease as a result of decreased atmospheric pressure, increased water temperature and salt content.

# 测定水中溶解氧的方法 Method for DO measurement

1. 碘量法 Iodometry ·2.9底质检测
2. 修正的碘量法 Revised iodometry
3. 氧电极法 Clark electrode method

# 四、氰化物 Cyanide

- 氰化物包括简单氰化物、络合氰化物和有机氰化物（腈）。简单氰化物易溶于水、毒性大；络合氰化物在水体中受pH值、水温和光照等影响离解为毒性强的简单氰化物。
- Cyanide includes simple, complex and organic cyanide (nitrile). Simple cyanide is highly soluble in water and is highly toxic; Complex cyanide in water can be dissociated to simple cyanide if influenced by pH, water temperature and light.

# 测定方法 method

| -------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| 催化快速比色法 Fast colorimetry with catalyst | 适用于环境污染事故应急监测的快读定性和半定量方法                                               |
| 离子选择电极法 Ion selective electrode method | 不稳定 Unstable                                                           |

硝酸银滴定法 Silver nitrate titration
取一定体积水样预蒸馏溶液，调节至pH为1以上,以试银灵作指示剂，用硝酸银标准溶液滴定,则氰离子与银离子生成银氰络合物[Ag（CN),]-，稍过量的银离子与试银灵反应，使溶液由黄色变为橙红色，即为终点。反应式如下：

Ag+ + 2CN− = Ag(CN)2−

HN-       -C=0  N(CH3)2ᴼᴴ− Ag—N-  -C=0 N(CH3)2 + H2O

Ag+ + S=C  C=CH—                   C=CH-

<c>             , S=C</c>

S                         S

（黄色)                            （橙红色)

另取与水样预蒸馏液同体积空白实验馏出液，按水样测定方法进行空白试验。根据二者消耗硝酸银标准溶液体积，按下式计算水样中氰化物浓度：

(VA-VB)·c×52.04 V2

式中:

- VA          滴定水样消耗硝酸银标准溶液量，mL;
- VB—滴定空白馏出液消耗硝酸银标准溶液量，mL;
- c     一硝酸银标准溶液浓度，mol/L;
- V1    一水样体积，mL;
- V2 一水样馏出液总体积，mL;
- V3             测定时所取水样馏出液体积，mL;

色谱 Chromatography

色谱又称色层法或层析法，是一种物理化学分析方法，它利用不同溶质（样品）与固定相和流动相之间的作用力（分配、吸附、离子交换等）的差别，当两相做相对移动时，各溶质在两相间进行多次平衡，使各溶质达到相互分离。

Chromatography, also known as chromatography or chromatography, is a physical and chemical analysis method that utilizes the differences in forces (distribution, adsorption, ion exchange, etc.) between different solutes (samples) and the stationary and mobile phases. When the two phases move relative to each other, each solute undergoes multiple equilibrations between the two phases to achieve mutual separation.

# 离子色谱的检测方式

# Detection Methods of IC

色谱又称色层法或层析法，是一种物理化学分析方法，它利用不同溶质（样品）与固定相和流动相之间的作用力（分配、吸附、离子交换等）的差别，当两相做相对移动时，各溶质在两相间进行多次平衡，使各溶质达到检测具有电导性化合物的通用型检测器。

# A Universal Detector for the Detection of Conductive Compounds

| -------- | ------------------------------------- |

| 荧光法      | Fluorescence method                   |

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 20】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 20】

离子色谱的优点

# Advantages

- 1 快速、方便（quick, convenient）；
- 6 对常规的7种阴离子(F⁻、Cl⁻、Br⁻、NO²⁻、NO³⁻、SO⁴²⁻、PO⁴³⁻)，种常见的阳离子(Li⁺、Na⁺、NH₄⁺、K⁺、Mg²⁺、Ca²⁺)分析时间小于10min。
- 2 灵敏度高(high sensitivity)；分析浓度为ug/L(ppb)～mg/L(ppm)，最低可达10⁻¹²g/L。
- 3 选择性好(good selectivity)；
- 4 可同时分析多种离子化合物(analysze multiple compounds the same time)；
- 5 分离柱的稳定性好，容量高(separation column is stable and has high capacity)。苯乙烯/二乙烯苯填料高pH及有机溶剂稳定。

> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 21】

# 离子选择电极法：

# Ion selective electrode method

- 氟离子选择电极是一种以氟化镧(LaF₃)单晶片为敏感膜的传感器
- Fluoride ion selective electrode is a sensor with a lanthanum fluoride (LaF3) single wafer as a sensitive film
- 由于单晶结构对能进入晶格交换的离子有严格的限制，所以有良好的选择性。
- Since the single crystal structure has strict limits on the ions that can enter the lattice, it has good selectivity.

对于氟离子选择电极，较佳的试剂酸度条件为pH5~6。

1. LaF3单晶膜； 2. 内参比浴液 pH&#x3C;5时，溶液中会发生下述弱酸配位反应：2F- + H+ = HF + F = HF2，使溶液中的F减少，会影响电极的灵敏度，使分析结果偏低。这是由于氟电极只对F响应对HF或HF2无响应，而且氟电极的氟化镧电极膜会增大被溶解，影响测定。

3. Ag-AgCl（内参比）电极； 4. 电极管

# F-选择电极原理示意图

# Schematic diagram of the fluoride ion selective electrode

当pH>8时，OH对电极的响应，将严重影响测定结果，使分析结果偏高。有研究表明，OH对氟电极的干扰还由于OH与膜表面发生化学反应，而引入试液额外的F。其反应式为：LaF3 + 3OH- = La(OH)3 + 3F-

# 氮 Nitrogen

# Measurement

有机氮：使用凯氏定氮法 。先煮沸以去除氨，然后消解，有机氮通过热和酸的作用转化为铵。

Organic nitrogen: Kjeldahl method. First boil the water to remove the ammonia, then digest to convert the organic nitrogen to ammonium through the action of heat and acid.

总凯氏氮：与有机氮相同，只是不去除氨。

Total Kjeldahl nitrogen (TKN): same as organic nitrogen except that the ammonia is not driven off.

氨：提高pH值，用蒸汽蒸馏出氨，冷凝蒸汽，比色测定，滴定测量，或用特定离子电极测量。

Ammonia: raising pH, distilling off the ammonia with the steam, condensing the steam, determined with colorimetry, measured with titration or with specific-ion electrodes.

亚硝酸盐：比色测定，不稳定，易氧化成硝酸盐。

Nitrite: determined with colorimetry, unstable and easily oxidized to the nitrate form.

硝酸盐：氧化最多的氮，用比色法测定，或用特定离子电极测定。

Nitrate: the most oxidized nitrogen, determined with colorimetry, or with specific-ion electrodes.

# （二）亚硝酸盐氮 nitrite

• 亚硝酸盐氮（NO₂⁻-N）是氮循环的中间产物。在氧和微生物的作用下， 可被氧化成硝酸盐；在缺氧条件下也可被还原为氨。

• Nitrite nitrogen (NO₂⁻-N) is an intermediate product of the nitrogen cycle. When there is oxygen, it can be oxidized to nitrate by some microorganisms; it can also be reduced to ammonia under anoxic conditions.

# （三）硝酸盐氮 Nitrate

- 硝酸盐是在有氧环境中最稳定的含氮化合物，也是含氮有机化合物经无机化作用最终阶段的分解产物。清洁的地面水硝酸盐氮（NO₃⁻-N）含量较低，受污染水体和一些深层地下水中（NO₃⁻-N）含量较高。
- Nitrate is the most stable nitrogen-containing compound in the aerobic environment.
- It is also a decomposition product of nitrogen-containing organic compounds in the final stage of inorganication.
- The clean surface water has a low content of nitrate nitrogen (NO₃⁻-N), and the contaminated water and some deep groundwater (NO₃⁻-N) have a high content.

# 2.4水样的预处理

# 2.9底质检测

# （三）硝酸盐氮 Nitrate

# • 水中硝酸盐氮的测定方法

Method for determination of nitrate nitrogen in water

- 酚二磺酸分光光度法
Phenol disulfonic acid spectrophotometry
- 气相分子吸收光谱法：NO₂⁻的干扰
Gas molecular absorption spectrometry: NO₂⁻ interference
- 紫外分光光度法
Ultraviolet spectrophotometry
