<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 31】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 31】

# （四）凯氏氮 Kjeldahl nitrogen

• 凯氏氮是指以凯氏（Kjeldahl）法测得的含氮量。它包括氨氮和在此条件下能转化为铵盐而被测定的有机氮化合物。

• Kjeldahl nitrogen refers to the nitrogen content measured by the Kjeldahl method. It includes ammonia nitrogen and organic nitrogen compound which can be determined by converting to an ammonium salt under such conditions.

# ·2.6金属和类金属化合物的测定

• 在评价湖泊、水库等水体富营养化时，是有意义的指标。

• It is a meaningful indicator when evaluating the eutrophication of water bodies such as lakes and reservoirs.

• 可用凯氏氮与氨氮的差值表示有机氮含量。

• The difference between Kjeldahl nitrogen and ammonia nitrogen can be used to indicate the organic nitrogen content.

# ·2.9底质检测

• 将有机氮转变成氨氮，然后在碱性介质中蒸馏出氨，用硼酸溶液吸收，以分光光度法或滴定法测定氨氮含量，即为水样中的凯氏氮含量。

• The organic nitrogen is converted into ammonia nitrogen, and then the ammonia is distilled off in an alkaline medium, and absorbed by a boric acid solution, and the ammonia nitrogen content is determined by spectrophotometry or titration, which is the Kjeldahl nitrogen content in the water sample.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 32】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 32】

# （五）总氮 Total nitrogen

• 水中的总氮含量是衡量水质的重要指标之一。 The total nitrogen content in water is one of the important indicators for measuring water quality.

• 其测定方法通常采用过硫酸钾氧化，使有机氮和无机氮化合物转变为硝酸盐，用紫外分光光度法或离子色谱法、气相分子吸收光谱法测定。

• It is usually measured by using potassium persulfate to oxidize, to convert organic nitrogen and inorganic nitrogen compounds into nitrates, and then measure by ultraviolet spectrophotometry, ion chromatography or gas molecular absorption spectrometry.

七、硫化物 Sulfide
# 第2章水质监测

- 地下水（特别是温泉水）及生活污水常含有硫化物。

Groundwater (especially hot spring water) and domestic sewage often contain sulfides.
- 一部分是在厌氧条件下，由于微生物的作用，使硫酸盐还原或含硫有机物分解而产生的。

Part of it is produced by the action of microorganisms to reduce sulfate or decompose sulfur-containing organic matter under the anaerobic conditions.
- 焦化、造气、选矿、造纸、印染、制革等工业废水中亦含有硫化物。

Industrial wastewater such as coking, gasification, beneficiation, paper making, printing and dyeing, and tanning also contains sulfides.
- 包括溶解性的H₂S，HS⁻，S²⁻，酸溶性的金属硫化物，以及不溶性的硫化物和有机硫化物。

These include soluble H₂S, HS⁻, S²⁻, acid-soluble metal sulfides, as well as insoluble sulfides and organic sulfides.

# 间接火焰原子吸收法：

- 在水样中加入磷酸，将硫化物转化为硫化氢，用氮气带出
- Adding phosphoric acid into the water sample to convert the sulfide to hydrogen sulfide, and then bring it out with nitrogen.
- 通入含有一定量的铜离子吸收液
- Adding an amount of copper ion absorbing solution
- 生成硫化铜沉淀
- Producing copper sulfide precipitate
- 分离沉淀，用火焰原子吸收法测上清液中的铜离子
- The precipitate was isolated, and copper ions in the supernatant are measured by flame atomic absorption

1. 反应瓶（装待测水样）；
2. 流量计；
3. 加酸漏斗；
4. 吸收管

# 气相分子吸收光谱法：

- 气相分子吸收光谱法是利用基态的气体分子能吸收特定紫外光谱的一种测量方法，利用气体的分子振动吸收原理，气体浓度与吸光度呈现一定的线性关系。
- Gas phase molecular absorption spectroscopy is a measurement method that utilizes the absorption of specific ultraviolet spectra by gas molecules in the ground state. By utilizing the principle of molecular vibration absorption of gases, there is a linear relationship between gas concentration and absorbance.

# 八、磷(总磷、溶解性磷酸盐和溶解性总磷)

# Phosphorus (total phosphorus, dissolved phosphate and dissolved total phosphorus)

- 在天然水和废（污）水中，磷主要以各种磷酸盐和有机磷（如磷脂等）形式存在，也存在于腐殖质粒子和水生生物中。磷是生物生长必需元素之一，但水体中磷含量过高，会导致富营养化，使水质恶化。
- In natural water and waste (sewage) water, phosphorus mainly exists as phosphates and organic phosphorus (such as phospholipids).
- It is also existed in humic agents and aquatic organisms.
- Phosphorus is one of the essential elements for biological growth, but the high phosphorus content in the water body can lead to eutrophication and deteriorates the water quality.

磷 Phosphorus

# 正磷酸盐 Orthophosphates:

PO₄³⁻, HPO₄²⁻, H₂PO₄⁻, H₃PO₄

通过直接添加诸如钼酸铵的物质来确定，该物质可与磷酸盐形成有色络合物

Determined by directly adding a substance such as ammonium molybdate which will form a colored complex with the phosphate

# 多磷酸盐 Polyphosphate:

具有两个或多个磷原子，氧原子或氢原子，可在水溶液中进行水解并转化为正磷酸盐

with two or more phosphorus atoms, oxygen atoms, or hydrogen atoms undergo hydrolysis in aqueous solutions and convert to the orthophosphate

# 有机结合磷 Organically bound phosphorus:

在大多数市政污水中含量较少，但可能是工业废物和废水污泥的重要组成部分。可用酸消化转化为正磷酸盐，然后测定

an important constituent of industrial wastes and wastewater sludge. It can convert to orthophosphates using acid digestion for measurement.

# （一）化学需氧量(COD)

# Chemical oxygen demand

•  化学需氧量是指在一定条件下，氧化1 L水样中还原性物质所消耗的氧化剂的量，以氧的mg/L表示。

Chemical oxygen demand refers to the amount of oxidant consumed by reducing substances in a 1 L water sample under certain conditions, expressed in mg/L of oxygen.

•  水中的还原性物质：有机物、亚硝酸盐、硫化物、亚铁盐等

Reducing substances in water: organic matter, nitrite, sulfide, ferrous salt, etc.

# 测定方法（Determination method）：

1. 重铬酸钾法（最经典）Potassium dichromate method (classic)
2. 库仑滴定法 Coulometric titration method
3. 快速密闭消解滴定法或光度法 Rapid closed digestion titration or spectrophotometry
4. 氯气校正法 Chlorine correction method

# 1. 重铬酸钾法测COD   Potassium dichromate method

- 重铬酸钾氧化性很强，可将大部分有机物氧化，但吡啶不被氧化，芳香族有机化合物不易被氧化；
- 挥发性直链脂肪族化合物、苯等存在于蒸气相，不能与氧化剂液体接触，氧化不明显。

# 2.6 金属和类金属化合物的测定

- 氯离子能被重铬酸钾氧化，并与硫酸银作用生成沉淀，可加入适量硫酸汞络合。

Potassium dichromate have a strong oxidizing ability and can oxidize most of the organic matters, but pyridine can not be oxidized, and aromatic organic compounds are not easily oxidized too;

Volatile linear aliphatic compounds, benzene, etc. exist in the vapor phase and cannot contact with the oxidant liquid, so the oxidation is not obvious.

Chloride ion can be oxidized by potassium dichromate and reacted with silver sulfate to form precipitate, which can complex with mercury sulfate.

| K2Cr2O7回流. | K2Cr2O7  | Ag2SOI催化 | 亚铁灵指示剂 |

# 终点 End point

# 2. 库仑滴定 Coulometric titration

- 建立在电解基础上 on the basis of electrolysis
- 在溶液中加入适当物质，一定强度恒定电流电解，在工作电极电解产生一种试剂
- Adding a suitable substance to the solution; electrolyzing at a constant intensity and constant current; generating a reagent at the working electrode
- 试剂与被测物质进行定量反应，终点通过电化学方法指示
- The reagent is quantitatively reacted with the test substance, and the end point can be indicated by the electrochemical method
- 依据电解消耗电荷量和法拉第电解定律计算被测物质含量
- According to the amount of charge consumed by electrolysis and Faraday's law of electrolysis, we can calculate the content of the substance to be tested.

W = I t M

96500 n

式中：

- W—— (Mass of electrode reactant)
- I——电解电流 (Electrolytic current)；
- t——电解时间 (Electrolysis time)；
- 96500——法拉第常数 (Faraday constant)；
- M——电极反应物的摩尔质量 (Molar mass of electrode reactant)；
- n——每摩尔电极反应物的电子转移数 (Number of electron transfer per mole of electrode reactant)。

# （二）高锰酸盐指数(IMn) Permanganate index

• 以高锰酸钾溶液为氧化剂测得的化学需氧量，称高锰酸盐指数，以氧的质量浓度（mg/L）表示。

• The chemical oxygen demand measured by using potassium permanganate (KMnO₄) solution as the oxidant is called permanganate index and expressed by the mass concentration of oxygen (mg/L).

• 该指数常被作为反映地表水受有机物和还原性无机物污染程度的综合指标。

This index is often used as a comprehensive indicator for the level of organic matters and reducing inorganic matters in surface water.

# 2.9 底质检测

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 51】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 51】

# （二）高锰酸盐指数(IMn) Permanganate index

- 按测定溶液的介质不同分类（碱性条件下高锰酸钾氧化能力较弱）Classified according to the medium of the determination solution (The oxidizing ability of potassium permanganate is weak under alkaline conditions)

化学需氧量（CODCr）和高锰酸盐指数（IMn）是采用不同的氧化剂在各自的氧化条件下测定的，难以找出明显的相关关系。一般来说，重铬酸钾法的氧化率可达90%，而高锰酸钾法的氧化率为50%左右，两者均未完全氧化，因而都只是一个相对参考数据

Chemical oxygen demand (CODCr) and permanganate index were determined using different oxidants under their respective oxidative conditions. The oxidation rate of the potassium dichromate method and potassium permanganate method can reach 90% and 50% respectively.

# （三）生化需氧量(BOD)

# Biochemical Oxygen Demand

• 生化需氧量是指在有溶解氧的条件下，好氧微生物在分解水中有机物的生物化学氧化过程中所消耗的溶解氧量。

• Biochemical oxygen demand refers to the amount of dissolved oxygen consumed by aerobic microorganisms in the biochemical oxidation process for decomposing organic matters in water.

• 有机物在微生物作用下，好氧分解分两个阶段：含碳有机物氧化阶段，硝化阶段

• The aerobic decomposition of organic matters can be divided into two stages: the carbon-containing organic matters oxidation stage and the nitrification stage.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 53】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 53】

# （三）生化需氧量(BOD)

# Biochemical Oxygen Demand

BOD是反映水体被有机物污染程度的综合指标，也是研究废水的可生化降解性和生化处理效果，以及生化处理废水工艺设计和动力学研究中的重要参数。

# 2.1 水体污染与监测

BOD is a comprehensive indicator reflecting the degree of water contamination by organic matters.

An important parameter for studying the biodegradability and effect of biochemical treatment, the process design and kinetics of biochemical wastewater treatment.

# 2.6 金属和类金属化合物的测定

# 2.9 底质检测

# 测定方法 Method

- ❑ 五天培养法（最经典） 5-day incubation method
- ❑ 微生物电极法 Microbial electrode method
- ❑ 其他方法 Other methods

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 54】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 54】

# Biochemical Oxygen Demand (BOD)

微生物用于分解水中有机物的氧气量

Amount of oxygen used by microorganisms to decompose organic matters in the water

理论BOD可以通过平衡化学方程来确定，其中所有有机物质都转化为CO₂

Theoretical BOD can be determined by balancing a chemical equation in which all organic matter is converted to CO₂

# Example

计算1.67 x 10⁻³mol葡萄糖（C₆H₁₂O₆）的理论需氧量：

Calculate the theoretical oxygen demand of 1.67 x 10⁻³ moles of glucose (C₆H₁₂O₆):

C₆H₁₂O₆ + O₂ 🡪 CO₂ + H₂O

C₆H₁₂O₆ + 6 O₂ 🡪 6 CO₂ + 6 H₂O

1.67x 10⁻³mol glucose/L ⨯ 6 mol O₂/ mol glucose ⨯ 32 g O₂/mol O₂ = 0.321 g O₂/L = 321 mg O₂/L

# Biochemical Oxygen Demand (BOD)

废水和地表水应用最广泛的有机污染参数是（BOD₅）。

The most widely used parameter of organic pollution applied to both wastewater and surface water is the 5-day BOD (BOD₅).

涉及微生物在有机物生化氧化中使用的溶解氧的测量

Involves the measurement of the dissolved oxygen used by microorganisms in the biochemical oxidation of organic matter.

| Under: | Specified Time        | 5 days                      |
| ------ | --------------------- | --------------------------- |
|        | Specified Temperature | 20 °C                       |
|        | Specified Conditions  | Dark                        |
|        |                       | In the Presence of Bacteria |

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 56】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 56】

# 1. 五天培养法 (BOD₅法：HJ 505-2009)        5-day incubation method

❑定义：也称标准稀释法。其测定原理是水样经稀释后，在20±1℃条件下培养5天，求出培养前后水样中溶解氧含量，二者的差值为BOD₅。如果水样五日生化需氧量超过6 mg/L，则需进行稀释，反之则可直接测定(如较清洁的河水)。

❑Standard dilution method. The water samples are incubated at 20±1°C for 5 days after dilution. Then determine the DO content in the water sample before and after the culture process. The difference between the two value.

❑If the water sample has a BOD₅ value >6 mg/L, it needs to be diluted, otherwise it can be directly measured (such as cleaner river water).

❑对于不含或少含微生物的工业废水，如酸性废水、碱性废水、高温废水或经过氯化处理的废水，在测定BOD₅时应进行接种，以引入能降解废水中有机物的微生物。

❑For industrial wastewater containing no or very little microorganisms, such as acidic wastewater, alkaline wastewater, high-temperature wastewater or chlorinated wastewater, microorganisms should be introduced to degrade organic matters in the wastewater.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 57】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 57】

1. 五天培养法 (BOD₅法：HJ 505-2009)       5-day incubation method

✓当废水中存在着难被一般生活污水中的微生物以正常速度降解的有机物或有剧毒物质时，应将驯化后的微生物引入水样中进行接种；

When the organic substances or highly toxic substances are difficult to be degraded by the microorganisms at a normal speed, the acclimation is needed for microorganisms for inoculation;

✓若水样中含有硝化细菌，应加入适量丙烯基硫脲溶液，抑制硝化反应；

If the water sample contains nitrifying bacteria, an appropriate amount of propylene thiourea solution should be added to inhibit the nitrification reaction;

✓污染的地表水和多数工业废水，含较多有机物，需稀释后再测定，以保证培养过程中有充足的溶解氧；

If the content of organic matters is too high, the water should be diluted before measurement to ensure sufficient dissolved oxygen during the cultivation process;

✓水样稀释程度应使五日培养中所消耗的溶解氧大于2 mg/L, 剩余溶解氧在2 mg/L以上

The dilution of water sample should make the DO consumed in the five-day cultivation >2 mg/L, and the remaining DO >2 mg/L.

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 58】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 58】

# 稀释水 Dilution water

- 稀释水一般用蒸馏水配制 usually prepared by distilled water
- 1) 干净无有机物 Clean and free of organic matters
- 2) (20±1)℃ 曝气 2-8h ， 使水中溶解氧 (DO) 接近饱和 。 (20±1) ℃ aeration for 2-8h, to make DO close to saturation.
- 3) 临用前加入少量氯化钙、氯化铁、硫酸镁等营养盐溶液及磷酸盐缓冲溶液 ， 混匀备用 。 Add a small amount of mixture from nutrient salt solution such as CaCl₂, FeCl₃, MgSO₄, or phosphate buffer solution.
- 4) 稀释水的最佳pH值为7.2，BOD₅应小于0.2mg/L。The optimum pH of the dilution water is 7.2, and the BOD₅ should be less than 0.2 mg/L.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 59】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 59】

# 稀释水 Dilution water

- 如水样中无微生物，则应在稀释水中加入接种液. If there is no microorganism in the water sample, the inoculum solution should be added to the dilution water. 即在每升稀释水中加入生活污水上层清液1—10mL，或表层土壤浸出液20—30mL，或河水、湖水10—100mL。这种水称为接种稀释水。 Adding 1-10 mL of the supernatant of the domestic sewage, or 20-30 mL of the surface soil leachate, or 10-100 mL of the river water and the lake water into per liter of diluted water.
- 为检查稀释水和接种液的质量，以及化验人员的操作水平，将每升含葡萄糖和谷氨酸各150mg的标准溶液以1∶50稀释比稀释后，与水样同步测定BOD₅，测得值应在180-230mg/L之间，否则，应检查原因，予以纠正。 To check the quality of the dilution water, the inoculum, and the operation level of the laboratory personnel, the standard solution containing 150 mg of glucose and glutamic acid per liter is diluted at a dilution ratio of 1:50, and then measure the BOD₅ value together with the water sample. The value should be between 180-230mg/L. Otherwise, the cause should be checked and corrected.

# Biochemical Oxygen Demand (BOD)

# Nitrification in the BOD Test

与氨氧化成硝酸盐相关的需氧量称为含氮生化需氧量（NBOD）

The oxygen demand associated with the oxidation of ammonia to nitrate is called the nitrogenous biochemical oxygen demand (NBOD)

# Conversion of ammonia to nitrite:

NH₃ + 3/2O₂ → HNO₂ + H₂O

# Conversion of nitrite to nitrate:

HNO2 + 1/2O₂ → HNO₃

# Overall conversion of ammonia to nitrate:

NH3 + 2O₂ → HNO₃ + H₂O

# 碳质BOD

碳质生化需氧量（CBOD）是氧化样品中可氧化碳的需氧量的量度

Carbonaceous biochemical oxygen demand (CBOD) is a measure of the oxygen demand exerted by the oxidizable carbon in the sample.

# 有机物好氧分解的两个阶段

# 第一阶段----含碳物质氧化阶段

主要是含碳有机物氧化为二氧化碳和水。

The carbonaceous organic matter oxidized to carbon dioxide and water

# 第二阶段----硝化阶段

主要是含氮有机化合物在硝化菌的作用下分解为亚硝酸盐和硝酸盐。对生活污水及性质与其接近的工业废水，硝化阶段大约在5-7日以后才显著进行，故常用的20℃五天培养法(BOD₅法)测定BOD值一般不包括硝化阶段的。

The nitrogen-containing organic compounds are decomposed into nitrites and nitrates by the action of nitrifying bacteria. For industrial wastewater and domestic sewage which has similar properties with it, the nitrification stage is only significant after 5-7 days. Therefore, the commonly used 20°C 5-day incubation method (BOD₅ method) generally does not include the nitrification stage.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 74】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 74】

# （四）总有机碳（TOC）

# Total organic carbon

• 有机碳是以碳的含量表示水体中有机物质总量的综合指标

Organic carbon is a comprehensive indicator of the total amount of organic matters in water expressed by the content of carbon.

由于TOC的测定采用燃烧法，因此能将有机物全部氧化，它比BOD₅或COD更能反映有机物的总量。

Since the TOC is measured by a combustion method, the organic matter can be completely oxidized, and it reflects the total amount of organic matter more than BOD₅ or COD.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 75】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 75】

# （四）总有机碳（TOC）Total organic carbon

- 目前广泛应用的测定TOC的方法是燃烧氧化-非色散红外吸收法。
- The currently widely used method for determining TOC is combustion oxidation-non-dispersive infrared absorption method.

其测定原理是：将一定量水样注入高温炉内的石英管，在900-950℃温度下，以铂和三氧化钴或三氧化二铬为催化剂，使有机物燃烧裂解转化为二氧化碳，然后用红外线气体分析仪测定CO₂含量，从而确定水样中碳的含量。因为在高温下，水样中的碳酸盐也分解产生二氧化碳，故上面测得的为水样中的总碳(TC)。

A certain amount of water sample is injected into a quartz tube of the high-temperature furnace. Platinum, cobalt trioxide or chromium trioxide is used as a catalyst to convert the organic matters into carbon dioxide by combustion at the temperature of 900-950℃. Then use the infrared gas analyzer measures the CO₂ content to determine the carbon content of the water sample. Since the carbonate in the water sample can also decomposes to produce carbon dioxide at high temperatures, the total carbon (TC) in the water sample can be measured by the method introduced above.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 76】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 76】

# 有机碳的测定

# Determination of organic carbon

- 为获得有机碳含量，可采用两种方法：
- 2.1水体污染与监测
- 2.6金属和类金属化合物的测定
- 一：将水样预先酸化，通入氮气曝气，驱除各种碳酸盐分解生成的二氧化碳后再注入仪器测定。
- 1. The water sample is pre-acidified, and N₂ is introduced to remove CO₂ generated by decomposition of various carbonates, and then injected into the instrument for measurement.
- 2.5水样物理指标测定
- 2.9底质检测
- 2.10活性污泥性质的测定

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 77】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 77】

有机碳的测定 Determination of organic carbon

# 二：使用高温炉和低温炉皆有的TOC测定仪

将同一等量水样分别注入高温炉(900℃)和低温炉(150℃)，水样中有机碳和无机碳均转化为CO₂，低温炉石英管中装有磷酸浸渍的玻璃棉，使无机碳酸盐在150℃分解为CO₂，有机物却不能被分解氧化。将高、低温炉中生成的CO₂依次导入非色散红外气体分析仪，分别测得总碳(TC)和无机碳(IC)，二者之差即为总有机碳(TOC)。该方法检出限0.1mg/L, 测定下限为0.5mg/L。

TOC meter with both high temperature furnaces and low temperature furnaces. When the same amount of water is injected into high temperature furnace (900℃) and low temperature furnace (150 C), the organic carbon and inorganic carbon are converted into CO₂, and the quartz tube of the low temperature furnace is filled with phosphoric acid impregnated glass cotton, which can decompose inorganic carbonates into CO₂ at 150°C, but organic matter cannot be. The CO₂ generated in high and low temperature furnaces is sequentially introduced into a non-dispersive infrared gas analyzer, and the total carbon (TC) and inorganic carbon (IC) are respectively measured, and the difference between the two is the total organic carbon (TOC). The detection limit of this method is 0.1 mg/L, and the lower limit of measurement is 0.5 mg/L.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 78】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 5 · Slide 78】

# BOD，COD和TOC之间的相互关系

# Interrelationships between BOD, COD, and TOC

- 未经处理的城市废水的BOD / COD比率的典型值在0.3-0.8的范围内。Typical values for the ratio of BOD/COD for untreated municipal wastewater are in the range of 0.3-0.8.
- 如果未经处理废水的BOD / COD比率为0.5或更高，则认为废物易于通过生物方法处理。If the BOD/COD ratio for untreated wastewater is 0.5 or greater, the waste is considered to be easily treatable by biological means.
- 如果该比率低于约0.3，则废物可能具有一些有毒成分或者其稳定化可能某些经过驯化的微生物。If the ratio is below about 0.3, either the waste may have some toxic components or acclimated microorganisms may be required in its stabilization.
- 未经处理废水的相应BOD / TOC比率在1.2至2.0之间变化 The corresponding BOD/TOC ratio for untreated wastewater varies from 1.2 to 2.0.
- 在使用这些比例时，重要的是要记住它们会随着废物的处理程度而发生显着变化。When using the ratios it is important to remember that they will change significantly with the degree of treatment the waste has undergone.

| Type of wastewater     | BOD/COD  | BOD/TOC  |
| ---------------------- | -------- | -------- |
| Untreated              | 0.3-0.8  | 1.2-2.0  |
| After primary settling | 0.4-0.6  | 0.8-1.2  |
| Final effluent         | 0.1-0.3a | 0.2-0.5b |

aCBOD/COD.

bCBOD/TOC.

# 2.8 有机污染物的测定

# Determination of organic pollutants

# 一、综合指标和类别指标

# Comprehensive index and category index

- 化学需氧量(COD) *Chemical oxygen demand*
- 高锰酸盐指数 *Permanganate index*
- 生化需氧量（BOD） *Biochemical oxygen demand*
- 总有机碳（TOC） *Total organic carbon*
- 挥发酚 *Volatile phenol*
- 硝基苯类 *Nitrobenzene*
- 石油类 *Petroleum*

# （一）化学需氧量(COD)

# Chemical oxygen demand

•  化学需氧量是指在一定条件下，氧化1 L水样中还原性物质所消耗的氧化剂的量，以氧的mg/L表示。

Chemical oxygen demand refers to the amount of oxidant consumed by reducing substances in a 1 L water sample under certain conditions, expressed in mg/L of oxygen.

•  水中的还原性物质：有机物、亚硝酸盐、硫化物、亚铁盐等

Reducing substances in water: organic matter, nitrite, sulfide, ferrous salt, etc.

# 测定方法（Determination method）：

1. 重铬酸钾法（最经典）Potassium dichromate method (classic)
2. 库仑滴定法 Coulometric titration method
3. 快速密闭消解滴定法或光度法 Rapid closed digestion titration or spectrophotometry
4. 氯气校正法 Chlorine correction method

# 1. 重铬酸钾法测COD

# Potassium dichromate method

| - + +     | + + Æ + + |
| --------- | --------- |
| 2         | 3         |
| 2         | Cr O      |
| 16        | H         |
| 3         | C         |
| 4         | Cr        |
| 8         | H O       |
| 2         | 7         |
| 3CO       | 2         |
| - + + + + | + + Æ + + |
| 2         | 2         |
| 3         | 3         |
| Cr O      | 14        |
| H         | 6         |
| Fe        | 6         |
| Fe        | 2         |
| Cr        | 2         |
| 7H O      | 7         |
| 2         | - ¥ ¥     |
| (         | V V       |

COD = c × 8 × 1000 / (O2, mg / cr2 L) × (0 1 V)

式中: V - 滴定空白时消耗硫酸亚铁铵标准溶液体积，mL;

V - 水样体积, mL;

c - 硫酸亚铁铵标准溶液浓度，mol/L;

8 - 氧(1 的摩尔质量，g/mol。

- 用0.25mol/L的重铬酸钾溶液可测定COD大于50 mg/L的水样；
- COD larger than 50 mg/L, use a 0.25 mol/L potassium dichromate solution
- 用0.025mol/L的重铬酸钾溶液可测定COD为5-50 mg/L的水样，但准确度较差
- COD within 5-50 mg/L, use a 0.025 mol/L potassium dichromate solution but with poor accuracy

# 1. 重铬酸钾法测COD

# Potassium dichromate method

- 重铬酸钾氧化性很强，可将大部分有机物氧化，但吡啶不被氧化，芳香族有机化合物不易被氧化；
- 挥发性直链脂肪族化合物、苯等存在于蒸气相，不能与氧化剂液体接触，氧化不明显。

# Comprehensive index and category index

- 氯离子能被重铬酸钾氧化，并与硫酸银作用生成沉淀，可加入适量硫酸汞络合。

# 化学需氧量(coD)

# Chemical oxygen demand

- Potassium dichromate have a strong oxidizing ability and can oxidize most of the organic matters, but pyridine can not be oxidized, and aromatic organic compounds are not easily oxidized too;
- Volatile linear aliphatic compounds, benzene, etc. exist in the vapor phase and cannot contact with the oxidant liquid, so the oxidation is not obvious.

# 硝基苯类

# Nitrobenzene

- Chloride ion can be oxidized by potassium dichromate and reacted with silver sulfate to form precipitate, which can complex with mercury sulfate.

# 2. 库仑滴定 Coulometric titration

- 建立在电解基础上 on the basis of electrolysis
- 在溶液中加入适当物质，一定强度恒定电流电解，在工作电极电解产生一种试剂
- Adding a suitable substance to the solution; electrolyzing at a constant intensity and constant current; generating a reagent at the working electrode
- 试剂与被测物质进行定量反应，终点通过电化学方法指示
- The reagent is quantitatively reacted with the test substance, and the end point can be indicated by the electrochemical method
- 依据电解消耗电荷量和法拉第电解定律计算被测物质含量
- According to the amount of charge consumed by electrolysis and Faraday's law of electrolysis, we can calculate the content of the substance to be tested.

---

# 综合索引和类别索引 Comprehensive index and category index

式中：

- W——电极反应物的质量 (Mass of electrode reactant)
- I——电解电流 (Electrolytic current)；
- t——电解时间 (Electrolysis time)；
- 96500——法拉第常数 (Faraday constant)；
- M——电极反应物的摩尔质量 (Molar mass of electrode reactant)；
- n——每摩尔电极反应物的电子转移数 (Number of electron transfer per mole of electrode reactant)。

# 2.8 有机污染物的测定

# （二）高锰酸盐指数(IMn) Permanganate index

- 以高锰酸钾溶液为氧化剂测得的化学需氧量，称高锰酸盐指数，以氧的质量浓度（mg/L）表示。
- The chemical oxygen demand measured by using potassium permanganate (KMnO₄) solution as the oxidant is called permanganate index and expressed by the mass concentration of oxygen (mg/L).
- 该指数常被作为反映地表水受有机物和还原性无机物污染程度的综合指标。
- This index is often used as a comprehensive indicator for the level of organic matters and reducing inorganic matters in surface water.

# 总有机碳（TOC）Total organic carbon

# 石油类Petroleum

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 13】 -->

# （二）高锰酸盐指数(IMn) Permanganate index

# Determination of organic pollutants

按测定溶液的介质不同分类（碱性条件下高锰酸钾氧化能力较弱）Classified according to the medium of the determination solution (The oxidizing ability of potassium permanganate is weak under alkaline conditions)

- ✓ 酸性高锰酸钾法 Cl⁻&#x3C;300 mg/L时使用 Acidic potassium permanganate method
- ✓ 碱性高锰酸钾法 Cl⁻>300 mg/L时使用 Alkali potassium permanganate method

# 高锰酸盐指数Permanganate index

化学需氧量（CODCr）和高锰酸盐指数（IMn）是采用不同的氧化剂在各自的氧化条件下测定的，难以找出明显的相关关系。一般来说，重铬酸钾法的氧化率可达90%，而高锰酸钾法的氧化率为50%左右，两者均未完全氧化，因而都只是一个相对参考数据

# 挥发酚Volatile phenol

Chemical oxygen demand (CODCr) and permanganate index were determined using different oxidants under their respective oxidative conditions. The oxidation rate of the potassium dichromate method and potassium permanganate method can reach 90% and 50% respectively.

# 2.8 有机污染物的测定

# （三）生化需氧量(BOD)

# Biochemical Oxygen Demand

# 综合指标和类别指标

• 生化需氧量是指在有溶解氧的条件下，好氧微生物在分解水中有机物的生物化学氧化过程中所消耗的溶解氧量。

• Biochemical oxygen demand refers to the amount of dissolved oxygen consumed by aerobic microorganisms in the biochemical oxidation process for decomposing organic matters in water.

• 有机物在微生物作用下，好氧分解分两个阶段：含碳有机物氧化阶段，

• 硝化阶段 挥发酚Volatile phenol

The aerobic decomposition of organic matters can be divided into two stages: the carbon-containing organic matters oxidation stage and the nitrification stage.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 15】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 15】

# （三）生化需氧量(BOD)

# Biochemical Oxygen Demand

BOD是反映水体被有机物污染程度的综合指标，也是研究废水的可生化降解性和生化处理效果，以及生化处理废水工艺设计和动力学研究中的重要参数。

Comprehensive index and category index BOD is a comprehensive indicator reflecting the degree of water contamination by organic matters.

An important parameter for studying the biodegradability and effect of biochemical treatment, the process design and kinetics of biochemical wastewater treatment.

# 测定方法 Method

- 五天培养法（最经典） 5-day incubation method
- 微生物电极法 Microbial electrode method
- 其他方法 Other methods

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 16】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 16】

# Biochemical Oxygen Demand (BOD)

微生物用于分解水中有机物的氧气量

Amount of oxygen used by microorganisms to decompose organic matters in the water

理论BOD可以通过平衡化学方程来确定，其中所有有机物质都转化为CO₂

Theoretical BOD can be determined by balancing a chemical equation in which all organic matter is converted to CO₂

# Example

计算1.67 x 10⁻³mol葡萄糖（C₆H₁₂O₆）的理论需氧量：

Calculate the theoretical oxygen demand of 1.67 x 10⁻³ moles of glucose (C₆H₁₂O₆):

C₆H₁₂O₆ + O₂ 🡪 CO₂ + H₂O

C₆H₁₂O₆ + 6 O₂ 🡪 6 CO₂ + 6 H₂O

1.67x 10⁻³mol glucose/L ⨯ 6 mol O₂/ mol glucose ⨯ 32 g O₂/mol O₂ = 0.321 g O₂/L = 321 mg O₂/L

# Biochemical Oxygen Demand (BOD)

废水和地表水应用最广泛的有机污染参数是（BOD₅）。

The most widely used parameter of organic pollution applied to both wastewater and surface water is the 5-day BOD (BOD₅).

涉及微生物在有机物生化氧化中使用的溶解氧的测量

Involves the measurement of the dissolved oxygen used by microorganisms in the biochemical oxidation of organic matter.

| Under: | Specified Time        | 5 days                   |
| ------ | --------------------- | ------------------------ |
|        | Specified Temperature | 20 °C                    |
|        | Specified Conditions  | In the Dark              |
|        |                       | the Presence of Bacteria |

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 18】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 18】

# 1. 五天培养法 (BOD₅法：HJ 505-2009)

# 5-day incubation method

❑定义：也称标准稀释法。其测定原理是水样经稀释后，在20±1℃条件下培养5天，求出培养前后水样中溶解氧含量，二者的差值为BOD₅。如果水样五日生化需氧量超过6 mg/L，则需进行稀释，反之则可直接测定(如较清洁的河水)。

❑Standard dilution method. The water samples are incubated at 20±1°C for 5 days after dilution. Then determine the DO content in the water sample before and after the culture process. The difference between the two value.

❑If the water sample has a BOD₅ value >6 mg/L, it needs to be diluted, otherwise it can be directly measured (such as cleaner river water).

❑对于不含或少含微生物的工业废水，如酸性废水、碱性废水、高温废水或经过氯化处理的废水，在测定BOD₅时应进行接种，以引入能降解废水中有机物的微生物。

❑For industrial wastewater containing no or very little microorganisms, such as acidic wastewater, alkaline wastewater, high-temperature wastewater or chlorinated wastewater, microorganisms should be introduced to degrade organic matters in the wastewater.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 19】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 19】

# 1. 五天培养法 (BOD₅法：HJ 505-2009) 5-day incubation method

- 当废水中存在着难被一般生活污水中的微生物以正常速度降解的有机物或有剧毒物质时，应将驯化后的微生物引入水样中进行接种；

When the organic substances or highly toxic substances are difficult to be degraded by the microorganisms at a normal speed, the acclimation is needed for microorganisms for inoculation;
- 若水样中含有硝化细菌，应加入适量丙烯基硫脲溶液，抑制硝化反应；

If the water sample contains nitrifying bacteria, an appropriate amount of propylene thiourea solution should be added to inhibit the nitrification reaction;
- 污染的地表水和多数工业废水，含较多有机物，需稀释后再测定，以保证培养过程中有充足的溶解氧；

If the content of organic matters is too high, the water should be diluted before measurement to ensure sufficient dissolved oxygen during the cultivation process;
- 水样稀释程度应使五日培养中所消耗的溶解氧大于2 mg/L, 剩余溶解氧在2 mg/L以上

The dilution of water sample should make the DO consumed in the five-day cultivation >2 mg/L, and the remaining DO >2 mg/L.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 20】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 20】

# 稀释水 Dilution water

- 稀释水一般用蒸馏水配制 usually prepared by distilled water
- 1) 干净无有机物 Clean and free of organic matters
- 2) (20±1)℃ 曝气 2-8h ， 使水中溶解氧 (DO) 接近饱和 。 (20±1) ℃ aeration for 2-8h, to make DO close to saturation.
- 3) 临用前加入少量氯化钙、氯化铁、硫酸镁等营养盐溶液及磷酸盐缓冲溶液 ， 混匀备用 。 Add a small amount of mixture from nutrient salt solution such as CaCl₂, FeCl₃, MgSO₄, or phosphate buffer solution.
- 4) 稀释水的最佳pH值为7.2，BOD₅应小于0.2mg/L。The optimum pH of the dilution water is 7.2, and the BOD₅ should be less than 0.2 mg/L.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 21】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 21】

# 稀释水 Dilution water

- 如水样中无微生物，则应在稀释水中加入接种液. If there is no microorganism in the water sample, the inoculum solution should be added to the dilution water. 即在每升稀释水中加入生活污水上层清液1—10mL，或表层土壤浸出液20—30mL，或河水、湖水10—100mL。这种水称为接种稀释水。Adding 1-10 mL of the supernatant of the domestic sewage, or 20-30 mL of the surface soil leachate, or 10-100 mL of the river water and the lake water into per liter of diluted water..
- 为检查稀释水和接种液的质量，以及化验人员的操作水平，将每升含葡萄糖和谷氨酸各150mg的标准溶液以1∶50稀释比稀释后，与水样同步测定BOD₅，测得值应在180-230mg/L之间，否则，应检查原因，予以纠正。To check the quality of the dilution water, the inoculum, and the operation level of the laboratory personnel, the standard solution containing 150 mg of glucose and glutamic acid per liter is diluted at a dilution ratio of 1:50, and then measure the BOD₅ value together with the water sample. The value should be between 180-230mg/L. Otherwise, the cause should be checked and corrected.

# Biochemical Oxygen Demand (BOD)

# Nitrification in the BOD Test

与氨氧化成硝酸盐相关的需氧量称为含氮生化需氧量（NBOD）

The oxygen demand associated with the oxidation of ammonia to nitrate is called the nitrogenous biochemical oxygen demand (NBOD)

# Conversion of ammonia to nitrite:

NH₃ + 3/2O₂ → HNO₂ + H₂O

# Conversion of nitrite to nitrate:

HNO2 + 1/2O₂ → HNO₃

# Overall conversion of ammonia to nitrate:

NH3 + 2O₂ → HNO₃ + H₂O

# 碳质BOD

碳质生化需氧量（CBOD）是氧化样品中可氧化碳的需氧量的量度

Carbonaceous biochemical oxygen demand (CBOD) is a measure of the oxygen demand exerted by the oxidizable carbon in the sample.

# 有机物好氧分解的两个阶段

# 第一阶段  2.8有机污染物的测定

# ----含碳物质氧化阶段

主要是含碳有机物氧化为二氧化碳和水。

The carbonaceous organic matter oxidized to carbon dioxide and water

# 第二阶段----硝化阶段

# 化学需氧量(coD)

# Stage 2---the nitrification stage

主要是含氮有机化合物在硝化菌的作用下分解为亚硝酸盐和硝酸盐。对生活污水及性质与其接近的工业废水，硝化阶段大约在5-7日以后才显著进行，故常用的20℃五天培养法(BOD₅法)测定BOD值一般不包括硝化阶段的。

The nitrogen-containing organic compounds are decomposed into nitrites and nitrates by the action of nitrifying bacteria. For industrial wastewater and domestic sewage which has similar properties with it, the nitrification stage is only significant after 5-7 days. Therefore, the commonly used 20°C 5-day incubation method (BOD₅ method) generally does not include the nitrification stage.

# （四）总有机碳（TOC）

# Total organic carbon

• 有机碳是以碳的含量表示水体中有机物质总量的综合指标

Organic carbon is a comprehensive indicator of the total amount of organic matters in water expressed by the content of carbon.

由于TOC 的测定采用燃烧法，因此能将有机物全部氧化，它比BOD₅或COD更能反映有机物的总量。

Since the TOC is measured by a combustion method, the organic matter can be completely oxidized, and it reflects the total amount of organic matter more than BOD₅ or COD.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 39】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 39】

# （四）总有机碳（TOC）Total organic carbon

- 目前广泛应用的测定TOC的方法是燃烧氧化-非色散红外吸收法。
- The currently widely used method for determining TOC is combustion oxidation-non-dispersive infrared absorption method.

其测定原理是：将一定量水样注入高温炉内的石英管，在900-950℃温度下，以铂和三氧化钴或三氧化二铬为催化剂，使有机物燃烧裂解转化为二氧化碳，然后用红外线气体分析仪测定CO2含量，从而确定水样中碳的含量。因为在高温下，水样中的碳酸盐也分解产生二氧化碳，故上面测得的为水样中的总碳(TC)。

A certain amount of water sample is injected into a quartz tube of the high-temperature furnace. Platinum, cobalt trioxide or chromium trioxide is used as a catalyst to convert the organic matters into carbon dioxide by combustion at the temperature of 900-950℃. Then use the infrared gas analyzer measures the CO₂ content to determine the carbon content of the water sample. Since the carbonate in the water sample can also decomposes to produce carbon dioxide at high temperatures, the total carbon (TC) in the water sample can be measured by the method introduced above.

---

# 有机碳的测定 Determination of organic carbon

# 2.8 有机污染物的测定

使用高温炉和低温炉皆有的TOC测定仪。

将同一等量水样分别注入高温炉(900℃)和低温炉(150℃)，水样中有机碳和无机碳均转化为CO₂，低温炉石英管中装有磷酸浸渍的玻璃棉，使无机碳酸盐在150℃分解为CO₂，有机物却不能被分解氧化。

将高、低温炉中生成的CO₂依次导入非色散红外气体分析仪，分别测得总碳(TC)和无机碳(IC)，二者之差即为总有机碳(TOC)。该方法检出限0.1mg/L, 测定下限为0.5mg/L。

TOC meter with both high temperature furnaces and low temperature furnaces. When the same amount of water is injected into high temperature furnace (900℃) and low temperature furnace (150 C), the organic carbon and inorganic carbon are converted into CO₂, and the quartz tube of the low temperature furnace is filled with phosphoric acid impregnated glass cotton, which can decompose inorganic carbonates into CO₂ at 150°C, but organic matter cannot be. The CO₂ generated in high and low temperature furnaces is sequentially introduced into a non-dispersive infrared gas analyzer, and the total carbon (TC) and inorganic carbon (IC) are respectively measured, and the difference between the two is the total organic carbon (TOC). The detection limit of this method is 0.1 mg/L, and the lower limit of measurement is 0.5 mg/L.

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 42】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 42】

# BOD，COD和TOC之间的相互关系

# Interrelationships between BOD, COD, and TOC

- 未经处理的城市废水的BOD / COD比率的典型值在0.3-0.8的范围内。Typical values for the ratio of BOD/COD for untreated municipal wastewater are in the range of 0.3-0.8.
- 如果未经处理废水的BOD / COD比率为0.5或更高，则认为废物易于通过生物方法处理。If the BOD/COD ratio for untreated wastewater is 0.5 or greater, the waste is considered to be easily treatable by biological means.
- 如果该比率低于约0.3，则废物可能具有一些有毒成分或者其稳定化可能某些经过驯化的微生物。If the ratio is below about 0.3, either the waste may have some toxic components or acclimated microorganisms may be required in its stabilization.
- 未经处理废水的相应BOD / TOC比率在1.2至2.0之间变化 The corresponding BOD/TOC ratio for untreated wastewater varies from 1.2 to 2.0.
- 在使用这些比例时，重要的是要记住它们会随着废物的处理程度而发生显着变化。When using the ratios it is important to remember that they will change significantly with the degree of treatment the waste has undergone.

| Type of wastewater     | BOD/COD  | BOD/TOC  |
| ---------------------- | -------- | -------- |
| Untreated              | 0.3-0.8  | 1.2-2.0  |
| After primary settling | 0.4-0.6  | 0.8-1.2  |
| Final effluent         | 0.1-0.3a | 0.2-0.5b |

aCBOD/COD.

bCBOD/TOC.

# （五）挥发酚 Volatile phenol

# Determination of organic pollutants

• 根据酚类物质能否与水蒸气一起蒸出，分为挥发酚与不挥发酚。通常认为沸点在230℃以下的为挥发酚（属一元酚），而沸点在230℃以上的为不挥发酚。

• Volatile phenol and non-volatile phenol: whether the phenolic substance can be distilled out together with water vapor.

• It is generally considered that the boiling point of volatile phenol is 230 ℃ or less (which is a monohydric phenol), and the boiling point of nonvolatile phenol is higher than 230 ℃.

# 酚的主要分析方法 Main analytical methods for phenol

- ✓ 4-氨基安替吡林分光光度法(国内外普遍采用）4-aminoantipyrine spectrophotometric method (widely used at home and abroad)
- ✓ 溴化滴定法(用于高浓度含酚废水）Bromination titration (for high concentration wastewater containing phenols)
- ✓ 色谱法 Chromatography

# （六）硝基苯类 Nitrobenzene

# Determination of organic pollutants

- 常见的硝基苯类化合物有硝基苯、二硝基苯、二硝基甲苯、三硝基甲苯、二硝基氯苯等。它们难溶于水。
- The commonest nitrobenzene compounds: nitrobenzene, dinitrobenzene, dinitrotoluene, trinitrotoluene, dinitrochlorobenzene and so on. They are non-soluble in water.
- 废水中一硝基和二硝基苯类化合物常采用还原-偶氮分光光度法。
- The mono-nitro and dinitrobenzene compounds in the wastewater are often measured by reduction-azo spectrophotometry.
- 三硝基苯类化合物采用氯代十六烷基吡啶分光光度法。
- The trinitrobenzene compound is measured by cetylpyridinium chloride spectrophotometric method.

# 矿物油类 Mineral oils

各种烃类的混合物，主要来自原有开采、加工、运输及炼制使用等行业排放的废水。

The mixture of various hydrocarbons, and It mainly comes from wastewater discharged from industries such as mining, processing, transportation and refining.

# 动植物油类 Plant and animal oils

主要来自动、植物及海洋生物加工等行业的废水和生活污水，其主要成分为各种三酰甘油和低级脂肪酸酯等。

It mainly comes from plant and animal and wastewater or domestic sewage of marine bioprocessing industries, and its main components are various triacylglycerols and lower fatty acid esters.

# 2.8 有机污染物的测定

# （七）油类 Oils

# 水中油类危害 Determination of organic pollutants

石油类化合物漂浮在水体表面，影响空气与水体界面间的氧交换；分散于水中的油可被微生物氧化分解，消耗水中的溶解氧，使水质恶化。矿物油中芳烃类化合物虽较烷烃类少，但毒性极大。

Petroleum compounds float on the surface of water, affecting the exchange of oxygen between the air and the interface of the water; oil dispersed in the water can be oxidatively decomposed by microorganisms, consuming dissolved oxygen in the water, and deteriorating the water quality. Although the aromatic hydrocarbons in mineral oil are less than alkanes, they are extremely toxic.

# 测定水中油类物质的方法 Methods for determining oils in water

- 重量法 Gravimetric method
- 红外分光光度法 Infrared spectrophotometry
- 非色散红外吸收法 Non-dispersive infrared absorption method

# 二、特定有机污染物 Specific organic pollutants

- 特定有机污染物指那些毒性大、积累性强、难降解、被列为优先污染物的有机化合物，其品种多、含量低。
- Organic compounds that are highly toxic, highly cumulative, difficult to degrade, and classified as priority pollutants.
- High varieties and low contents.

# (一)挥发性卤代烃 Volatile halogenated hydrocarbons

- 挥发性卤代烃主要指三卤甲烷(三氯甲烷、一溴二氯甲烷、三溴甲烷)及四氯化碳等。各种卤代烃均有特殊的气味和毒性，可通过皮肤接触、呼吸或饮水进入人体。
- 总有机碳（TOC）Total organic carbon
- Volatile halogenated hydrocarbons mainly refer to trihalomethanes (trichloromethane, monobromodichloromethane, tribromomethane) and carbon tetrachloride.
- All halogenated hydrocarbons have special odor and toxicity and can enter the body through skin, breathing or drinking.

---

• 挥发性卤代烃广泛应用于化工、医药及实验室，其废(污)水排入环境而污染水体；生活饮用水氯化消毒过程也产生三氯甲烷。

• Volatile halogenated hydrocarbons are widely used in chemical, pharmaceutical and laboratories, where waste (sewage) water is discharged into the environment and then pollutes water bodies.

• The chlorination of domestic drinking water can also produce chloroform.

# 2.8 有机污染物的测定

# 气相色谱法原理和仪器

# The principle and instrument of Gas chromatography

# 综合指标和类别指标

色谱法：利用混合物不同组分在固定相和流动相中分配系数（或吸附系数、渗透性等）的差异，使不同组分在做相对运动的两相中进行反复分配，从而实现分离的分析方法。

| 化学需氧量(coD) | Chemical oxygen demand |
| ---------- | ---------------------- |
| 高锰酸盐指数     | Permanganate index     |
| 硝基苯类       | Nitrobenzene           |
| 石油类        | Petroleum              |

# Chromatography

A separation analysis method is achieved by utilizing the differences in distribution coefficients (or adsorption coefficients, permeability, etc.) of different components in the fixed and mobile phases of a mixture, to repeatedly distribute different components in the relative motion of the two phases.

# 火焰离子化检测器(FID) Flame ionization detector (FID)

- 破坏性质量型通用检测器，灵敏度高，线性范围宽，广泛应用于有机物的常量和微量检测。
- A quality universal detector with high sensitivity and wide linear range, widely used for constant and trace detection of organic compounds. Destructive
- 主要原理为：氢气和空气燃烧生成火焰，当有机化合物进入火焰时，由于离子化反应，生成比基流高几个数量级的离子，在电场作用下，带正电的离子和电子分别向负极和正极移动，形成离子流，此离子流经放大器放大后，可被检测。
- Main principle: Hydrogen and air burn to generate a flame. When organic compounds enter the flame, ionization reactions generate ions several orders of magnitude higher than the base current. Under the action of an electric field, positively charged ions and electrons move towards the negative and positive electrodes respectively, forming an ion flow. This ion can be detected after being amplified by an amplifier.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 55】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 55】

# 火焰离子化检测器(FID) Flame ionization detector (FID)

# Determination of organic pollutants

- 火焰离子化检测器只能分析有机物(含碳化合物)，不适于分析惰性气体、空气、水、CO、CO₂、CS₂、NO、SO₂及H₂S等。Only for organic compounds (carbon-containing compounds), not suitable for inert gases, air, water, CO, CO₂, CS₂, NO, SO₂, H₂S.

# 高锰酸盐指数Permanganate index

- 特别适合于有机化合物的常量到微量分析，是目前环保领域中，空气和水中有机化合物检测的好手段。Especially suitable for constant to trace analysis of organic compounds. A good method for detecting organic compounds in air and water.

# 2.8 有机污染物的测定

- 电子捕获检测器(ECD) Electronic capture detector (ECD)
- 高选择性检测器，在分析痕量电负性有机化合物上有很好的应用。仅对那些能俘获电子的化合物，如卤代烃、含N、O和S等杂原子的化合物有响应。
- High selectivity, good application in analyzing trace electronegative organic compounds. Only selective for compounds that can capture electrons, such as halogenated hydrocarbons and compounds containing heteroatoms such as N, O, and S.
- 灵敏度高、选择性好，多年来已广泛用于环境样品中痕量农药、多氯联苯等的分析。
- High sensitivity and good selectivity. Widely used for the analysis of trace pesticides, polychlorinated biphenyls, etc.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 57】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 57】

# 电子捕获检测器(ECD) Electronic capture detector (ECD)

- 主要原理为检测室内的放射源放出β-射线(初级电子)，与通过检测室的载气碰撞产生次级电子和正离子，在电场作用下，分别向与自己极性相反的电极运动，形成基流，当具有负电性的组分(即能捕获电子的组分)进入检测室后，捕获了检测室内的电子，变成带负电荷的离子，由于电子被组分捕获，使得检测室基流减少，产生色谱峰信号。
- Main principle: The β- radiation released from sources in detection chamber, collides with the carrier gas passing through the detection chamber to generate secondary electrons and positive ions; Under the action of an electric field, they move towards electrodes with opposite polarity to each other, forming a base current. When a negatively charged enters the detection chamber, it captures the electrons in the detection chamber and becomes negatively charged ions. Due to the capture of electrons by the component, the base current in the detection chamber is reduced, resulting in a chromatographic peak signal.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 58】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 58】

# 热导检测器(TCD) Thermal conductivity detector (TCD)

• 通用的非破坏性浓度型检测器，理论上可应用于任何组分的检测，但因其灵敏度较低，故一般用于常量分析。A non-destructive concentration detector that can theoretically be applied to the detection of any component. But due to its low sensitivity, it is generally used for constant analysis.

• 基于不同组分与载气有不同的热导率的原理而工作。热导检测器的热敏元件为热丝，如镀金钨丝、铂金丝等。当被测组分与载气一起进入热导池时，由于混合气的热导率与纯载气不同(通常是低于载气的热导率)，热丝传向池壁的热量也发生变化，致使热丝温度发生改变，其电阻也随之改变，进而使电桥输出端产生不平衡电位而作作为信号输出，记录该信号从而得到色谱峰。

• Based on the principle that different components and carrier gases have different thermal conductivity. The thermal sensitive element of the thermal conductivity detector is a hot wire, such as gold-plated tungsten wire, platinum wire, etc. When the tested component and the carrier gas enter the thermal conductivity cell together, the difference in thermal conductivity between the mixed gas and the pure carrier makes the heat from the hot wire to the cell wall change, causing a change in the temperature of the hot wire and its resistance, which in turn generates an unbalanced potential at the output end of the bridge as a signal output. This signal is recorded to obtain the chromatographic peak.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 59】 -->

stable, large linear range, high quantitative accuracy, simple operation and maintenance, low cost, easy to promote and popularize, suitable for constant and semi trace analysis.

# 火焰光度检测器(FPD) Flame photometric detector (FPD)

• 质量型选择性检测器，主要用于测定含硫、磷化合物。

Quality selective detector, mainly used for the determination of sulfur and phosphorus compounds.

• 使用中通入的氢气量必须多于通常燃烧所需要的氢气量，即在富氢情况下燃烧得到火焰。广泛应用于石油产品中微量硫化合物及农药中有机磷化合物的分析。

The amount of hydrogen introduced must be greater than the usual amount required for combustion, that is, to obtain a flame when burned in a hydrogen rich environment. Widely used for the analysis of trace sulfur compounds in petroleum products and organic phosphorus compounds in pesticides.

# 石油类Petroleum

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 61】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 61】

# 火焰光度检测器(FPD) Flame photometric detector (FPD)

• 主要原理为组分在富氢火焰中燃烧时组分不同程度地变为碎片或分子，其外层电子由于互相碰撞而被激发，当电子由激发态返回低能态或基态时，发射出特征波长的光谱，这种特征光谱通过经选择滤光片后被测量。如硫在火焰中产生350-430nm的光谱，磷产生480-600nm的光谱，其中394nm和526nm分别为含硫和含磷化合物的特征波长。

• Main principle: When a component burns in a hydrogen rich flame, it becomes fragments or molecules, and its outer electrons are excited. When the electrons return from the excited state to the low-energy or ground state, they emit a spectrum of characteristic wavelengths. Sulfur produces a spectrum of 350-430nm in flames, while phosphorus produces a spectrum of 480-600nm, with 394nm and 526nm being the characteristic wavelengths of sulfur containing and phosphorus containing compounds, respectively.

• FPD是一种高灵敏度、高选择性的检测器，对含P和S特别敏感，主要用于含P和S的有机化合物和气体硫化物中P和S的微量和痕量分析，如有机磷农药、水质污染中的硫醇、天然气中含硫化物的气体等。Highly sensitive and selective, particularly to P and S, mainly used for trace analysis of P and S in organic compounds and gas sulfides.

Principle: pre-treating the water sample by headspace method (see section 4 of this chapter), and taking an appropriate amount of gas sample in the liquid space, then the sample is measured by the gas chromatograph with an electron capture detector, and then quantified by the standard curve method. # 吹扫捕集-气相色谱 Purge and trap gas chromatography

色谱柱:长60m,内径0.25mm毛细管柱,内壁涂渍厚1.0μm TC−Aquatic。

柱温:40℃(1min) 4℃/min→100℃ (6 min) 10℃/min→200℃(5min)。

温度:进样口180℃;检测器220℃°。

气体流速:载气(高纯氮)1.7mL/min;氢气35mL/min;空气350mL/min。

吹脱捕集条件:吹脱时间8min，捕集温度35℃;脱附温度180℃，脱附时间6min;烘烤温度220℃，烘烤时间25min，吹脱气体为高纯氮，吹脱流速40mL/min。

底质系指江、河、湖、库、海等水体底部表层沉积物质。它是矿物、岩石、土壤的自然侵蚀和废（污）水排出物沉积及生物活动、物质之间物理、化学反应等过程的产物。

The surface sediments at the bottom of water bodies such as rivers, lakes, reservoirs and seas. It is the product of the natural erosion of minerals, rocks, soils and the deposition of waste (sewage) water and biological activities, physical and chemical reactions.

# 2.9 底质监测 Sediment monitoring

# 一、底质监测的意义和目的 Significance and purpose

- 了解水环境污染现状，追溯水环境污染历史，研究污染物的沉积、迁移转化规律和对水生生物，特别是底栖生物的影响；
- Understand the current situation of water pollution, trace the history of water pollution, study the deposition, migration and transformation of pollutants and find out its impact on aquatic organisms, especially on benthic organisms;
- 对评价水体质量、预测水质变化趋势和沉积污染物对水体的潜在危险提供依据。
- Provide a basis for evaluating water quality, predicting the changing trends of water quality, and potential hazards of sedimentary pollutants to water bodies.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 69】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 2 · Lecture 6 · Slide 69】

# 二、样品的采集 Sample collection

- 底质监测断面的位置应与水质监测断面重合，采样点在水质采样点垂线的正下方，可略作移动。
- The monitoring section of sediment should coincide with the section of the water quality monitoring. The sampling point is directly below the vertical line of the water sampling point and can be slightly moved.
- 湖（库）底质采样点一般应设在主要河流与污染源水进入后与湖（库）水混合均匀处，采样点应避开底质沉积不稳定、易受搅动和表层水草茂盛处。
- The sampling point of the lake (reservoirs) should generally be located at the place where the main river and the polluted water are evenly mixed after entering the lake (reservoirs).
- The sampling point should avoid the place unstable and vulnerable to agitation, and the surface water grass can not be too lush.

---

# 四、污染物质的测定

底质中的污染物也分为金属化合物、非金属化合物和有机化合物，其具体测定项目应与相应水质监测项目相对应。

Contaminants in the sediment can be classified into metal compounds, non-metal compounds and organic compounds, and the specific measurement items should correspond to the corresponding water quality monitoring items.

通常测定镉、铅、锌、铜、铬、砷、无机汞、有机汞、硫化物、氰化物、氟化物等金属、非金属无机污染物和酚、多氯联苯、有机氯农药、有机磷农药等有机污染物。

Cadmium, lead, zinc, copper, chromium, arsenic, inorganic mercury, organic mercury, sulfide, cyanide, fluoride and other metals or non-metallic inorganic pollutants and phenol, polychlorinated biphenyls, organochlorine pesticides, organic phosphorus pesticides and other organic pollutants.

# 二、活性污泥性质的测定

# Determination of the properties of activated sludge

- 污泥沉降比 sludge settling velocity (SV)

将混匀的曝气池活性污泥混合液迅速倒进1000 mL量筒中至满刻度，静置30 min，则沉降污泥与所取混合液之体积比为污泥沉降比(%)，又称污泥沉降体积(SV₃₀)，以mL/L表示。

The activated sludge mixture from aeration tank is quickly poured into 1000 mL measuring cylinder until it is full, then the volume ratio of the sludge to the liquid: sludge settling velocity (%) or sludge setting volume (SV₃₀), which is expressed as mL/L.
- 污泥体积指数 Sludge volume index (SVI)

污泥体积指数简称污泥指数(SI)，系指曝气池活性污泥混合液经30 min沉降后, 1g干污泥所占的体积(以mL计)。

The sludge volume index (sludge index for short) refers to the volume (in mL) of 1 g dry sludge after 30 minutes of settling of the activated sludge mixture in the aeration tank.

# 2.8 有机污染物的测定

# 二、活性污泥性质的测定

# Determination of the properties of activated sludge

# Determination of organic pollutants

- 污泥浓度 mixed liquid suspended solids (MLSS)
- 1 L曝气池污泥混合液所含干污泥的重量称为污泥浓度 (g/L, mg/L)
- The weight of dry sludge contained in the 1 L of sludge mixture in aeration tank: sludge concentration.
- 挥发性污泥浓度，也叫悬浮物浓度 (MLVSS)：1L曝气池活性污泥混合液中所含挥发性悬浮物的质量 (mg/L)，能比较确切的反映反应器中微生物的数量
- Volatile sludge concentration, also known as mixed liquor volatile suspended solids (MLVSS): the mass of volatile suspended solids (mg/L) contained in the 1L aeration tank activated sludge mixture, which can accurately reflect the number of microorganisms in the reactor.

# 2. Harm of air pollution

Air pollution: With the rapid development of industry and transportation, especially the massive use of coal and oil, a large amount of harmful substances will be produced (dust, sulfur dioxide, nitrogen oxides, carbon monoxide, hydrocarbons) and discharged into the air.

When the concentration exceeds the limit concentration allowed by the environment and lasts for a certain period of time, it will change the normal composition of the air, destroy the natural, physical, chemical and ecological balance, and thus endanger people’s life, work and health, and cause damage to natural resources and property, utensils, etc.

空气污染：随着工业及交通运输业等的迅速发展，特别是煤和石油的大量使用，将产生大量有害物质如烟尘、二氧化硫、氮氧化物、一氧化碳、碳氢化合物等排放到空气中，当其浓度超过环境所允许的极限浓度并持续一定时间后，就会改变空气的正常组成，破坏自然的物理、化学和生态平衡体系，从而危害人们的生活、工作和健康，损坏自然资源及财产、器物等。

# Pathways of air pollution to the human body:

| 1. | Respiratory inhalation     | 呼吸道吸入    |
| -- | -------------------------- | -------- |
| 2. | Ingest with food and water | 随食物和饮水摄入 |
| 3. | Skin contact               | 体表接触侵入   |

# Harm of air pollution to human health

1. Atmospheric particulate matter
颗粒物的大小决定其沉积于呼吸道中的位置；化学组成决定沉积位置上对组织的影响。

The size of the particles determines the position they are deposited in the respiratory tract; The chemical composition determines the effect on the tissue at the deposition site.
2. Sulfur dioxide
损害肝脏。且由于SO2通常与多种污染物共存，吸入之后产生的复合作用危害更大。

Causes damage to liver. Usually coexists with a variety of contaminants, and the complex will be more harmful.
3. Carbon monoxide
所有大气污染物中散布最广的一种，严重阻碍血液输氧，引起缺氧中毒。

The most widely distributed of all atmospheric pollutants, seriously impeding blood oxygenation, causing hypoxia poisoning.

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 10】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 10】

# Harm of air pollution to human health

# 4. Nitrogen oxides

NO₂对呼吸器官有刺激性，可引起肺水肿、慢性支气管炎等疾病，若与SO₂共存，则危害更重。

NO₂ irritating to the respiratory organs, can cause pulmonary edema, chronic bronchitis and other diseases. Coexistence with SO₂ will be more harmful.

# 5. Photochemical oxidant

臭氧对鼻子、咽喉、肺等呼吸器官有刺激作用，运动时则吸入更严重。

Ozone has a stimulating effect on the respiratory organs such as the nose, throat, and lungs. Inhalation is more serious during exercise.

# Development of Indoor Air Quality

- Initially, because of concerns with outdoor pollution and the understanding that outdoor pollution impacted indoor environments, the indoor pollutants that received the greatest attention were noxious substances that originated outdoors, especially sulfur dioxide, nitrogen oxides, ozone and airborne particles.
- Subsequently attention turned to pollutants that were of particular concern indoors and readily measured; these included formaldehyde, radon, asbestos, tobacco smoke and nonpolar volatile organic.

# 4. Air pollutants and their state

| 形成      | 一次污染物   | Primary pollutants   |
| ------- | ------- | -------------------- |
| 按形成过程分类 | 二次污染物   | Secondary pollutants |
| state   | 分子状态污染物 | Molecular            |
| 按存在状态分类 | 粒子状态污染物 | Particles            |

一次污染物 由污染源直接排入环境的、其物理和化学性状未发生变化的污染物

在物理、化学或生物因素的作用下，或是与环境中其他物质发生反应

二次污染物 物理、化学性状与一次污染物不同的新污染物

继发性污染物

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 19】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 19】

# 4. Air pollutants and their state

# 一次污染物

直接从各种污染源排放到大气中的有害物质，常见的有：

- SO₂, CO, NOₓ (NO, NO₂)
- 颗粒物，其中包括重金属等
- 3,4苯并芘

# Primary pollutants

Harmful substances directly emitted into the atmosphere from various sources of pollution:

- SO₂, CO, NOₓ (NO, NO₂)
- Particulate matter (including metals)
- 3,4-BaP

# 二次污染物

一次污染物之间以及它们与大气正常组分之间的反应产生的新污染物：

- 臭氧、醛类、过氧乙酰硝酸酯（光化学氧化剂）、硫酸雾、硝酸雾等

# Secondary pollutants

Pollutants generated by the reaction of primary pollutants and/or normal atmospheric components:

- O₃, aldehyde, peroxyacetyl nitrate, sulfuric/nitric acid mist

多为气溶胶

Aerosol:

small particles, more toxic than the primary pollutants

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 20】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 20】

# 四、空气中的污染物及其存在状态

# 4. Air pollutants and their state

- # （一）分子状态污染物

沸点低，气体分子形式存在，并以分子状态进入大气，或者常温下液体，但挥发性强，受热时易以蒸汽进入大气中，如SO₂，CO， NOx， HCN, 苯，汞，NH₃，H₂S， Pb，Cd，氟化物等。
- # Molecular pollutants

Low boiling point, in the form of gas molecules, and enter the atmosphere as a molecular state, Liquid at room temperature, but highly volatile, easy to steam into the atmosphere when heated, such as SO₂, CO, NOₓ, HCN, benzene, mercury, NH₃, H₂S, Pb, Cd, fluoride, etc.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 21】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 21】

# 四、空气中的污染物及其存在状态

# 4. Air pollutants and their state

- （二）粒子状态污染物
- ❖  分散在大气中的微小液体和固体颗粒。粒径大小在0.01-100μm之间，复杂的非均相体系。
- ❖  粒径大于100μm的颗粒物能较快地沉降到地面上，称为降尘。
- ❖  粒径小于100μm的颗粒物称为总悬浮颗粒物，是空气常规监测项目，可长期飘浮在空气中，称为可吸入颗粒物或飘尘（IP），又名气溶胶（雾、烟、尘）。
- ❖  颗粒物(PM₁₀)：粒径小于10μm的颗粒物，可通过呼吸进入肺部，在肺泡累积，并通过血液循环输往全身，可吸入颗粒物。
- ❖  颗粒物(PM2.5)：粒径小于2.5μm的颗粒物，粒径更小，活性更强，更易附带有毒有害物质，且在大气中的停留时间长、输送距离远，因而对人体健康和大气环境质量的影响更大。

- Particle state pollutants
- ❖  Dispersed in the atmosphere as tiny liquids and solid particles. particle size (0.01-100μm), complex heterogeneous systems.
- ❖  Particle size >100μm, the particles can settle to the ground, dust.
- ❖  Particle size &#x3C;100μm, total suspended particulate matter a routine air monitoring item.
- ❖  Particulate matter (PM₁₀): &#x3C; 10μm, enter the lungs through breathing, accumulates in the alveoli, and is transported to the body through the blood circulation. Inhalable particles.
- ❖  Particulate matter (PM2.5): &#x3C; 2.5μm.

# 五、空气中污染物的时空分布特点

# 5. Temporal and spatial distribution of pollutants in the air

与其他环境要素中的污染物质相比较，空气中的污染物质具有随时间、空间变化大的特点。

空气污染物的时空分布及其浓度与污染物排放源的分布、排放量及地形、地貌、气象等条件密切相关。

Compared with the pollutants in other environmental mediums, pollutants in the air have a large variation with time and space.

The temporal and spatial distribution of air pollutants and their concentrations are closely related to the distribution of pollutant sources, emissions and topography, geomorphology and meteorology.

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 26】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 26】

# 六、空气中污染物浓度表示方法

# 6. Method for expressing pollutant concentration in the air

# （一）质量浓度

单位体积空气中所含污染物的质量，常用mg/m³或μg/m³表示。对任何状态的污染物都适用。

# （二）体积分数

指单位体积空气中含污染气体或蒸气的体积，常用mL/m³和μL/m³表示。仅适用于气态或蒸气态物质，不受空气温度和压力变化的影响。

两种浓度表示方法之间的换算：

(1) Mass concentration

The mass of pollutants contained in air per unit volume, unit: mg / m³ or μg/m³. Applicable to contaminants in any state.

(2) Volume fraction

The volume of contaminated gas or vapor per unit volume of air, unit: mL/m³ or μL/m³. Only applicable only to gaseous or vaporous substances, not affected by changes in air temperature and pressure. = 22.4 ∙

The relation of the two concentrations:

# 第2节 空气污染监测方案的制订

# 3.2 Development of an air pollution monitoring programme

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 28】 -->

# 监测目的 Purpose

- 调查研究 Investigation
- 资料收集 Data collection
- 综合分析 Comprehensive analysis
- 确定监测项目 Identify monitoring items
- 布点 Sampling sites
- 选择采用频率、方法和监测方法 Choose frequency, method and monitoring method
- 建立质量保证程序和措施 Establish quality assurance procedures and measures
- 提出进度、安排计划、监测结果报告要求等 Plans, schedule, monitoring report requirements

> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 29】

# 一、监测目的 Monitoring purpose

- 通过对环境空气中主要污染物质进行定期或连续地监测，判断空气质量是否符合《环境空气质量标准》或环境规划目标的要求，为空气质量状况评价提供依据。
- For air quality assessment.
- 为研究空气质量的变化规律和发展趋势、开展空气污染的预测预报以及研究污染物迁移转化情况提供基础资料。
- Basic data for research on the changes and development of air quality forecasting air pollution, and the fate of pollutants.
- 为政府环保部门执行环境保护法规、开展空气质量管理及修订空气质量标准提供依据和基础资料。
- Basic information for the government to implement and improve the environmental protection regulations.

# 二、调研及资料收集

# （一）污染源分布及排放情况

污染源类型、数量、位置、排布，原料、燃料及消耗量，烟囱高低，二次污染物高浓度处离污染源位置

Type, quantity, location, distribution, raw materials, fuel and the consumption, the height of the chimney, and distance between the source and the point of secondary pollutants with high concentration

# （二）气象资料

污染物在空气中的扩散、迁移和一系列的物理、化学变化取决于气象条件，风向、风速、气温、气压、降水、日照、相对湿度、温度垂直梯度、逆温层底部高度等。

The diffusion, transformation and a series of physical and chemical changes of pollutants in the air depend on meteorological conditions, including wind direction, wind speed, temperature, pressure, precipitation, sunshine, relative humidity, vertical gradient of temperature, and height of the bottom of the inversion layer.

# （三）地形资料

# （四）土地利用和功能分区情况

# （五）人口分布及人群健康情况

# 四、监测站(点)的布设 Monitoring site

# （一）布设采样站(点)的原则和要求Principles and requirements

周围50m范围内不应有污染源、不能有阻碍环境空气流通的高大建筑物、树木等，采样口周围水平面保证270度以上的采集空间，周围环境情况相对稳定，无强大电磁干扰，有合适的车辆通道

No pollution sources within 50m, no tall buildings or trees which can hinder the air circulation, and horizontal plane around the sampling port is guaranteed as > 270q, the environment is stable, no strong electromagnetic interference, a suitable vehicle channel

# （二）采样站(点)数目的确定 Number

| 市区人口/万人   | SO₂、NOₓ、TSP | 灰尘自然沉降量 | 硫酸盐化速率  |
| --------- | ----------- | ------- | ------- |
| <50       | 3           | ≥3      | ≥6      |
| 50～100    | 4           | 4 ～ 8   | 6 ～ 12  |
| 100 ～ 200 | 5           | 8 ～ 11  | 12 ～ 18 |
| 200 ～ 400 | 6           | 12 ～ 20 | 18 ～ 30 |
| >400      | 7           | 20 ～ 30 | 30 ～ 40 |

# (三)采样站(点)布设方法 Method

1. 功能区布点法 (Function division)：多用于区域性常规监测，划分为工业区、商业区、居民区、工业居民混合区、交通稠密区、清洁区等，在各功能区设置一定数量的采样点，不要求平均分布。

regional routine monitoring, divided into industrial areas, commercial areas, residential areas, industrial-residential mixed areas, intensive transportation areas, clean areas, etc. A certain number of sampling points are set in each function area, not needing equally distributed.
2. 网格布点法 Grid method
3. 同心圆布点法 Concentric circle method
4. 扇形布点法 Sector method

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 36】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 36】

# 网格布点法

# Grid method

- 有多个污染源，且污染源分布均匀的地区，反应污染物空间分布.
- Multiple pollution sources, equally distributed,
- 主导风向明显，下风向设采样点多，占总数的60%
- Dominant wind direction, 60% of sampling points locate at downwind direction
- 网格大小根据污染源强度、人口分布、人力物力条件等确定
- Grid size is determined by intensity of pollution source, population distribution, human and material conditions

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 37】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 37】

# 同心圆布点法

# Concentric circle method

- 多个污染源构成污染群,且大污染源较集中的地区Groups of multiple pollution sources, areas with large pollution sources
- 找出污染群中心,交点作为采样点 The center of the group, intersection point as sampling point
- 不同圆周上采样点数目不一定相等different number of sampling points on different circles
- 常年主导风向的下风向多设一些采样点more sampling points at the downwind direction

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 38】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 38】

# 扇形布点法

# Sector method

- 孤立的高架点源,主导风向明显的地区 Isolated elevated point source, area with dominant wind direction
- 以点源作为顶点,主导风向作为轴线 Point source as peak, dominant wind direction as the axis
- 角度一般为45度,可大些,但不能超过90度 The angle is generally as 45q, cannot exceed 90q.
- 每条弧线3-4个采样点 3-4 sample point on each arc
- 上风向设对照点 Control point at upwind direction

# 五、采样频率和采样时间

# Frequency and time

- 采样频率系指在一个时段内的采样次数。The sampling frequency refers to the number of samples in a period of time.
- 采样时间指每次采样从开始到结束所经历的时间。The sampling time refers to the time from the beginning to the end of each sampling.
- 二者要根据监测目的、污染物分布特征、分析方法灵敏度等因素确定。Determined according to the purpose of monitoring, the distribution of pollutants, and the sensitivity of analytical methods.
- 长期变化趋势:连续或间歇自动采样; Long-term trend: continuous or intermittently automatic sampling;
- 突发性事故:采样时间尽量短 Emergency accident: shorten the sampling time.

# 六、采样方法、监测方法和质量保证

# Methods for sampling and analysis, quality control

- 根据污染物的存在状态、浓度、理化性质及监测方法选择采样方法和仪器。According to the state, concentration, physicochemical properties and analytical methods of the pollutants.
- 尽可能选择国家标准方法——《空气和废气监测分析方法》（第四版）Choose the national standard method——《Analytical Methods for Air and Fuel Gas Monitoring 》(4th edition)
- 空气污染物测定:分光光度法和气相色谱法 Spectrophotometry and gas chromatography

# 一、直接采样法 direct sampling method

被测组分浓度较高,监测方法灵敏度高,直接采集少量气样

High concentration of pollutants, high sensitivity of monitoring methods

# （一）注射器采样 Syringe sampling

100mL注射器,有机蒸气样品,当天分析

100mL syringe, organic vapor sample, immediate analysis

# （二）塑料袋采样 Plastic bag sampling

与被测组分不发生反应,聚四氟乙烯\聚乙烯\聚酯袋

Does not react with the measured component, PTFE\Polyethylene\Polyester bag

# （三）采气管采样 Tube sampling

管式玻璃容器,两端旋塞,比气管容积大6-10倍的气体

Glass tube container

# （四）真空瓶采样 Vacuum bottle sampling

耐压玻璃容器,装入吸收液,抽真空

Pressure resistant glass container, Loading absorbing solution, vacuum

# 二、富集(浓缩)采样法 Enrichment (concentrate) sampling

- 污染物浓度比较低(10⁻⁹-10⁻⁶数量级) Low concentration
- 采样时间长,代表采样时段平均浓度 Long sampling time, represents the average concentration during the sampling period
- 溶液吸收法\填充柱阻留法\滤料阻留法\低温冷凝法/静电沉降法/扩散(或渗透)法/自然积集法
Solution absorption \Packed column retention\Filter retention method\Cryogenic condensation/Electrostatic deposition/diffusion(Or infiltration)law/Natural accumulation method

# （一）溶液吸收法 Solution absorption method:

采集空气中气态\蒸气态及某些气溶胶污染物的常用方法 Common method for collecting gaseous gas

| 125 | 80 | 100 | 75 |
| --- | -- | --- | -- |

气泡吸收管 冲击式吸收管 多孔筛板吸收管 玻璃筛板吸收瓶

气体吸收管（瓶）示意图

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 47】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 47】

# （一）溶液吸收法 Solution absorption method:

采集空气中气态\蒸气态及某些气溶胶污染物的常用方法

Common method for collecting gaseous gas

- 吸收效率取决于吸收速率和气样与吸收液的接触面积 Absorption efficiency depends on the absorption rate and the contact area between the gas and the absorption liquid.
- 吸收液：水、水溶液、有机溶剂 water, aqueous solution, organic solvent
- 吸收原理：
1. 气体分子溶解于溶液中的物理作用 physical: dissolve of the gases；
2. 化学反应 chemical reaction: 除溶液度特别大的气态污染物外，一般都选用伴有化学反应的吸收液 The chemical reaction is always used unless the gas is very easily to be dissolved.

# 吸收液选择原则 Criteria for adsorbent selection：

- 与被采集污染物发生化学反应或者对其溶解度大 React with the pollutants or high solubility
- 污染物被吸收后有足够的稳定时间，满足分析测定需求 Stable for a relatively long time for analysis
- 污染物被吸收后，有利于下一步分析测定，最好直接用于测定 Benefit the following measurement or can be directly measured.
- 吸收液毒性小、价格低、易购买，最好能回收利用 low in toxicity and price, easy to purchase, and preferably recycled

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 48】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 48】

# （二）填充柱阻留法 packed column retention

# 吸附型填充柱，分配型填充柱，反应型填充柱 Adsorption/participation/reactive

- 颗粒状固体吸附剂（活性炭、硅胶、分子筛、高分子多孔微球等）
- Granular solid adsorbent (activated carbon, silica gel, molecular sieve, polymeric porous microspheres, etc.)

| • 表面涂渍高沸点有机溶剂的惰性多孔颗粒物或纤维状物 | • Inert porous particles (diatomaceous earth) coated with high boiling point organic solvents |
| -------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| • 在有机溶剂中分配系数大的组分被保留        | • Components with a larger partition coefficient will be retained                             |
| • 发生化学反应而被滞留               | • Retain in the column due to chemical reaction                                               |

# 颗粒状填充剂 filler

- 空气 air
- 抽气泵 pump

# 填充柱阻留法示意图

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 49】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 49】

# (三) 滤料阻留法 Filter material retention method

- 将滤料（滤纸、滤膜等）放在采样夹上，用抽气装置抽气，则空气中的颗粒物被阻留在滤料上，称量滤料上富集的颗粒物质量，根据采样体积，可算空气中的颗粒物浓度. Place the filter material (filter paper/membrane, etc.) on the sampling clamp, and then pump, and the particles in the air will be retained on the filter material. The mass of the particulate matter will be weighed and calculated.
- 直接阻截、惯性碰撞、扩散沉降、静电引力、重力沉降等. Direct interception, inertial collision, diffusion settlement, electrostatic attraction, gravity settlement, etc.
- 低速采样：扩散沉降为主，对细小颗粒物采样效率高；高速采样：以惯性碰撞为主，对较大颗粒物采样效率高. Low-speed sampling: rely on diffusion and settlement, high efficiency for fine particles; High-speed sampling: mainly inertial collision, high efficiency for larger particles.
- 纤维状滤料、多孔状滤料. Fibrous/porous filter material.

# （四）低温冷凝法Low temperature condensation method

- 空气中某些沸点比较低的气态污染物：烯烃、醛类等Gaseous pollutants with low boiling point: olefins, aldehydes, etc.
- 半导体制冷器法和制冷剂法Semiconductor refrigerator and refrigerant method
- 效果好、采样量大、利于组分稳定，但是要考虑水蒸气的影响Effective, large sampling amount, stable components, but the influence of water vapor should be considered.

# （五）静电沉降法Electrostatic deposition

- 空气样品通过高压电场，气体分子电离，所产生的离子附着在气溶胶颗粒物上，使颗粒物带电荷，并在电场作用下收集
- The air sample is ionized by a high-voltage electric field, and the generated ions are attached to the aerosol particles, making the particles charged and collected under the electric field.

# （六）扩散(或渗透)法Diffusion (infiltration)

- 用于个体采样器采集气态和蒸气态有害物质Individual samplers to collect gaseous and vaporous hazardous substances
- 利用被测污染物分子自身扩散或渗透到达吸收层被吸收或者吸附The measured contaminant molecules diffuse or penetrate into the absorption layer to be absorbed/adsorbed
- 体积小、轻便Small and light

# （七）自然积集法Natural accumulation

- 利用重力、空气动力或浓差扩散作用采集被测物质by gravity, aerodynamics or concentration diffusion
- 降尘样品采集Dust collection
- 硫酸盐化速率样品采集 Sulfation rate sample collection

# （八）综合采样法A mixed method

- Alkali tablet method: glass fiber filter impregnated with potassium carbonate solution, sulfur dioxide, sulfuric acid mist can react with carbonate to form sulfate

# 四、采样效率efficiency

在规定的采样条件（如采样流量、污染物浓度范围、采样时间等）下所采集到的污染物量占其总量的百分数，由于污染物存在状态不同，评价方法也不同The percentages will be different based on different methods and the state of pollutants.

# (一) 采集气态和蒸气态污染物质效率的评价方法Evaluation method for the collection efficiency

| 绝对比较法                                     | 相对比较法                                                                         |
| ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 精确配制一个已知浓度的标准气，用所选用方法采集，测定被采集污染物浓度，计算采样效率 | 配制一个恒定的但不要求知道待测污染物准确浓度的气体样品，将2-3个采样管串联，采集所配置气体样品，采样结束后，分别测定各采样管中污染物浓度，计算采样效率. |
| K = 1 ¥ 100%                              | K = c + c¹ + c₂ ¥ 100%                                                        |
| c                                         | c₃                                                                            |

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 59】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 59】

# 四、采样效率efficiency

在规定的采样条件（如采样流量、污染物浓度范围、采样时间等）下所采集到的污染物量占其总量的百分数，由于污染物存在状态不同，评价方法也不同The percentages will be different based on different methods and the state of pollutants.

# （二）采集颗粒物效率的评价方法Evaluation method for efficiency of collecting particulate matter

- 颗粒数采样效率：所采集到的颗粒物颗粒数占总颗粒物的百分数
- Sampling efficiency for number of particles: the number of particles collected as a percentage of total particles
- 质量采样效率：所采集到的颗粒物质量占颗粒物总质量的百分数
- Sampling efficiency for mass: the mass of collected particulate matter as a percentage of the total mass of the particulate matter

# 4.1 二氧化硫的测定

# Determination of sulfur dioxide

# ❖ 性质及危害 Properties and effect

SO₂是一种无色、易溶于水、有刺激性气味的气体，能通过呼吸进入气管，对局部组织产生刺激和腐蚀作用，是诱发支气管炎等疾病的原因之一，特别是当它与烟尘等气溶胶共存时，可加重对呼吸道黏膜的损害。

colorless, water-soluble, pungent odorous gas, can enter the trachea through breathing and cause irritation and corrosion, cause disease like bronchitis. When it coexists with aerosols such as smoke and dust, the damage to the respiratory mucosa can be increased.

# ❖ 来源 Source

煤和石油等燃料的燃烧、含硫矿石的冶炼、硫酸等化工产品生产排放的废气。

Exhaust gas from the coal and petroleum combustion, the processes for the smelting of sulfur-bearing ore, and chemical products such as sulfuric acid.

# ❖ 测定方法 Methods

a.天然源：火山爆发

- ⮚ 分光光度法 Spectrophotometry
- ⮚ 恒电流库仑滴定法 Constant current coulometric titration

工业一酸性气体的释放 （例如冶炼厂和发电厂）

b.人工源：含S燃料的燃烧，矿石冶炼，化工厂生产过程的产物。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 66】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 66】

# 4.1 二氧化硫的测定

# Determination of sulfur dioxide

# ❑ 分光光度法

# 1、甲醛吸收-副玫瑰苯胺分光光度法

Formaldehyde absorbing-pararosaniline spectrophotometry

# 2、四氯汞盐吸收-副玫瑰苯胺分光光度法

Tetrachloromercury salt absorption-pararosaniline spectrophotometry

# 3、钍试剂分光光度法

Thorium reagent spectrophotometry

# ❑ 定电位电解法

Constant-potential electrolysis

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 67】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 67】

# 4.1 二氧化硫的测定

# Determination of sulfur dioxide

# 1、甲醛缓冲液吸收-盐酸副玫瑰苯胺分光光度法

# Formaldehyde buffer absorbing - pararosaniline hydrochloride spectrophotometry

# ❖ 原理 principle

气样中的SO₂被甲醛缓冲液吸收后，生成稳定的羟基甲基磺酸加成化合物，加入氢氧化钠溶液使加成化合物分解，释放出SO₂与盐酸副玫瑰苯胺反应，生成紫红色络合物，其最大吸收波长为577nm，用分光光度法定量测定。

SO₂ in the gas sample is absorbed by the formaldehyde buffer to form a stable additive compound of hydroxymethanesulfonic acid. The compound is decomposed by adding NaOH solution, and the released SO₂ reacts with the pararosaniline hydrochloride to form a purple-red complex. The maximum absorption wavelength of the material is 577 nm, which can be quantitatively determined by spectrophotometry.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 68】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 68】

# 4.1 二氧化硫的测定

# Determination of sulfur dioxide

# ❖测定要点 Key point

# 短时间采集的样品 For samples by short-time sampling:

- 将吸收管中的样品溶液移入10mL比色管中，用少量甲醛缓冲液洗涤吸收管，洗液并入比色管中并稀释至标线。加入0.5mL氨基磺酸钠溶液，混匀，放置10min除去氮氧化物的干扰。测定空气中二氧化硫的检出限为0.007mg/m³，测定下限为0.028mg/m³，测定上限为0.667mg/m³。
- The sample solution in the absorption tube is transferred into a 10 mL colorimetric tube. The absorption tube is washed with a small amount of formaldehyde buffer, and the washing solution is incorporated into the colorimetric tube and diluted to the mark. Place the samples for 10 min after mixing with 0.5 mL of sodium sulfamate solution to remove the interference of nitrogen oxides. The detection limit of sulfur dioxide in the air is 0.007 mg/m³. The lower limit of determination is 0.028 mg/m³, and the upper limit of determination is 0.667 mg/m³.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 69】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 69】

# 4.1 二氧化硫的测定

# Determination of sulfur dioxide

# ❖测定要点 Key point

24h连续采样样品 For samples after 24h continuous sampling:

- 将样品移入50mL容量瓶，用少量甲醛缓冲液洗涤吸收瓶后再倒入容量瓶中并稀释至标线。吸收适当体积样品于10mL比色管中，甲醛溶液稀释至标线。加入0.5mL氨基磺酸钠溶液，混匀，放置10min除去氮氧化物的干扰。测定空气中二氧化硫的检出限为0.004mg/m³ ，测定下限为0.014mg/m³，测定上限为0.347mg/m³。
- Transfer the sample into a 50mL volumetric flask, then wash the absorption bottle with a small amount of formaldehyde buffer and pour into the volumetric flask and dilute to the mark. Then take an appropriate volume of sample into a 10 mL colorimetric tube and dilute the formaldehyde solution to the mark. Place the samples for 10 min after mixing with 0.5 mL of sodium sulfamate solution to remove the interference of nitrogen oxides. The detection limit of sulfur dioxide in the air is 0.004 mg/m³. The lower limit of determination is 0.014 mg/m³, and the upper limit of measurement is 0.347 mg/m³.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 70】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 70】

# 4.1 二氧化硫的测定

# Determination of sulfur dioxide

# 2、四氯汞盐吸收-副玫瑰苯胺分光光度

Tetrachloromercury salt absorption-pararosaniline spectrophotometry

- 用KCl和HgCl₂配置的K₂[HgCl₄]吸收液，气样中的SO₂用该溶液吸收，生成稳定的二氯亚硫酸盐络合物，该络合物再与甲醛和盐酸副玫瑰苯胺作用，生成紫色络合物，其颜色深浅与SO₂含量成正比，用分光光度法测定。
- Prepare the adsorption solution, then adsorb SO₂ in the gas sample to form a stable dichlorosulfite complex.
- The complex react with formaldehyde and pararosaniline hydrochloride to form a purple-red complex. Then measure by spectrophotometry.

与经典的方法(GB)相比, 灵敏度高、选择性好，但吸收液毒性较大Compared with the classical method (GB), the sensitivity is high and the selectivity is good, but the absorption liquid is more toxic.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 71】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 71】

# 4.1 二氧化硫的测定

# Determination of sulfur dioxide

浓度计算公式：

SO₂（mg/m3)=(（A-A0)×Bs/Vn

式中：

- A—试样溶液的吸光度：
- A0—试剂空白溶液的吸光度；
- Bs—计算因子，单位吸光度对应的SO2的微克数，b/a，1/K
- Vn—换算至标准状态下的采样体积(L)。

# a 注意事项

1. 温度，酸度
1. 温度，酸度，显色时间等因素影响显色反应；标准溶液和试样溶液操作条件应保持一致
2. 氮氧化物，臭氧及锰，铁，铬等离子对测定有干扰。
1. 一采集后放置片刻，臭氧可自行分解：
2. 一加入磷酸和乙二胺四乙酸二钠盐可消除或减少某些金属离子的干扰。

# 4.2 氮氧化物(NOx)的测定

# Determination of nitrogen oxides (NOx)

# • 氮氧化物(NOₓ)的来源 Source

| 电力热力  | 4.50%  | 5.70% |
| ----- | ------ | ----- |
| 机动车尾气 | 2%     | 4%    |
| 非金属矿物 | 1.20%  |       |
| 石油化工  | 40.80% |       |
| 化工厂   | 11.20% |       |
| 生活源废气 | 30.60% |       |
| 黑色金属  |        |       |
| 其他    |        |       |

# • 存在状态及毒性 State and toxicity

NO为无色、无臭、微溶于水的气体，在空气中易被氧化成NO₂。 NO₂为棕红色具有强刺激性臭味的气体，毒性比NO高4倍，是引起支气管炎、肺损害等疾病的有害物质。

NO is a colorless, odorless gas with slightly soluble in water, which is easily oxidized to NO₂ in the air. NO₂ is a reddish-brown gas with a strong pungent odor. It is four times more toxic than NO and is a harmful substance that causes diseases such as bronchitis and lung damage.

Oxidation by acidified potassium permanganate solution

# 4.2 氮氧化物(NOx)的测定

# Determination of nitrogen oxides (NOx)

# 1、盐酸萘乙二胺分光光度法

# N-(1-naphthy)lethylenediamine dihydrochloride spectrophotometry

# ❖ 评价 Evaluation

采样与显色同时进行，操作简便，灵敏度高，是国内外普遍采用的方法。测定氮氧化物(NOx)时，需将NO氧化成NO2，因而采用酸性高锰酸钾溶液氧化法。Forming color components simultaneously with sampling. Easy to operate, high sensitivity, a commonly used method. When measuring NOx, it is necessary to oxidize NO to NO₂, and thus an acidified potassium permanganate solution is employed.

# ❖ 原理 principle

空气中NO2被吸收转变成亚硝酸和硝酸，用冰乙酸、对氨基苯磺酸和盐酸萘乙二胺配成溶液采样。在冰乙酸存在条件下，亚硝酸与对氨基苯磺酸发生重氮化反应，然后再与盐酸萘乙二胺偶合，生成玫瑰红色偶氮染料，其颜色深浅与气样中NO2的浓度成正比，用分光光度法测定。The NO2 in the air is absorbed and converted into nitrous acid and nitric acid, and is sampled by glacial acetic acid, p-aminobenzenesulfonic acid and naphthylethylenediamine hydrochloride. In the presence of glacial acetic acid, nitrous acid is diazotized with p-aminobenzenesulfonic acid, and then coupled with naphthylethylenediamine hydrochloride to form a rose red azo dye. The color depth is proportional to the concentration of NO₂ in the gas sample. Then measure by spectrophotometry.

---

> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 75】

# 4.2 氮氧化物(NOx)的测定

# Determination of nitrogen oxides (NOx)

# 1、盐酸萘乙二胺分光光度法

# N-(1-naphthy)lethylenediamine dihydrochloride spectrophotometry

吸收及显色反应如下

The absorption and color reaction are as follows:

2NO2 + H2O = HNO2 + HNO3
HO3S—NH2 + HNOCH3COOH → HO3SN+=N]CHCOO + 2H2O
HO3S—−N+=N]CH3COO + N−CH2−CH2−NH2−NH2·2HCl
HO3S—N=N

（玫瑰红色偶氮染料）

> 【来源：Chapter 3 · Lecture 7 · Slide 76】

# 4.2 氮氧化物(NOx)的测定

# Determination of nitrogen oxides (NOx)

# 2. 酸性高锰酸钾溶液氧化法

# Oxidation by acidified potassium permanganate solution

- 空气中NO₂被串联的第一支吸收瓶中吸收液吸收生成偶氮染料，空气中NO不与吸收液反应，通过氧化管被氧化为NO₂后，被串联的第二支吸收瓶中的吸收液吸收生成粉红色的偶氮染料，在波长540nm处，以蒸馏水为参比，测量其吸光度
- The NO₂ in the air is absorbed by liquid in the first absorption bottle to form an azo dye, and the NO in the air does not react with the absorption liquid but can be adsorbed by the second bottle after being oxidized to NO₂ through the oxidation tube. Measure its absorbance at a wavelength of 540 nm with distilled water as a reference.

| 1.空气入口 | 2.显色吸收液瓶 | 3.酸性高锰酸钾溶液氧化瓶 | 4.显色吸收液瓶 | 5.干燥瓶 | 6.止水夹 | 7.流量计 | 8.抽气泵 |
| ------ | -------- | ------------- | -------- | ----- | ----- | ----- | ----- |

# Schematic diagram of NO₂, NO, and NOx sampling processes in air

# 4.2 氮氧化物 (NOx) 的测定

# Determination of nitrogen oxides (NOx)

# 2. 酸性高锰酸钾溶液氧化法

# Oxidation by acidified potassium permanganate solution

# 注意事项

# Precautions：

1. 当空气中SO₂质量浓度为NOₓ质量浓度的30倍时，NO₂的测定结果偏低;

When the SO₂ mass concentration in the air is 30 times of the value of NOx, the result of NO₂ is underestimate.
2. 当空气中含有过氧乙酰硝酸酯（PAN）时，NO₂的测定结果偏高;

When the air contains peroxyacetyl nitrate (PAN), the result of NO₂ is overestimates.
3. 当空气中臭氧质量浓度超过0.25mg/m³, NO₂的测定结果偏低;

When the mass concentration of ozone in the air exceeds 0.25 mg/m³, the result of NO₂ is underestimate.
