# 二、危险废物的定义和鉴别

# 定义（ Definition ）

指在国家危险废物名录中，和根据国务院环境保护部门规定的危险废物鉴别标准认定的具有危险性的废物。(contained in the National Hazardous Wastes List and identified as hazardous based on the standard of the Environmental Protection Departments)

工业废物中的危险废物占总量的5%-10%(Hazardous wastes account for 5%-10% of the industrial wastes)

# 鉴别（ identification ）

1. 是否引起或严重导致人类和动植物死亡率增加 (Cause serious increases in human, animal and plant mortality?)
2. 是否引起各种疾病的增加 (Cause an increase in various diseases?)
3. 是否降低对疾病的抵抗力 (Reduce the resistance to diseases?)
4. 是否在处理、贮存、运送、处置或其他管理不当时，对人体健康或环境会造成现实的或潜在的危害 (Pose a real or potential hazard to human health or the environment during the handling, storage, transportation, disposal ?)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 20】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 20】

# 危险废物的特性 (Characteristics of hazardous waste)

- • 含闪点低于60℃的液体 (Liquid with a flash point below 60℃)
- • 经摩擦或吸湿和自发的变化具有着火倾向的固体 (Solid that has a tendency to ignite through friction or moisture absorption and spontaneous changes)
- • 含水固体废物，或本身不含水但加入水后其浸出液的pH≤2或pH≥12.5的废物 (Wet solid waste, or dry waste which can generate leachate with pH ≤ 2 or pH ≥ 12.5 after adding water)
- • 或最低温度为55.7℃以下时对钢制品每年的腐蚀深度大于0.64cm/a 的废物 (Wastes with a corrosion depth > 0.64 cm/a for steel products at a minimum temperature of ≤ 55.7℃)
- • 无爆震时就很易发生剧烈变化 (dramatic change without strong knock);
- • 和水剧烈反应 (significant reaction with water);
- • 能和水形成爆炸性混合物 (can form explosive mixture with water);
- • 和水混合产生毒性气体、蒸汽或烟雾 (can generate toxic gases, vapors or fumes when mixed with water);
- • 有引发源或加热时能爆震或爆炸 (can explode when activated or heated);
- • 常温常压下易爆炸和爆炸性反应 (explosive at normal temperature and pressure);
- • 根据其它法规定义的爆炸品 (explosive materials mentioned in other regulations).

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 21】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 21】

# 危险废物的特性

# Characteristics of hazardous waste：

- 含有天然放射性元素的废物，放射性比活大于3700 Bq/kg 的固体废物
- (Waste with natural radioactive elements or with an activity>3700Bq/kg);
- 含有人工放射性元素的废物或放射性比活(以Bq/kg为单位)大于限制浓度的10-100倍(半衰期>60天)的固体废物
- (Solid waste with artificial radioactive elements or activity that is 10-100 times (half-life > 60 days) than the limit concentration of open-air water sources).

# 易燃性 (Flammability)

# 腐蚀性 (Corrosive)

- 按规定的浸出方法进行浸取，当浸出液中有一种或一种以上有害成分的浓度超过表4.1.1所示鉴别标准的固体废物
- (Solid waste whose leachate contains one or more harmful components exceeding the limit listed in Table 4.1.1., after being leached according to the standard method)

# 反应性 (Reactivity)

# 放射性 (Radioactivity)

- 能引起小鼠(大鼠) 在48 h内，死亡半数以上的固体废物，参考制定的有害物质卫生标准的试验方法，进行半数致死量(LD50)试验，评定毒性大小
- (Solid waste that can cause more than half of the deaths for mice (rats) within 48 hours. A half-lethal dose (LD50) test can be used to assess the toxicity according to the standard method.)

# 急性毒性 (Acute toxicity)

- 如水生生物毒性、植物毒性、生物累积性、遗传变异性和刺激性等
- (Solid waste which can cause other toxicity such as aquatic toxicity, phytotoxicity, bioaccumulation, genetic variability and irritation)

# Disposal

# Sanitary landfills

Sanitary Landfills are designed to greatly reduce or eliminate the risks that waste disposal may pose to the public health and environmental quality.

- The bottom and sides of landfills are lined with layers of clay or plastic to keep the liquid waste, known as leachate, from escaping into the soil.
- The leachate is collected and pumped to the surface for treatment. Monitoring wells are dug in the vicinity of the landfill to monitor groundwater quality.
- Some sanitary landfills are used to recover energy. The natural anaerobic decomposition of the waste in the landfill produces landfill gases which include carbon dioxide, methane and traces of other gases.

| MODERN LANDFILL | METHANE GAS RECOVERY SYSTEM  | LEACHATE TREATMENT CAP SYSTEM |
| --------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| TRASH           | WELL TO MONITOR GROUND WATER | LEACHATE COLLECTION SYSTEM    |
| CLAY LINER      | AQUIFER                      |                               |

# 4.2 固体废物样品的采集和制备

# (Collection and preparation of solid waste sample)

# 一、样品的采集 (Sample collection)

- # (一) 采样工具(Sampling tools)

尖头钢锹(Pointed steel shovel)、钢尖镐(Steel tip)、采样铲(Sampling Shovel)(采样器)(Sampler)、具盖采样桶或内衬塑料的采样袋(Covered sampling drum or plastic-lined sampling bag)。
- # (二) 采样方案的制订(Develop a sampling plan)

采样前应先进行采样方案的设计，内容包括：采样目的、背景调查和现场踏勘、采样程序、安全措施、质量控制、采样记录和报告等(The sampling plan should be designed before sampling, including sampling purposes, background investigation and site survey, sampling procedures, safety measures, quality control, sampling records and reports)。

1. 采样目的(sampling purposes)
2. 背景调查和现场踏勘(background investigation and site survey)
3. 采样程序(sampling procedures)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 33】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 33】

# 1. 采样目的 (Sampling purposes)

采样的具体目的据固体废物监测目的来确定 (The specific purpose of sampling is determined by the purpose of solid waste monitoring)

# 固体废物监测目的主要有 (The main purposes of solid waste monitoring)：

- 鉴别固体废物的特性并对其进行分类，进行固体废物环境污染监测，为综合利用或处置固体废物提供依据 (Identify the characteristics of solid waste and classify the waste, conduct environmental pollution monitoring of solid waste, provide a basis for integrated utilization or disposal of solid waste)；
- 污染环境事故调查分析和应急监测 (Investigation and analysis of environmental pollution accidents and emergency monitoring)；
- 科学研究或环境影响评价等 (Scientific research or environmental impact assessment, etc)。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 34】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 34】

# 2. 背景调查和现场踏勘 (Background investigation and field survey)

进行现场踏勘时，应着重了解工业固体废物的以下几个方面 (When conducting on-site surveys, the following aspects of industrial solid waste should be emphasized)：

- 生产单位或处置单位 (Departments of production and disposal)
- 种类、形态、数量和特性(物理特性和化学特性) (Type, morphology, quantity and characteristics (physical and chemical properties))
- 实验及分析的误差和要求(Errors and requirements of experiment and analysis)
- 环境污染、监测分析的历史资料(Historical data on environmental pollution, monitoring and analysis)
- 产生、堆存、综合利用及现场和周围情况，了解现场及周围环境 (The site and the surrounding for production, storage, integrated utilization)

# 3. 采样程序 (Sampling procedures)

份样是指由一批固体废物中的一个点或一个部位，按规定量取出的样品(个数)，根据固体废物批量大小确定应采的份样数

Number of samples refers to the sample (number) with specific amount taken from a point or a part for a batch of the solid waste. The number of samples should be determined according to the batch size of the solid waste.

根据固体废物的最大粒度(95%以上能通过的最小筛孔尺寸)确定份样量

Determine the number of samples based on the maximum particle size of the solid waste (the minimum mesh size which can be passed by more than 95% of the sample)

根据采样方法，随机采集份样，组成总样(见下图)，并认真填写采样记录表

According to the sampling method, collect the samples randomly for a total sample (see the figure below), and fill in the sampling record form carefully.

| 批量   | 份样 | 份样 | 份样 |
| ---- | -- | -- | -- |
| 总样   |    |    |    |
| 样品制备 |    |    |    |

# 4.2 固体废物样品的采集和制备

# (Collection and preparation of solid waste sample)

# ❖  (三) 份样数(Number of samples)

| 批量大小/(液体kL, t(固体)⁻1 | 最少份样数/份 |
| ------------------- | ------- |
| <5                  | 5       |
| 5\~10               | 10      |
| 50\~100             | 15      |
| 100\~500            | 20      |
| 500\~1000           | 25      |
| 1000～5000           | 30      |
| >5000               | 35      |

# ❖  (四) 份样量 (Sample mass)

份样量是指构成一个份样的固体废物的质量。样品多才具有代表性，因此份样量不能少于某一限度。份样量达到一定限度后，再增加质量不能显著提高采样的准确度 (the mass of solid waste for a sample. To be representative, the sample mass cannot be less than a certain limit. When the sample mass reaches a certain limit, the accuracy of the sampling cannot be increased significantly.)

取决于固体废物的粒度 (Depend on the particle size of the solid waste)

最小份样量大致与固体废物的最大粒径的a次方成正比，与固体废物的不均匀度成正比 (The minimum sample mass is roughly proportional to the α power of the maximum particle size of the solid waste, and is also proportional to the heterogeneity of the solid waste.)

# 最小份样量和采样铲容量(Minimum sample mass and the volume of sampling shovel)

| 最大粒度(Maximum granularity)/mm | 最小份样量(Minimum sample mass)/kg | 采样铲容量 (volume of sampling shovel)/mL |
| ---------------------------- | ----------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| >150                         | 30                            |                                      |
| 100\~150                     | 15                            | 16000                                |
| 50\~100                      | 5                             | 7000                                 |
| 40\~50                       | 3                             | 1700                                 |
| 20\~40                       | 2                             | 800                                  |
| 10\~20                       | 1                             | 300                                  |
| <10                          | 0.5                           | 125                                  |

# (五) 采样点 (Sampling point)

采样点原则(Principle of sampling points)：

- 对于堆存、运输中的固态工业固体废物和大池(坑、塘)中的液态工业固体废物，可按对角线、梅花形、棋盘式、蛇形等布点法确定采样点 (For industrial solid waste in stockpiling or transportation, or liquid industrial solid waste in large pools (pit, pond), sampling points can be determined according to the diagonal line, quincunx, checkerboard, S-shape etc.)
- 对于粉末状、小颗粒状的工业固体废物，可按垂直方向、一定深度的部位等点分布确定采样点(For powder and small granular industrial solid waste, the sampling points can be determined equally in the vertical direction within a certain depth)
- 对于运输车及容器内的固体废物，按表中选取所需最少采样车数(容器数)，可按上部(表面下相当于总体积的1/6深处)、中部(表面下相当于总体积的1/2深处)、下部(表面下相当于总体积的5/6深处) 确定采样点 (For solid waste in transport vehicles and containers, choose the minimum number of vehicles (containers) required according to Table 4.2.1, and determine the sampling points as below: upper (depth around 1/6 volume below the surface), middle (depth around 1/2 volume), lower (depth around 5/6 of the total volume below the surface)。)

所需最少的采样车数表 (Minimum number of vehicles required)

| 车数（容器） (Number of vehicles(containers)) | 所需最少采样车数 (Minimum number of vehicles required) |
| --------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
| <10                                     | 5                                              |
| 10\~25                                  | 10                                             |
| 25\~50                                  | 20                                             |
| 50\~100                                 | 30                                             |
| >100                                    | 50                                             |

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 38】 -->

# (五) 采样点 (Sampling point)

# 运输车及容器采样(Transport vehicle and container sampling)

- 当车数不多于该批废物规定的份样数时 (the number of vehicles is less than the number of samples in the batch of waste)：
- 每车应采份样数＝规定份样数/车数(number of samples per vehicle= specified number of samples divided by the number of cars)

当车数多于该批废物规定的份样数时(the number of vehicles is more than the number of samples in the batch of waste)：
- 按下表确定所需最少采样车数(determine the minimum number of sampling vehicles according to the table below)

| 车数/辆   | 所需最少采样车数/辆 |
| ------ | ---------- |
| <10    | 5          |
| 10～25  | 10         |
| 25～50  | 20         |
| 50～100 | 30         |
| >100   | 50         |

车中采样点的布设(Layout of sampling points in the vehicle)：

> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 39】

# (五) 采样点 (Sampling point)

废渣堆采样点(Sampling point in waste piles)

# (六) 采样方法(Sampling method)

- 简单随机采样法(Simple random sampling)：抽签法(lottery)；随机数字表达法(Random number representation)
- 系统采样法(System sampling)：在生产现场按照一定顺序排列或以运送带、管道等形式连续排出的固体废物，应先确定废物的批量，然后按一定的质量或时间间隔采集一个样本(Solid wastes that are arranged in a certain order at the production site or continuously discharged in the form of conveyor belts, pipes, etc., should first determine the batch size of the waste, and then collect a sample at a certain quality or time interval.)
- 分层采样法(Stratified sampling)
- 两阶段采样法(Two-stage sampling)：由许多桶、箱、袋装时，要分阶段采样(When using many barrels, boxes, and bags, it is necessary to sample in stages.)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 40】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 40】

# 二、样品的制备(Samples preparation)

# ❑(一)制样工具(Tools for sample preparation)

粉碎机、药碾、钢锤、标准套筛、十字样板、机械缩分器 (Crusher, mortar, steel hammer, sieve, sample divider)。

# ❑(二)制样要求(Requirements for sample preparation)

1. 制样过程中，防止样品产生任何化学变化和污染(Avoid any chemical changes and contamination)；
2. 湿样品应在室温下自然干燥，使其达到适于破碎、筛分、缩分的程度(Wet samples should be naturally dried at room temperature)；
3. 制备的样品过筛后装瓶备用(The prepared sample is sieved and bottled for use)。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 41】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 41】

# 二、样品的制备(Samples preparation)

# (三)制样程序(Sample preparation program)

1. 粉碎(Crush)

把全部样品逐级破碎，过5mm筛孔。粉碎过程中，不可随意丢弃难于破碎的粗粒 (All samples were crushed step by step and then passed through a 5mm mesh. Coarse particles which are difficult to break should not be discarded.)
2. 缩分(shrink)

将样品于清洁、平整、不吸水的板面上堆成圆锥型，每铲物料自圆锥顶端落下，使均匀地沿锥尖散落，不可使圆锥中心错位。反复转堆，至少三周，使其充分混合。然后将圆锥顶端轻轻压平，摊开物料后，用十字板自上压下，分成四等份，取两个对角的等份，重复操作数次，直至不少于1kg试样为止 (The sample is stacked into a conical shape, and the materials should fall from the top of the pile. Repeat the stack for at least three times. Then gently flatten the top of the cone, spread the material, press it from the top with a cross plate, divide it into four equal parts, take two diagonal parts, repeat the operation several times until the final sample is no less than 1kg)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 42】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 42】

# 三、样品水分的测定 (Determination of sample moisture)

1. 无机物(inorganic)：称取样品20g左右于105℃下干燥，恒重至±0.1g，测定水分含量

Weigh 20g of sample and dry at 105°C, then determine the moisture content
2. 有机物(organic)：样品于60 ℃下干燥24h，测定水分含量

The sample should be dried at 60 °C for 24h for moisture content
3. 固体废物测定结果以干样品计算，当污染物含量小于0.1％时以mg/kg表示，含量大于0.1％时则以百分含量表示，并说明是水溶性或总量

The measurement results are calculated based on dry weight. When the pollutant content is less than 0.1%, it should be expressed as mg/kg. When the content is larger than 0.1%, it should expressed as percentage. Specify whether it is water soluble or total amount.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 43】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 43】

# 四、样品pH值的测定(Determination of sample pH)

- 见本章第三节，三、腐蚀性试验方法 (Section 3 of this chapter: Corrosion test methods)
- 由于固体废物的不均匀性，测定时应将各点分别测定，测定结果以实际测定pH值范围表示，而不是通过计算混合样品平均值表示 (Due to heterogeneity of solid waste, each point should be measured separately, and the measurement results should be expressed in the actual measured pH range, rather than by calculating the average value)
- 由于样品中二氧化碳含量影响pH值，并且二氧化碳达到平衡极为迅速，所以采样后必须立即测定 (Because the carbon dioxide content could influence the pH value of the sample and the carbon dioxide can reach equilibrium very quickly, the pH value must be measured immediately after sampling)

---

❖ 贴标签：注明编号、废物名称、采样地点、批量、采样人、制样人、时间 (Labeling: the number, waste name, sampling location, batch, sampler, time)

# 4.3 有害特性的监测方法

# (Method for monitoring harmful characteristics)

# 一、急性毒性的初筛试验

# (Screening test for acute toxicity)

# 毒性试验动物

(Animals for toxicity test)：体重18-24g小白鼠 (或200-300g大白鼠) Mouse weight of 18-24g or 200-300g

# 试验步骤

(Test procedure)：

1. 称取制备好的样品100g，置于500mL具磨口玻璃塞的三角瓶中，加入100mL水 (pH为5.8~6.3) (固液比为1:1)，振摇3min于室温下静止浸泡24 h，用中速定量滤纸过滤，滤液留待灌胃用
2. Weigh 100g of the prepared sample, place it in a 500mL triangular flask with a ground-in glass stopper, add 100mL water (pH 5.8~6.3) (solid-liquid ratio is 1:1), shake for 3min and soak at room temperature for 24 h, filtered with medium-speed filter paper, and the filtrate is reserved for intragastric administration.
3. 对10只小白鼠（或大白鼠）进行一次性灌胃，每只灌浸出液0.50(或4.80)mL，对灌胃后的小白鼠（或大白鼠）进行中毒症状观察，记录48 h内动物死亡数
4. Intragastric infusion for 10 small mice (or big mice) with 0.50 (or 4.80) mL filtrate. Then observe the poisoning symptoms and record the number of animal deaths within 48 hours.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 48】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 48】

# 二、易燃性的试验方法(Test method for flammability)

易燃性 (Flammability)：测定闪点 (Determine the flash point)

- 闪点较低的液态状废物和燃烧剧烈而持续的非液态状废物，由于摩擦、吸湿、点燃等自发的化学变化会发热、着火，或可能由于它的燃烧引起人体或环境的危害 (Liquid waste with low flash point and solid waste that burns violently and continuously, may cause heat or fire due to spontaneous chemical changes such as friction, moisture absorption, ignition, etc., and therefore may cause damage to human or environment.)
- 采用闭口闪点测定仪测定闪点。温度计采用1号温度计(-30-170℃)或2号温度计(100-300℃) 。防护屏采用镀锌铁皮制成，高度550-650mm，宽度以适用为度，屏身内壁漆成黑色 (Use a closed flash point tester to determine the flash point)

# 试验步骤(Test procedure)

- 按标准要求加热试样至一定温度，停止搅拌，每升高1℃点火一次，至试样上方刚出现蓝色火焰时，立即读出温度计上的温度值 (Heat the sample to a certain temperature according to the standard requirements, stop stirring, and ignite every elevated 1°C. When the blue flame appears above the sample, read the temperature value on the thermometer immediately.)
- 操作过程细节参见国标GB/T261-2008 (闪点测定法 宾斯基-马丁闭口杯法）(The operation process details should be referred to the national standard GB/T261-2008.)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 49】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 49】

# 三、腐蚀性的试验方法(Test method for corrosive)

# 腐蚀性 (Corrosivity)：

- 通过接触能损伤生物细胞组织或腐蚀物体而引起危害 Causing damage by direct contact

# 测定方法(test methods)：

- 一种是测定pH值；另一种是指测定在55.7℃以下对钢制品的腐蚀率
- One method is to measure the pH value;
- The other is to determine the corrosion rate of steel products below 55.7℃。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 50】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 50】

# 三、腐蚀性的试验方法(Test method for corrosive)

# pH值的测定 (pH计或酸度计) (pH value by pH meter or acidity meter)

- 对稠状物料可离心或过滤后，测其液体的pH值
- After centrifuging or filtering the thick material, measure the pH value of the liquid
- 对粉、粒、块状物料，称取制备好的样品50g(干基)，置于1 L塑料瓶中，加入新鲜蒸馏水250 mL，使固液质量比为1:5，加盖密封后，放在振荡机上，于室温下，连续振荡30 min，静置30 min后，测上清液pH值，每种废物取两个平行样品测定其pH值，差值不得大于0.15。否则应再取样品重复试验，取中位数报告结果
- For powder, granules and bulk materials, weigh 50g sample (dry), place it in a 1 L plastic bottle, add 250 mL of fresh distilled water, and make the solid-liquid mass ratio as 1:5. Shake it continuously for 30 min at room temperature. After standing for 30 min, measure the pH of the supernatant. Take two parallel samples for each waste and measure the pH value. The difference should not exceed 0.15. Otherwise, the sample should be repeated again.
- 对含水量高、呈流态状的稀泥或浆状物料，可将电极直接插入进行pH值测量
- For mud or slurry materials with high water content, the pH value can be measured by directly inserting the electrode.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 51】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 51】

# 四、反应性的试验方法(Test method for reactive)

# 测定方法 (Test method)：

- 撞击感度测定 (Impact sensitivity test)
- 摩擦感度测定 (Friction sensitivity test)
- 差热分析测定 (Differential thermal analysis)
- 爆炸点测定 (Explosion point test)
- 火焰感度测定 (Flame sensitivity test)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 52】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 52】

# 五、遇水反应性的试验方法(Test method for water reactivity)

# 遇水反应性 (Water reactivity)：

1. 固体废物与水发生剧烈反应而放出热量，使体系温度升高。可用温升试验测定 (The solid wastes react with water violently and release heat, which cause temperature increase.)
2. 与水反应释放出有害气体，如乙炔、硫化氢、砷化氢、氰化氢 (React with water and release harmful gas, such as acetylene, hydrogen sulfide, arsine, hydrogen cyanide).

# 试验步骤 (Test procedures)：

称取固体废物50g，置于250mL的反应器内，加入25 mL水 (用1mol/L HCl调节pH值为4)，加盖密封后，固定在振荡器上，振荡频率为110±10次/min，振荡30 min后停机，静置10 min. 用注射器抽气50 mL，注入不同的5 mL吸收液中，测定其氰化氢、硫化氢、砷化氢、乙炔的含量。

Weigh 50g solid waste, place it in a 250 mL reactor, add 25mL water (pH 4 by 1mol/L HCl), seal it, and fix it on the shaker at a frequency of 110±10 times/min, after 30 min of shaking, stop and stand for 10min. Pump 50mL with a syringe, inject into different 5mL of absorption liquid, and determine the content of hydrogen cyanide, hydrogen sulfide, arsine and acetylene).

# 固体废物反应器示意图

| Solid waste reactor schematic |            |
| ----------------------------- | ---------- |
| 1.固体废物                        | 2.250mL塑料瓶 |
| 3.橡皮塞                         | 4.玻璃管      |
| 5.乳胶管                         | 6.止水夹      |
| 7.气体抽气口                       |            |

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 53】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 53】

# 六、浸出毒性试验 (Leaching toxicity)

# 浸出方法(Leaching method )：

- 国际上各种浸出方法：德国的 DEV-S4、荷兰的 NEN7345，以及美国的 1312 (SPLP) 和 1311 (TCLP)等
- International leaching methods: DEV-S4 in Germany, NEN7345 in the Netherlands, and 1312 (SPLP) and 1311 (TCLP) in the US

# 4.4 生活垃圾和卫生保健机构废物的监测

# (Monitoring of municipal solid waste and health-care waste)

# 法规标准生活垃圾处理技术指南

2010-04-22

生活垃圾处理是城市管理和公共服务的重要组成部分，是建设资源节约型和环境友好型社会，实施治污减排，确保城市公共卫生安全，提高人居环境质量和生态文明水平，实现城市科学发展的一项重要工作。我国已颁布的《城市生活垃圾处理及污染防治技术政策》与我国经济发展水平相适应，符合国际生活垃圾处理技术发展方向，在其指导下，我国生活垃圾处理设施建设与处理水平有了较大提高。但是，随着我国经济社会的快速发展和城镇化进程的加快，城市人口不断增加，生活垃圾产生量持续上升同处理能力不足间的矛盾日益凸显，生活垃圾处理与管理工作面临严峻挑战。为保障我国生活垃圾无害化处理能力的不断增强、无害化处理水平不断提高，指导各地选择适宜的生活垃圾处理技术路线，有序开展生活垃圾处理设施规划、建设、运行和监管，依据《中华人民共和国固体废物污染环境防治法》。

# 法规标准生活垃圾焚烧污染控制标准

2014-07-01

生活垃圾焚烧污染控制标准（GB18485-2014代替GB18485-2001）《生活垃圾焚烧污染控制标准》（GB18485-2014）修改单

# 法规标准生活垃圾焚烧污染控制标准（已废止）

2002-01-01

生活垃圾焚烧污染控制标准（GB18485-2001）

# 法规标准生活垃圾填埋场污染控制标准

2008-07-01

生活垃圾填埋场污染控制标准（GB16889-2008）

# 法规标准生活垃圾焚烧飞灰污染控制技术规范（试行）

2020-08-27

生活垃圾焚烧飞灰污染控制技术规范（试行）（HJ1134—2020）

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 55】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 55】

# 一、生活垃圾及其分类 (Municipal solid waste and its classification)

- # 生活垃圾的概念 (Definition)

• 城镇居民在日常生活中抛弃的固体垃圾，主要包括：生活垃圾、医院垃圾、市场垃圾、建筑垃圾和街道扫集物等 (The solid waste discarded by urban residents in their daily lives, including domestic waste, hospital waste, market waste, construction waste and street sweeps)。

• 其中医院垃圾和建筑垃圾单独处理 (Hospital waste and construction waste should be treated separately)
- # 生活垃圾的分类 (Classification)

1. 废品类；废金属、废塑料、废纸类等 (scrap metal, waste plastics, waste paper, etc)
2. 厨房类 (亦称厨余垃圾) (kitchen waste)；
3. 灰土类；修建、清理时的土、煤、灰渣等 (soil, coal, ash during construction and cleaning)
- # 处理方法 (Disposal methods)

• 焚烧 (包括热解、气化)(Incineration (including pyrolysis, gasification) )；

• 卫生填埋 (Sanitary landfill)；

• 堆肥 (Compost )。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 56】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 56】

# 二、生活垃圾特性分析

# 1.垃圾采集和试样处理

充分研究垃圾产生地区的基本情况，如居民情况、生活水平、堆放时间；还要考虑在收集、运输、储存过程等可能的变化，然后制定周密的采样计划。采样过程必须详细记录地点、时间、种类、表观特性等。在记录卡传递过程中，必须有专人签署便于查核。

Study the conditions of the areas, such as the residents living conditions, living standards, and stacking time; Consider possible changes during collection, transportation, and storage processes. Develop a careful sampling plan. The sampling process must record the location, time, type, and apparent characteristics in detail.

采样量(Sampling weight)

G＝0.06d
式中 G—试样质量 (Sample weight), kg
d—垃圾的最大粒度 (Maximum granularity of the waste), mm

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 57】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 57】

# 二、生活垃圾特性分析

# 2. 垃圾的粒度分级

粒度采用筛分法，按筛目排列，依次连续摇动 15 min，依次转到下号筛子，然后计算每一粒度微粒所占的百分比。如果需要在试样干燥后再称量，则需在70℃的温度下烘干24 h，然后再在干燥器中冷却后筛分。

The particle size is sieved, arranged according to the mesh, continuously shaken for 15 minutes, sequentially transferred to the next sieve, and then the percentage of particles per particle size is calculated. If it is necessary to weigh the sample after drying, it should be dried at 70 ° C for 24 h, then cooled in a desiccator and sieved。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 58】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 58】

# 二、生活垃圾特性分析

# 3. 淀粉的测定 (Starch test)

堆肥过程中，需借助淀粉量分析堆肥的腐熟程度，生成淀粉碘化络合物，颜色变化取决于堆肥的腐熟程度.

During the composting process, the maturity degree of the compost is analyzed by the amount of starch. A starch iodinated complex can be generated and the color change depends on the degree of composting.

# 分析试验的步骤(Analytical test steps):

1. 将1g堆肥置于100mL烧杯中，滴入几滴酒精使其湿润，再加20mL 36％的高氯酸 (Place 1g of compost in a 100mL beaker, add a few drops of alcohol to make it moist, and add 20mL of 36% perchloric acid);
2. 用纹网滤纸(90号纸)过滤 (Filter with mesh filter paper);
3. 加入20mL碘反应剂到滤液中并搅动 (Add 20 mL of iodine reagent to the filtrate and stir);
4. 将几滴滤液滴到白色板上，观察其颜色变化。深蓝-浅蓝-灰-绿-黄 (Drop a few drops of the filter onto a white plate and observe the color change. Dark blue – light blue - gray-green–yellow).

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 59】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 59】

# 4. 生物降解性的测定 (biodegradability)

垃圾中含有大量天然的和人工合成的有机物，有的容易被生物降解，有的难以降解，可以在室温下对垃圾生物降解适当估计COD (Municipal solid waste contains plenty of natural and synthetic organic matter, some of which are easily biodegraded while some are difficult to be degraded. COD can be properly estimated for biodegradation of waste at room temperature.)

# 在室温下粗测COD的试验步骤 (Procedures for COD measurement at room temperature):

1. 称取0.5 g已烘干磨碎的试样于500 mL锥形瓶中 (Weigh 0.5 g of dried sample in a 500mL conical flask);
2. 准确量取20mL[c1/6(K₂Cr₂O₇)＝2mo1/L]重铬酸钾溶液加入样品瓶中并充分混合 (Add 20 mL[c1/6(K₂Cr₂O₇)＝2mo1/L] of potassium dichromate solution into the sample);
3. 量取20mL硫酸加到样品瓶中 (Measure 20 mL of sulfuric acid into the sample);
4. 在室温下将这一混合物放置12 h且不断摇动 (Shake this mixture at room temperature for 12 h);
5. 加入约15 mL蒸馏水 (Add about 15 mL of distilled water);
6. 再依次加入10 mL磷酸，0.2 g氟化钠和30滴指示剂 (Then add 10 mL of phosphoric acid, 0.2 g of sodium fluoride and 30 drops of indicator);
7. 用标准硫酸亚铁铵溶液滴定 (Titration with standard ammonium ferrous sulfate solution);
8. 用同样的方法在不放试样的情况下做空白试验 (Blank test).

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 60】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 60】

# 5．热值的测定：热量计法或热耗法

(Calorific value test: calorimeter method or heat loss method)

焚烧是有机工业固体废物、生活垃圾、部分医院垃圾处置的重要方法，部分垃圾焚烧可以发电
(Incineration is an important method for disposing organic industrial waste, municipal solid waste and some hospital waste. Waste incineration can generate electricity)

热值是垃圾焚烧处置的重要指标，分为高热值(H₀)和低热值(Hₙ)
(Calorific value is an important indicator for waste incineration disposal, which is divided into high calorific value (H₀) and low calorific value (Hₙ))

垃圾中可燃物质燃烧时产生的反应水一般以水蒸气形式挥发，因此，相当一部分能量不能被利用。
所以当垃圾高热值测出后，应扣除水蒸气和燃烧时加热物质所需要的热量，由高热值换算成低热值
(The water generated during combustion is generally volatilized as water vapor, and therefore consumes a considerable portion of energy. Therefore, after measuring the high calorific value of the waste, the energy required for the steam and heating the substances during combustion should be deducted, which can be converted to a low calorific value)

# 高热值与低热值换算：

| Hₙ | = | Hₒ | × | 100 - (w + W) / 100 - 1 | - 5.85W |
| -- | - | -- | - | ----------------------- | ------- |

式中：

- Hₙ——低热值，kJ/kg；
- Hₒ——高热值，kJ/kg；
- w₁——惰性物质含量（质量分数）, ％；
- W——垃圾的表面湿度，％；
- WL——剩余的和吸湿性的湿度，％。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 61】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 61】

# 三、渗滤液分析 (Leachate analysis)

填埋初期，由于地下水和地表水的流入、雨水的渗入以及垃圾本身的分解，产生大量污水，称为垃圾渗滤液 (In the early stage of landfill, a large amount of sewage is produced due to the inflow of groundwater and surface water, the infiltration of rainwater, and the decomposition of the waste itself, which is called landfill leachate).

是填埋处置中最主要的污染源 (The most important pollution source of landfill disposal)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 62】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 62】

# 三、渗滤液分析 (Leachate analysis)

# 1．渗滤液的特性 (Leachate characteristics)

1. 成分的不稳定性：主要取决于垃圾组成 (Instability: mainly depends on the composition of the waste)
2. 浓度的可变性：主要取决于填埋时间 (The variability of concentration: mainly depends on the landfill time)
3. 组成的特殊性：垃圾中存在的物质，渗滤液中不一定存在。而一般废水中存在的组分渗沥液中也不一定存在，例如油类、氰化物、铬和汞等 (The particularity of the composition: the substances present in the municipal may not exist in the leachate. The components in wastewater may be also not contained in leachate, like oils, cyanides, chromium and mercury)
4. 渗滤液是不同于生活污水的特殊污水 (Leachate is a special sewage different from domestic sewage)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 63】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 63】

# 三、渗滤液分析 (Leachate analysis)

# 2．渗滤液的分析项目 Monitoring Items

理化分析和细菌学检验方法 ：色度、总固体、总溶解性固体与总悬浮性固体、硫酸盐、氨态氮、凯氏氮、氯化物、总磷、pH值、BOD、COD、钾、钠、细菌总数、总大肠菌群等。其中细菌总数和大肠菌群是我国特有的监测项目

Color, total solids, total dissolved solids and total suspended solids, sulfate, ammonia nitrogen, Kjeldahl nitrogen, chloride, total phosphorus, pH, BOD, COD, potassium, sodium, total number of bacteria, total coliforms, etc. The total number of bacteria and E.coli are unique monitoring items in China。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 64】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 64】

# 4.5 有害物质的毒理学研究方法

# (Methods for toxicology of hazardous substances)

环境是复杂体系，污染物种类繁多，含量多变，各污染因素之间存在拮抗、相加、相乘等作用 The environment is complicated with a wide variety of pollutants in various contents. There are antagonistic, additive, and multiplying effects among various pollution factors.

环境综合质量很难以各污染物个别影响来评价 It is difficult to evaluate the comprehensive quality of the environment by the individual impact of each pollutant.

利用生物在该环境中的反应，确定环境的综合质量，是理想和重要的手段 It is an ideal and important means to determine the overall quality of the environment by using the response of the organisms in the environment.

环境毒理学研究方法：通过用实验动物对污染物进行毒性试验，确定污染物毒性和剂量的关系，找出毒性作用和阈剂量(阈浓度) (Environmental toxicology: Determine the toxicity and threshold dose (threshold concentration) by testing the toxicity of pollutants to experimental animals.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 65】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 65】

# 一、实验动物的选择及毒性试验分类

# (Selection of experimental animals and classification of toxicity tests)

# （一）实验动物的选择(Selection of experimental animals)

- ❖常用的动物有小鼠、大鼠、兔、豚鼠、猫、狗和猴等 (Commonly used animals: mice, rats, rabbits, guinea pigs, cats, dogs and monkeys)
- ❖鱼类有鲢鱼、草鱼和金鱼等 (salmon, grass carp and goldfish)
- ❖不同动物对毒性反应不一致 (Different animals have inconsistent toxicity)
- ❖要判断某种物质的环境最高允许浓度，除了毒性外，还要考试感官性状、稳定性、及自净过程等因素 (To determine the maximum allowable concentration of a substance in the environment, it is also necessary to examine the sensory traits, stability, and self-purification process besides toxicity)
- ❖根据实验动物的毒性试验所得到的毒性大小、安全浓度和半数致死浓度等数据不能直接推断到人体，还要进行流行病学调查研究才能反映人体受影响情况 (Data such as toxicity, safe concentration and median lethal concentration obtained from the toxicity test of experimental animals cannot be directly inferred to the human body, and epidemiological investigations are required to reflect the situation for human beings)。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 66】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 66】

# （二）毒性试验分类(classification of toxicity tests)

- # ❖急性毒性试验 (Acute toxicity test)

一次（或几次）投给实验动物较大剂量的化合物，观察在短期内（一般24小时到2周以内）中毒反应 (A large dose of the compound was administered to the experimental animals once (or several times) and the toxic reaction was observed in a short period of time (typically within 24 hours to 2 weeks))
- # ❖亚急性毒性试验 (Subacute toxicity test)

一般用半致死剂量的1/5~1/20，每天投毒，连续半个月到3个月左右。了解毒物是否有积累作用和耐受性 (Generally, 1/5~1/20 of the semi-lethal dose is administered daily, and last for half a month to 3 months. Whether the poison can be accumulated or the tolerance can be generated)
- # ❖慢性毒性试验(Chronic toxicity test)

用较低剂量进行3个月到1年的投毒，观察病理、生理、生化反应以及寻找中毒诊断指标，并为制定最大允许浓度提供科学依据 (Use a low dose continuously for 3 months to 1 year. Observe pathological, physiological, biochemical reactions and find diagnostic indicators for poisoning, and provide a scientific basis for the development of the maximum allowable concentration)。
- # ❖终生性毒性试验 (Lifetime toxicity test)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 67】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 67】

# （三）污染物的毒性作用剂量(Toxic dose of pollutants)

# 污染物的毒性和剂量关系

| 无害剂量                   | 中毒剂量         | 致死剂量          |
| ---------------------- | ------------ | ------------- |
| (Harmless dose)        | (Toxic dose) | (Lethal dose) |
| (Maximum safe dose)    |              |               |
| (Absolute lethal dose) |              |               |
| (Minimum lethal dose)  |              |               |
| (Maximum tolerance)    |              |               |
| 最 半 (Half lethal dose) |              |               |
| 最                      | 大            | 最 数 绝         |
| 大                      | 耐            | 小 致 对         |
| 安                      | 受            | 致 死 致         |
| 全                      | 量            | 死 量 死         |
|                        | 量            |               |

半数致死量（浓度）是评价毒性的主要指标之一 (The LD₅₀ (LC₅₀) median lethal dose (concentration) is one of the main indicators to evaluate the toxicity)

计算方法很多，最简单的：曲线法 (There are many calculation methods, the simplest: curve method)

横坐标：投毒剂量，纵坐标：死亡率 (X-axis: dose of poison; Y-axis: mortality)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 68】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 4 · Lecture 9 · Slide 68】

# 某化合物小鼠一次灌胃死亡情况

# (A case of death of a compound in mice)

| 剂量(dose)/(mg·L⁻¹)      | 5  | 15 | 25 | 35 | 45 | 55 | 65  |
| ---------------------- | -- | -- | -- | -- | -- | -- | --- |
| 动物数(Number of animals) | 10 | 10 | 10 | 10 | 10 | 10 | 10  |
| 存活数(Survival number)   | 10 | 9  | 8  | 6  | 3  | 1  | 0   |
| 死亡数(Number of deaths)  | 0  | 1  | 2  | 4  | 7  | 9  | 10  |
| 死亡率(mortality rate)/ ％ | 0  | 10 | 20 | 40 | 70 | 90 | 100 |

# 急性毒性分级表

# (Acute toxicity rating table)

| 等级 | 名称   | 小鼠一次口服的半数致死量(Half-lethal dose of mice once orally)/(mg·kg⁻¹) | 小鼠一次吸入2小时的半数致死量(Half-lethal dose of mice inhaled for 2 hours)/(mL·m⁻³) | 兔子一次皮肤涂毒的半数致死量(Half-lethal dose of rabbit skin poisoning)/(mg·kg⁻¹) |
| -- | ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1  | 剧毒   | <10                                                          | <50                                                                    | <10                                                                 |
| 2  | 高毒   | 11～100                                                       | 51～500                                                                 | 10～50                                                               |
| 3  | 中等毒性 | 101～1000                                                     | 501～5000                                                               | 51～500                                                              |
| 4  | 低毒   | 1001～10000                                                   | 5001～50000                                                             | 501～5000                                                            |
| 5  | 微毒   | >10000                                                       | >50000                                                                 | >5000                                                               |

# 神秘的先秦时代

北京市中山公园，天安门西有个社稷坛，内坛面上铺五色土，分别为中黄、东青、南红、西白、北黑，以五行学说中的五色对应五方，象征“普天之下皆为王土”。五色土象征着我们广博的大中华，在社稷坛的东边是青土，代表着东边的大海；西边是白土，代表西部白色的沙；南边是红土，预示南方的红土地；北边是黑土，象征北部的黑土地；而中间的黄土，就是黄土高原的寓意。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 3】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 3】

# 土壤

# 土壤环境质量

土壤污染状况详查结果显示，全国农用地土壤环境状况总体稳定，影响农用地土壤环境质量的主要污染物是重金属，其中镉为首要污染物。完成《土壤污染防治行动计划》确定的受污染耕地安全利用率达到90%左右和污染地块安全利用率达到90%以上的目标。

# 水土流失

2019年，全国水土流失面积271.08万平方千米，与2018年相比，减少2.61万平方千米。其中，水力侵蚀面积113.47万平方千米，风力侵蚀面积157.61万平方千米。按侵蚀强度分，轻度、中度、强烈、极强烈和剧烈侵蚀面积分别占全国水土流失总面积的62.92%、17.10%、7.55%、5.89%和6.54%。

# 耕地质量

截至2019年底，全国耕地质量平均等级为4.76等。其中，一至三等、四至六等和七至十等耕地面积分别占耕地总面积的31.24%、46.81%和21.95%。

# 全国土地荒漠化和沙化情况

根据第五次全国荒漠化和沙化监测结果，全国荒漠化土地面积为261.16万平方千米，沙化土地面积为172.12万平方千米。根据岩溶地区第三次石漠化监测结果，全国岩溶地区现有石漠化土地面积10.07万平方千米。

依据《耕地质量等级》（GB/T33469—2016）评价，耕地质量划分为十个等级，一等地耕地质量最好，十等地耕地质量最差。一等至三等、四等至六等、七等至十等分别划分为高等地、中等地、低等地。截至本公报发布时，2019年耕地质量为最新数据。

截至本公报发布时，2019年水土流失监测结果为最新数据。截止本公报发布时，第五次全国荒漠化和沙化监测结果、第三次石漠化监测结果均为最新数据。

# 5.1 土壤基本知识

# Basic knowledge about soil

# 一、土壤组成

# Soil constituents

土壤是指陆地地表具有肥力并能生长植物的疏松表层。它介于大气圈、岩石圈、水圈和生物圈之间，厚度一般在2m左右，是环境中特有的组成部分。

Soil can be defined as the organic and inorganic materials on the surface of the earth that provide the medium for plant growth. Among atmosphere, lithosphere, hydrosphere and biosphere. The thickness is generally around 2m, and it is a unique component of the environment.

土壤是由矿物质、动植物残体腐解产生的有机物质、土壤生物、水分和空气等固、液、气三相组成的。

The soil is composed of three phases: solid, liquid and gas, including minerals, organic matters, soil organisms, water and air.

# (一) 土壤矿物质 Minerals

土壤矿物质是由岩石经风化而来的，一般占土壤固体部分质量的95％～98％。矿物质直接影响土壤性质，又是植物矿质养分的主要来源，故同土壤肥力有密切关系。按成因分为原生和次生矿物质。Soil minerals are derived from weathering of rocks and generally account for 95% to 98% of the mass of soil solids. Minerals affect soil properties directly and are the main source of mineral nutrients for plants. Minerals can be classified as primary and secondary minerals.

# 1. 土壤矿物质的组成 Composition of soil minerals

- (1) 原生矿物质 primary minerals：岩石经过物理风化作用被破碎形成的碎屑，其原来的化学组成没有改变：硅酸盐类、氧化物类、硫化物类、磷酸盐类等。The debris formed by the physical weathering of the rock with unchanged chemical composition: silicates, oxides, sulfides, phosphates, etc.
- (2) 次生矿物质 secondary minerals：原生矿物质经过化学风化后形成的新矿物，其化学组成和晶体结构均有所改变：简单盐类、三氧化物类、次生铝硅酸盐类等。New minerals formed by chemical weathering of the primary minerals. The chemical composition and crystal structure have changed: salts, trioxides, secondary aluminosilicates, etc.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 11】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 11】

# (一) 土壤矿物质 Minerals

# 2. 土壤化学组成 Chemical composition

氧、硅、铝、铁、钙、钠、钾、镁八大元素含量约占96％以上，与岩石中各元素的含量相似。Eight elements (oxygen, silicon, aluminum, iron, calcium, sodium, potassium and magnesium) account for more than 96%, similar to the content of each element in the rock.

# 3. 土壤机械组成 Mechanical composition

指不同大小颗粒(石块、石砾、沙砾、粉粒、黏粒) 的相对含量。不同粒径的矿物质颗粒的成分和物理化学性质有很大差异，如对污染物的吸附、解吸和迁移、转化能力，有效含水量及保水、保温能力等。Refers to the relative content of particles with different sizes (stone, gravel, grit, silt, clay particles). The composition and physicochemical properties of minerals vary greatly among particles with different particle sizes, e.g.: as adsorption, desorption, migration and conversion capacity to pollutants, effective water content, water retention and thermal insulation capacity.

我国土壤质地分类参见表5.1.1；国际制土壤质地分类见表5.1.2。The soil texture classification in China is shown in Table 5.1.1; the international soil texture classification is shown in Table 5.1.2.

# (二) 土壤有机质

是土壤中有机化合物的总称

Soil organic matter: a general term for organic compounds in soil

- 由进入土壤的植物、动物、微生物残体及施入土壤的有机肥料经分解转化逐渐形成，通常可分为非腐殖物质和腐殖物质两类；是土壤形成的重要基础，与土壤矿物质共同构成土壤的固相部分，占5%。
- Soil organic matter is formed by the decomposition of plants, animals, microbial residues and organic fertilizers applied to the soil. It can be divided into non-humus and humus; it is important for soil formation. The soil organic matter accounts for 5% of the soil mass.
- 土壤有机质中含有大量营养元素，分解后可提供植物生长发育的需要，是植物养分的重要来源。有机质腐解后形成的腐殖质，能把土粒粘结成团粒结构。这种结构保水、保肥能力强，类似储存水肥的小仓库，随时供给植物吸收利用。有机质是微生物的食物，土壤有机质丰富而其他条件又适宜时，就能促进微生物的旺盛活动。
- Soil organic matter contains a lot of nutrients, which can meet the needs of plants growth and development. The humus formed after the decomposition of organic matter can bond the soil particles into agglomerate structure. This structure has strong water retention and fertilizer retention capacity, and provide water and fertilizer for plant absorption and utilization. Organic matter is the food of microorganisms. When the soil is rich in organic matter under suitable conditions, it can promote the vigorous activity of microorganisms.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 15】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 15】

# (二) 土壤有机质

是土壤中有机化合物的总称

# Soil organic matter

a general term for organic compounds in soil

- # 非腐殖物质

包括糖类化合物 (如淀粉、纤维素等)、含氮有机合物及有机磷和有机硫化合物。10-15%.
- # Non-humus

including saccharides (such as starch, cellulose, etc.), nitrogen-containing organic compounds, and organophosphorus and organic sulfur compounds. 10-15%.
- # 腐殖物质

是植物残体中稳定性较大的木质素及其类似物在微生物作用下，部分被氧化形成的一类特殊的高分子聚合物，具有芳环结构，苯环周围连有多种官能团，如羧基、羟基、甲氧基及氨基等，使之具有表面吸附、离子交换、络合、缓冲、氧化还原作用及生理活性等性能。
- # Humus

A special high molecular polymer formed by oxidation of lignin and its analogues under the action of microorganisms. It has an aromatic ring structure, and there are many functional groups connected to the benzene ring, such as carboxyl, hydroxyl, methoxy and amino groups. So it has properties such as surface adsorption, ion exchange, complexation, buffering, redox and physiological activity.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 16】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 16】

# (三) 土壤生物 Soil organisms

- 土壤中生活的微生物 (细菌、真菌、放线菌、藻类等) 及动物(原生动物、蚯蚓、线虫类等) 对进入土壤的有机污染物的降解及无机污染物(如重金属)的形态转化起着主导作用，是土壤净化功能的主要贡献者。
- Microorganisms (bacteria, fungi, actinomycetes, algae, etc.) and animals (protozoa, earthworms, nematodes, etc.) living in the soil play leading roles in the degradation of organic pollutants and the transformation of inorganic pollutants (such as heavy metals) in the soil. They are the main contributors to the soil purification function.

蚯蚓 earthworm

放线菌 actinomycetes

细菌 bacteria

线虫 nematodes

# (四) 土壤溶液

土壤溶液是土壤水分及其所含溶质的总称，存在于土壤缝隙中。溶有土壤中可溶成分的稀溶液，来源主要有：大气降水、降雪、地表径流、灌溉、地下水。

Soil solution is the general term of soil water and its solutes existing in the soil crevices. The main sources of soil solution are precipitation, snowfall, surface runoff, irrigation and groundwater.

# (五) 土壤空气 Soil air

- 土壤空气指存在于未被水分占据的土壤孔隙中气体的总称。Soil air refers to the gases present in the pores of soil that are not occupied by water.
- 来源：来源于大气、生物化学反应和化学反应产生的气体(如甲烷、硫化氢、氢气、氮氧化物、二氧化碳等)。Source: Gases are derived from the atmosphere, biochemical reactions, and chemical reactions (such as methane, hydrogen sulfide, hydrogen, nitrogen oxides, carbon dioxide, etc.).
- 影响因素：土壤水分、生物活动、土壤深度、土壤湿度、土壤酸碱度、季节变化等。Influencing factors: soil moisture, biological activity, soil depth, soil pH, seasonal changes, etc.
- 积水的土壤通气不良，导致对植物的危害。Excessive water accumulation in the soil can lead to poor ventilation and cause damage to plants.

# 二、土壤的基本性质 Basic properties

# (一) 吸附性 Adsorption

- 与土壤中存在的胶体物质密切相关。Closely related to the presence of colloidal substances in the soil.
- 土壤胶体（具有巨大的比表面积、胶粒带电、分散在水中时产生双电层）
Soil colloid (a large specific surface area, charged colloidal particles, and an electric double layer when dispersed in water)
- - ✓ 无机胶体 Inorganic colloid：粘土矿物、铁铝硅等水合氧化物 hydrated oxides such as clay minerals, iron aluminum silicon
- ✓ 有机胶体 Organic colloid：腐殖质及少量生物活动产生的有机物 humus and organic matter produced by a small amount of biological activity
- ✓ 有机－无机复合胶体 organic-inorganic composite colloid

对有机污染物（如有机磷和有机氯农药）和无机污染物（如Hg⁺、Pb²⁺、Cu²⁺、Cd²⁺等重金属离子）有较强的吸附能力或离子交换吸附能力。
Strong adsorption capacity or ion exchange adsorption capacity for organic pollutants (organic phosphorus and organochlorine pesticides) and inorganic pollutants (heavy metal ions such as Hg⁺、Pb²⁺、Cu²⁺、Cd²⁺).

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 20】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 20】

# 二、土壤的基本性质 Basic properties

# (二) 酸碱性 Acidity and alkalinity

土壤的酸碱度可以划分为九级 nine grades.：

- pH&#x3C;4.5：极强酸性土(Extremely strong acid soil)；
- pH=4.5～5.5：强酸性土(Strong acid soil)；
- pH>5.5～6.0：酸性土(Acid soil)；
- pH>6.0～6.5：弱酸性土(Weakly acidic soil)；
- pH>6.5～7.0：中性土(Neutral soil)；
- pH>7.0～7.5：弱碱性土(Weak alkaline soil)；
- pH>7.5～8.5：碱性土(Alkaline soil)；
- pH>8.5～9.5：强碱性土(Strong alkaline soil)；
- pH>9.5：极强碱性土(Extremely alkaline soil)。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 21】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 21】

# 二、土壤的基本性质 Basic properties

# (二) 酸碱性 Acidity and alkalinity

- 中国土壤的pH大多在4.5～8.5范围内，并呈东南酸西北碱的规律。土壤的酸碱性直接或间接影响污染物在土壤中的迁移转化。
- Soil pH in China is mostly in the range of 4.5-8.5, and is acid in the southeast but alkali in the northwest. Soil acidity and alkalinity can affect the migration and transformation of pollutants in the soil.
- 活性酸度：有效酸度，土壤溶液中游离氢离子浓度
- Active acidity: effective acidity, concentration of the free hydrogen ions in the soil solution
- 潜性酸度：土壤胶体吸附的可交换氢离子和铝离子交换作用后产生的酸度
- Potential acidity: The acidity generated after the ion exchange of exchangeable hydrogen and aluminum ions adsorbed by soil colloids

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 22】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 22】

# 二、土壤的基本性质 Basic properties

# (三) 氧化-还原性 Oxidation-reduction

- 因土壤中含有氧化性和还原性无机物质和有机物质，使其具有氧化性和还原性，可以用氧化还原电位(Eh)来衡量。The soil contains oxidizing and reducing inorganic and organic substances, making it has oxidizing and reducing properties, which can be measured by the oxidation-reduction potential (Eh).
- Eh>300mV：氧化体系起主导作用，土壤处于氧化状态。
- Eh>300mV: The oxidation system plays a leading role and the soil is in an oxidation state.
- Eh&#x3C;300mV：还原体系起主导作用，土壤处于还原状态。
- Eh&#x3C;300mV: The reduction system plays a leading role and the soil is in a reduction state.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 23】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 23】

# 三、土壤背景值 Soil Background value

- 判断土壤是否受到污染或污染程度的标准。Criteria for the evaluation of soil contamination
- 土壤背景值又称土壤本底值，它代表一定环境单元中的一个统计量的特征值。The soil background value means the eigenvalue of a statistic in a certain environmental unit.
- 背景值（地质学）：指在各区域正常地质地理条件和地球化学条件下元素在各类自然体中的正常含量。Background value (geology): refers to the normal content of elements in natural bodies under normal geological and geographical conditions and geochemical conditions in each region.
- 环境科学上：指在未受或少受人类影响的情况下，尚未受或少受污染和破坏的土壤中元素的含量。In environmental science: refers to the content of elements in soil that has not been or is less contaminated and destroyed.

※ 不同土壤的本底值相差很大（见下页表5.1.3）

The background values of different soils vary greatly (Table 5.1.3)

# 四、土壤污染

Soil pollution: When the quantity and speed of pollutants entering the soil exceed the capacity and purification speed of the soil, the natural dynamic balance of the soil environment will be destroyed. Changes in the composition, structure and properties of the soil, functional imbalance, quality decline and soil environmental pollution.

# 天然污染源

矿物风化后自然扩散、火山灰

# 人为污染源

农药、化肥、污水灌溉、污泥（垃圾、工业废渣）施肥

pesticide, fertilizer, sewage irrigation, sludge fertilization

| Chemical pollutant | 重金属 |
| ------------------ | --- |
| 化学污染物              | 硫化物 |
|                    | 氟化物 |
|                    | 农药  |

# 污染物种类

| Type of pollutant | 生物类污染物：病原微生物          |
| ----------------- | --------------------- |
|                   | Biological pollutant  |
|                   | 放射性污染物：⁹⁰锶、¹³⁷铯       |
|                   | Strontium, cesium     |
|                   | Radioactive pollutant |

# 他国土壤安全经验

# 美国：“超级基金”法案

拉夫运河位于美国东北部，20世纪40年代被废弃成工业废弃物垃圾填埋场，随着，化学废弃物渗入土壤。居住在周边地区的居民随之出现各种怪病。经调查，在土壤中发现11种致癌物。拉夫运河的污染物清理工作直到2004年才宣告完成，用时24年，耗资4亿多美元。

1980年，美国通过法案，批准设立污染场地管理与修复基金，即“超级基金”法案，只要认定污染，就可以无限期对污染企业进行追责，并被征收高额罚金。

（20世纪90年代开始，美国用于污染土壤修复方面的投资已有几千亿美元，但至今仍有50万幅地块需要治理，美国超级基金在投资污染治理方面，存在投入产出比不高的情况，中国应当避免。）

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 30】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 30】

# 他国土壤安全经验

# 德国：摸清家底、区别对待

德国的土壤保护工作做得比较深入细致。首先，全面开展土壤监测，根据土地的用途，对土壤进行监测，随时了解土壤特性的信息变化，评估治理措施是否有效。其次，对全国有污染嫌疑的地块进行排查、筛选，对重点污染地块进行详细调查，开展土壤修复研究，制定技术方案并实施。第三，建立污染场地数据库，通过数据库，进行有效的动态管理。

# 澳大利亚：改造后建成公园

澳大利亚将很多受污染严重的土地改造后建成了一些大型的森林公园、湖滨公园，在这些公园中，被污染的土壤并没有被挖出来运走，而是与有机物重新结合起来加以利用，通过自然法则，慢慢实现土壤净化。

# 五、土壤质量标准

Environmental quality standard for soils

规定了土壤中污染物的最高允许浓度或范围 ， 是判断土壤质量的依据 。 The maximum allowable concentration or range of pollutants in the soil, the criteria for soil quality evaluation.

我国颁布的这类标准有

- 土壤环境质量标准(GB 15618-1995) Environmental quality standard for soils
- 无公害农产品蔬菜产地土壤环境质量指标(GB/T18407-2001) Soil Environmental Quality Index in vegetables-producing Areas of Non-polluted Agricultural Products
- 无公害农产品茶叶产地土壤环境质量指标(NY5020-2001) Soil Environmental Quality Index in Tea-producing Areas of Non-polluted Agricultural Products
- 省(市) 制定的此类地方标准Local standards

Monitoring purpose

Sampling point layout

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 35】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 35】

# 一、监测目的Monitoring purpose

# 1.土壤质量现状监测 Monitoring of soil quality status

监测土壤质量标准要求测定的项目，判断土壤是否被污染及污染水平，并预测其发展变化趋势。Monitoring the items required by soil quality standards, evaluating soil pollution level, predicting the tendency.

《土壤环境质量标准》将土壤环境分为三类，分别执行一、二、三级标准three levels

# 2.土壤污染事故监测Monitoring of Soil Pollution Accidents

调查分析引起土壤污染的主要污染物，确定污染的来源、范围和程度，为行政主管部门采取对策提供科学依据。Investigate and analyze the main pollutants, determine the source and extent of pollution, and provide a scientific basis for the administrative authorities.

# 图5.2.1  汉中“5·23”剧毒物品对土壤的渗漏污染现场照片

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 36】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 36】

# 3. 污染物土地处理的动态监测

Dynamic monitoring of remediated land

在进行污水、污泥土地利用、固体废弃物的土地处理过程中，对残留的污染物进行定点长期动态监测，既能充分利用土地的净化能力，又可防止土壤污染。In the land treatment process of sewage, sludge and solid waste, fixed-point long-term dynamic monitoring of residual pollutants can fully utilize the purification ability of the land and prevent soil pollution.

# 4. 土壤背景值调查

Investigation of Soil Background Value

通过分析测定土壤中某些元素的含量，确定这些元素的背景值水平和变化。By analyzing and determining the contents of some elements in soil, the background values and changes of these elements are determined.

# 二、资料的收集 Data collection

- 自然环境方面的资料：土壤类型、植被、区域土壤元素背景值、土地利用、水土流失、自然灾害、水系、地下水、地质、地形地貌、气象等。

Natural environment: soil type, vegetation, regional soil element background value, land use, water and soil loss, natural disasters, water system, groundwater, geology, topography, meteorology, etc.
- 社会环境方面的资料：工农业生产布局、工业污染源种类及分布、污染物种类及排放途径和排放量、农药和化肥使用状况、污水灌溉及污泥施用状况、人口分布、地方病等。

Social environment: distribution of industrial and agricultural production, types and distribution of industrial pollution sources, types and discharge routes of pollutants, utilization of pesticides and fertilizers, sewage irrigation and sludge application, population distribution, endemic diseases, etc.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 38】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 38】

# 三、监测项目 Monitoring items

- 根据监测项目的确定 According to the monitoring items
- 背景值要求测定土壤中各种元素的含量； The background value of every element is needed.
- 污染事故监测仅测定可能造成土壤污染的项目； Pollution accident monitoring only measures items that may cause soil pollution.
- 土壤质量监测测定影响自然生态和植物正常生长及危害人体健康的项目。 Soil quality monitoring analyzes the items affecting natural ecology and normal plant growth as well as those endangering human health.

> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 39】

# 第类

汞、铅、镉，DDT及其代谢产物与分解产物，多氯联苯(PCB)

石油产品，DDT以外的长效性有机氯

# 坐类

四氯化碳醋酸衍生物、氯化脂肪族，砷、锌、硒、铬、镍、锰、钒，有机磷化合物及其它活性物质（抗菌素、激素、致畸性物质、催畸性物质和诱变物质)等

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 40】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 40】

# 金属化合物

镉（Cd）、铬（Cd）、铜（Cu）、

汞（Hg）、铅（Pb）、锌（Zn）

# 非金属无机化合物

砷（As）、氰化物、氟化物、硫化物等

# 有机化合物

苯并（a）花、三氯乙醛、油类、

# 无机化合物

挥发酚、DDT、六六六等

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 41】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 41】

# 四、采样点的布设 Sampling point

# (一) 布设原则 Principles

1. 合理地划分采样单元 Divide the sampling unit reasonably.
2. 对于土壤污染监测，哪里有污染就在哪里布点。For monitoring of soil pollution, layout the points where there is pollution.
3. 采样点不能设在田边、沟边、路边、肥堆边及水土流失严重或表层土被破坏处。Sampling points should not locate at fields, ditches, roadsides, the edge of the compost and places where soil erosion is serious or topsoil is destroyed.

Video 1

Video 2

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 42】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 42】

# (二) 采样点数量 Number of sampling points

- 根据监测目的、区域范围大小及其环境状况等因素确定。
- According to the purpose of monitoring, the size of the area and the environmental conditions.
- 一般每个采样单元最少设3个采样点。
- Generally, there are at least 3 sampling points per sampling unit.
- 单个采样单元内采样点数可按下式估算 The number of sampling points in a single sampling unit can be estimated as below:

n = ( s                 t )2 / d

式中：

- n——每个采样单元布设的最少采样点数；
Minimum number of samples per sample unit
- s——样本相对标准偏差，即变异系数；
Relative standard deviation of sample, coefficient of variation
- t——置信因子，当置信水平为95%时，t值为1.96；
Confidence factor, when the confidence level is 95%, the t is 1.96
- d——允许偏差，当规定抽样精度不低于80%时，d取0.2。
Allowable deviation, when the required sampling accuracy is not less than 80%, d is 0.2.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 43】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 43】

# (三) 采样点布设方法 Sampling point layout method

- # 1. 对角线布点法 Diagonal Layout Point

适用于面积较小、地势平坦的污水灌溉或污染河水灌溉的地形端正的田块。由田块的进水口向对角引一直线，将对角线划分为若干等分(一般3-5等分)，在每等分的中点处采样。Applicable to regular and small-scale flat field irrigated with sewage or polluted river water. Draw a diagonal line from the water inlet, and divide the line into several equal parts and samples are taken at the midpoint of each aliquot.
- # 2. 梅花形布点法 Plum Blossom-shaped Layout Point

适用于面积较小、地势平坦、土壤物质和污染程度较均匀的地块。中心点设在两对角线相交处，一般设5-10个采样点。Suitable for flat and small area, which is equally polluted. The center point is located at the intersection of two diagonal lines, generally 5-10 sampling points.
- # 3. 棋盘式布点法 Checkerboard shaped Layout Point

适用于中等面积、地势平坦、地形完整开阔的地块，一般设10个以上分点。该法也适用于受固体废物污染的土壤，应设20个以上分点。Applicable to medium area, flat and open terrain, generally with more than 10 points. Also applicable to soil contaminated by solid waste, and more than 20 points.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 44】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 44】

# 4. 蛇形布点法 Serpentine Layout Point

适用于面积较大、地势不很平坦、土壤不够均匀的田块。布设采样点数目较多。For large area, uneven terrain and uneven soil, always with many sampling points.

# 5. 放射状布点法 Radial Layout Point

适用于大气污染型土壤。以大气污染源为中心，向周围画射线，在射线上布设采样点。在主导风向的下风适当增加采样点之间的距离和采样点数量。For air-polluted soil. Air pollution source as the center, draw the radiation, and lay sampling points on the radiation. Increase the distance between sampling points and the number of sampling points in the downwind of the dominant wind direction.

# 6. 网格布点法 Grid Layout Point

适用于地形平缓的地块。将地块划分成若干均匀网状方格，采样点设在两条直线的交点处或方格的中心。农用化学物质污染型土壤、土壤背景值调查常用这种方法. For relatively flat area, divide the area into uniform mesh squares, and the sampling points are located at the intersection of two straight lines or at the center of the squares. Commonly used in investigating background values of soil and soil polluted by agricultural chemicals.

对于综合污染型土壤，还可以采用两种以上布点方法相结合的方法。For comprehensive polluted soils, a combination of two or more methods can be used.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 45】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 45】

# 五、监测方法Monitoring method

- 包括土壤样品预处理方法和分析测定方法。Including soil sample pretreatment method and analytical method
- 分析测定常用原子吸收光谱法、分光光度法、原子荧光法、气相色谱法、电化学分析法及化学分析法等。此外，电感耦合等离子体原子发射光谱(ICP-OES)、X射线荧光光谱法、中子活化法、液相色谱法及气相色谱-质谱(ICP-MS)等近代分析方法在土壤检测中也已得到应用。atomic absorption spectrometry, spectrophotometry, atomic fluorescence spectrometry, gas chromatography, electrochemical analysis and chemical analysis methods. In addition, modern analytical methods such as inductively coupled plasma atomic emission spectrometry (ICP-OES), X-ray fluorescence spectrometry, neutron activation method, liquid chromatography and gas chromatography-mass spectrometry (ICP-MS) have also been applied in soil detection.
- 选择分析方法的原则： Principle of choosing an analytical method
1. 标准方法； Standard method
2. 权威部门规定或推荐的方法; The method prescribed or recommended by the authority;
3. 自选等效方法。 Optional equivalent method

运用评价参数进行单项污染物污染状况评价、区域综合污染状况评价、划定土壤质量等级. Use different parameters to evaluate the environmental pollution by single pollutant or for regional comprehensive pollution, and define grade of the soil quality.

# 评价方法 Evaluation method

一般以单项污染指数为主，但是当区域内土壤质量作为一个整体与外区域土壤质量比较时，或一个区域内土壤质量在不同历史阶段比较时，应用综合污染指数评价。

Generally rely on the single pollution index, but when the soil quality in a region is compared with that in other regions, or when the soil quality in a region is compared at different historical stages, the comprehensive pollution index will be used to evaluate the soil quality.

# 表5.2.2 土壤污染分级标准

# Standard for classification of soil pollution

| 土壤级别 | 综合污染指数（P综）  |      | 污染等级          | 污染水平             |
| ---- | ----------- | ---- | ------------- | ---------------- |
| 1    | P综≤0.7      |      | 安全            | 清洁               |
| 2    | 0.7\<P综≤1.0 |      | 警戒限           | 尚清洁              |
| 3    | 1.0\<P综≤2.0 |      | 轻污染           | 土壤污染超过背景值，作物开始污染 |
|      |             |      | 中污染           | 土壤、作物均受到中度污染     |
| 4    | 2.0\<P综≤3.0 |      | 重污染           | 土壤、作物受污染已相当严重    |
| 5    | P综>3.0      | 严重污染 | 土壤和作物已经受到严重污染 |                  |

# 土壤样品的采集和处理

土壤样品的采集和处理是土壤分析工作的一个重要环节，采集有代表性的样品，是测定结果能如实反映土壤环境状况的先决条件。实验室工作者只能对来样的分析结果负责，如果送来的样品不符合要求，那么任何精密仪器和熟练的分析技术都将毫无意义。因此，分析结果能否说明问题，关键在于样品的采集和处理。

# The collection and processing of soil samples

is important. The collection of representative samples is a prerequisite for the determination of soil environmental conditions. Laboratory workers can only be responsible for the results of the sample analysis. If the samples do not meet the requirements, then any precision instruments and skilled analytical techniques will be meaningless. Therefore, the reasonability of the analysis results is depended by the collection and processing of samples.

---

# 一、土壤样品的采集

# ⮚ (一) 土壤样品的类型、采样深度及采样量

# Type, sampling depth and sampling amount

# 1. 混合样品

- 一般了解土壤污染状况时采集混合样品：将一个采样单元内各采样分点采集的土样混合均匀制成 。 需要用四分法弃取 。 For the analysis of soil contamination status, the mixed samples should be collected at different sampling points of each sampling unit and mixed evenly.
- 对种植一般农作物的耕地，只需采集0～20 cm耕作层土壤；对于种植果林类农作物的耕地，采集0～60cm耕作层土壤。For land planted with general crops, only 0-20 cm tillage layer soil should be collected; For land planted with fruit and forest crops, 0-60 cm tillage layer soil should be collected.

# 土壤剖面采样规范

剖面规格一般为长1.5m、宽0.8m、深1.0m，每个剖面采集A、B、C三层土样。过渡层（AB、BC）一般不采样。

当地下水位较高时，挖至地下水出露时止。现场记录实际采样深度，如0-20、50-65、80-100 cm。在各层次典型中心部位自下而上采样，切忌混淆层次、混合采样。

| 典型深度 (cm) | 层次 |
| --------- | -- |
| 0         | A  |
| 25        | AB |
| 50        | B  |
| 70        | BC |
| 100       | C  |

在山地土壤土层薄的地区，B层发育不完整时，只采A、C层样。

干旱地区剖面发育不完整的土壤，采集表层（0～20cm）、中土层（50cm）和底土层（100cm）附近的样品。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 56】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 56】

# （二）采样时间和频率Sampling time and frequency

可随时采集样品进行测定 Samples can be collected at any time.

如需同时掌握作物污染情况，则土壤在农作物收获期采样测定，必测项目一年测定一次，其他项目3 ～ 5 年测定一次。

If it is necessary to test the contamination of crops, the soil should be sampled and determined in the harvest period of crops, once a year for the required items and once every 3-5 years for the other items.

# （三）采样量及注意事项Sample amount and precautions

- （1）填写土壤样品标签、采样记录、样品登记表。1份放入样品袋内，1份扎在袋口 Fill in soil sample label, sampling record and sample registration form. Put one in the sample bag and one in the mouth of the bag.
- （2）测定重金属的样品，尽量用竹铲、竹片直接采集样品。Samples for heavy metals should be collected directly with bamboo shovels and slices as far as possible.

|   | 经纬度（）： | 东经：  |      | 北纬：   |      |   |
|   | 采样深度：  |      | cm   | 土壤类型： |      |   |

# 二、样品加工与管理 Sample Processing and Management

# (一) 样品加工处理Sample processing

- 制成满足分析要求的土壤样品; Prepare soil samples to meet the requirements of analysis.
- 测定不稳定的项目用新鲜土样(如游离挥发酚、NH₃-N、NO₃－-N、Fe²⁺); Fresh soil samples are needed for the unstable items such as free volatile phenols, NH₃-N、NO₃－-N、Fe²⁺.
- 测定多数稳定项目用风干土样。程序是：风干 磨细 过筛 混合 分装
- Use air-dried soil samples to determine stable items.
- The procedure is: air drying, grinding, sieving, mixing, dispensing

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 58】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 58】

# 土样风干 Air-dried soil samples

野外采集样品在塑料薄膜上或达半干状态运到实验室瓷盘内风干

铺成薄层，阴凉处风干压碎，除石块残根等杂物

1. Samples collected in the field are transported to the laboratory;
2. Air-dried to semi-dry state on plastic film or ceramic dishes;
3. Crushed to remove debris and other impurities;
4. Pave into thin layers, air-dried in shady places;

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 59】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 59】

# 磨碎与过筛Grinding and Screening

取风干样品100——200g，放在木板上用圆木棍辗碎，经反复处理使土样全部通过2mm孔径的筛子，将土样混均储于广口瓶内，作为土壤颗粒分析及物理性质测定。1927年国际土壤学会规定通过2mm孔径的土壤用作物理分析，通过1mm或0.5mm孔径的土壤用作化学分析。

# PYS-130型盘式研磨筛分器

# 样品保存 Sample storage

土壤样品管理包括土样加工处理、分装、分发过程中的管理和土样入库保存管理。Soil sample management includes the management of soil sample processing, packing, distribution and storage.

建立严格的管理制度和岗位责任制，按照规定的方法和程序工作，认真按要求做好各项记录。Strict management system and post responsibility system should be established, work in accordance with the prescribed methods and procedures, and make good records according to the requirements.

风干土样存于阴凉、干燥的样品库内；新鲜土壤样品，放在玻璃瓶中，置于低于4℃的冰箱内存放，保存半个月。Air-dried soil samples should be stored in a cool, dry sample bank; fresh soil samples should be stored in glass bottles in a refrigerator below 4 ℃ for half a month.

---

# 一、土壤样品分解

Decomposition of soil samples

Purification and concentration

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 62】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 62】

土壤样品组分复杂，污染组分含量低，并处于固体状态。在测定之前，往往需要处理成液体状态和将欲测组分转变为适合测定方法的形态、浓度，并消除共存组分的干扰。 The soil sample has complex components, low levels of contaminating components, and is in the solid state. Before the determination, it is often necessary to transform the soil into liquid state, and to transform the desired components to forms suitable for the determination, and to eliminate the interference of coexisting components.

# 土壤样品预处理方法

# Pretreatment methods for soil samples:

- 分解法 Decomposition method
- 提取法 Extraction method

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 63】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 63】

# 一、土壤样品分解

# Decomposition of soil samples

- 作用：破坏土壤的矿物晶格和有机质，使待测元素进入试样溶液中
- Function: destroy mineral lattice and organic matter of soil, so that the element to be tested can enter into the sample solution

# (一) 酸分解法

# Acid decomposition

称消解法，是测定土壤中重金属常选用的方法。常用混合酸消解体系，必要时加入氧化剂或还原剂加速消解反应。

Also known as digestion, it is a method commonly used to determine heavy metals in soil. A mixed acid digestion system is commonly used, and the oxidizing agent or reducing agent can be added to accelerate the digestion reaction if necessary.

# 分解土壤常用混合酸体系

# Common mixed acid system for decomposing soil：

- 盐酸-硝酸-氢氟酸-高氯酸
- Hydrochloric acid-nitric acid-hydrofluoric acid-perchloric acid
- 硝酸-氢氟酸-高氯酸
- Nitric acid-hydrofluoric acid-perchloric acid
- 硝酸-硫酸-高氯酸
- Nitric acid-sulfuric acid-perchloric acid
- 硝酸-硫酸-磷酸
- Nitric acid-sulfate-phosphoric acid

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 64】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 64】

# (二) 碱熔分解法 Alkali fusion method

将土壤样品与碱混合，在高温下熔融，使样品分解。

Soil samples are mixed with alkali and then melted at high temperatures to make the samples decompose.

所用器皿：铝坩埚、瓷坩埚、镍坩埚、铂金坩埚

aluminum crucible, porcelain crucible, nickel crucible, platinum crucible.

常用熔剂：碳酸钠、氢氧化钠、过氧化钠、偏硼酸锂

sodium carbonate, sodium hydroxide, sodium peroxide, lithium metaborate

操作要点：称取适量土样于坩埚中，加入适量溶剂，充分混匀，移入马弗炉中高温熔融。熔融温度和时间视所用熔剂而定。如用碳酸钠于900-920 ℃熔融30 min,用过氧化钠于650-700 ℃熔融20-30 min等，熔融后的土样冷却至60-80 ℃，移入(聚四氟乙烯)烧杯中，于电热板上加水和(1+1)盐酸加热浸取中和，酸化镕融物，待大量盐类溶解后，滤去不溶物，滤液定容，供分析测定。

Weigh appropriate amount of soil sample in crucible, add appropriate amount of solvent, mix well and melt in muffle furnace at high temperature. The melting temperature and time depend on the flux used. If sodium carbonate is used, it will melt at 900-920°C for 30 minutes and 20-30 minutes at 650-700°C when using sodium peroxide. The melted soil sample is cooled to 60-80°C and transferred into the beaker of polytetrafluoroethylene (PTFE). Water and (1+1) hydrochloric acid are added into the beaker placed on electric heating plate to neutralize and acidify the melt. After being dissolved, the insoluble matter is filtered out and the filtrate is used for analysis.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 65】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 65】

# (三) 高压釜密闭分解法

Autoclave closed decomposition method

将用水润湿、加入混合酸并摇匀的土样放入密封的聚四氟乙烯坩埚内，置于耐压的不锈钢套筒中，放在烘箱内加热（一般不超过180℃）分解。 The soil sample is wetted with water, added with mixed acid and shaken well, and then is placed in a sealed polytetrafluoroethylene crucible. Place it in a pressure-resistant stainless steel sleeve, and heat it in an oven (generally not exceeding 180℃) for decomposition.

# (四) 微波炉加热分解法

Microwave oven heating decomposition method

将土壤样品和混合酸放入聚四氟乙烯容器中，置于微波炉内加热使试样分解的方法。Soil samples and mixed acids are placed in PTFE containers and heated in microwave ovens for decomposition.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 66】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 66】

# 二、土壤样品提取 Extraction of soil samples

测定土壤中的有机污染物、受热后不稳定的组分以及进行组分形态分析时，需要采用提取方法。提取溶剂常用有机溶剂、水和酸。Extraction methods are needed for the determination of organic pollutants in soil, unstable components after heating and speciation analysis of components. Extraction solvents usually include organic solvents, water and acids.

# (一) 有机污染物的提取 Extraction of Organic Pollutants

测定土壤中的有机污染物，一般用新鲜土样。称取适量土样放入锥形瓶中，放在振荡器上，用振荡提取法提取。对于农药、苯并（a）芘等含量低的污染物，常用索氏提取器提取法。Fresh soil samples are usually used for the determination of organic pollutants in soils. A proper amount of soil sample is weighed and put into a conical bottle, then place it on an oscillator, and extracted by oscillating extraction method. Soxhlet extractor is commonly used for the extraction of low-content pollutants such as pesticides and benzo(a)pyrene.

# (二) 无机污染物的提取 Extraction of Inorganic Pollutants

土壤中易溶无机物组分、有效态组分可用酸或水浸取。Soluble inorganic components in soil can be leached by acid or water.

# 样品的代表性和采样误差的控制

- 土壤的不均一性是造成采样误差的最主要原因。
- Soil heterogeneity is the main cause of sampling errors.
- 土壤是固、气、液三相组成的分散体系，各种外来物进入土壤后流动、迁移、混合较难，所以采集的样品往往具有局限性。一般情况下，采样误差要比分析误差高得多。为保证样品的代表性，必须采取以下两个技术措施控制采样误差。
- Soil is a dispersed system consisting of solid, gas and liquid phases. It is difficult for the substances to flow, migrate and mix after they enter the soil, so the collected samples often have limitations. Generally speaking, the sampling error is much higher than the analysis error. To ensure the representativeness of samples, the following two technical measures must be taken to control sampling errors.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 69】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 69】

# 1. 采样前的准备工作

（1）采样前要进行现场勘察和有关资料的收集，根据土壤类型、肥力等级和地形等因素将研究范围划分为若干个采样单元，每个采样单元的土壤要尽可能均匀一致。Before sampling, field investigation and data collection should be carried out. According to soil type, fertility grade and topography, the research area should be divided into several sampling units. The soil of each sampling unit should be as uniform as possible.

（2）要保证有足够多的采样点，使之能充分代表采样单元的土壤特性。采样点的多少，取决于研究范围的大小，研究对象的复杂程度和试验研究所要求的精密度等因素。采样点设置过少，所采样品的偶然性增加，缺乏足够的代表性；采样点设置过多，则增大了采样的工作量，浪费了人力、物力和财力。Ensure that there are enough sampling points to fully represent the soil characteristics of the sampling unit. The number of sampling points depends on the size of the research scope, the complexity of the research object and the precision required by the experimental research. If the number of sampling points is too small, the contingency of the sampled products will increase and the representation will be insufficient. If the sampling points are too many, the workload of sampling will be increased and the human, material and financial resources will be wasted.

---

# 一、土壤水分 Water content

• 土壤水分是土壤生物生长必须的物质，不是污染组分。但无论是用新鲜土样还是风干土样测定污染组分时，都需要测定土壤含水量，以便计算按烘干土样为基准的测定结果。Water content is a necessary substance for soil biological growth, not a contaminated component. Whether fresh or air-dried soil samples are used to determine the contaminated components, soil water content needs to be measured to calculate the results based on the drying soil samples.

• 样品在105 ℃烘干、称重、计算。The samples were dried, weighed and calculated at 105°C.

| 含水率（% | = 风干土样 - 烘干土样 | ¥100 | ） |
| ----- | ------------- | ---- | - |
| 烘干土样  |               |      |   |

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 73】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 73】

# 二、pH值：玻璃电极法pH: Glass electrode method

• 测定要点：称取通过1 mm孔径筛的土样10 g于烧杯中，加无二氧化碳蒸馏水25 mL，轻轻摇动后用电磁搅拌器搅拌1 min，使水和土充分混合均匀，放置30 min，用pH计测量上部浑浊液的pH值。Weigh 10 g of soil sample that can pass through 1-mm sieve into beaker, then add 25 mL distilled water without carbon dioxide, shake and stir for 1 minute with electromagnetic stirrer, so that water and soil are fully mixed, stand for 30 minutes. Measure the pH value of turbid liquid of upper part with pH meter.

• 土粒的粗细及水、土比例均对pH值有影响。一般酸性土壤的水土比（质量比）保持5:1～1:1；碱性土壤水土比以1:1或2.5:1为宜，水土比增加，测得pH值偏高。The size of soil particles and the ratio of water to soil have an effect on the pH value. The water-soil ratio (mass ratio) of acidic soil is 5:1-1:1: the ratio of water to soil in the alkaline soil is preferably 1:1 or 2.5:1. When the water-soil ratio increases, the pH value will be higher.

• 风干土壤和潮湿土壤测得的pH有差异，尤其是石灰性土壤，由于风干作用，使土壤中大量二氧化碳损失，导致pH偏高，因此风干土壤的pH为相对值。The pH values of air-dried soil are different from those of wet soil, especially for calcareous soil. Because of air-drying, a large amount of carbon dioxide is lost in the soil, which leads to high pH. Therefore, the pH of air-dried soil is a relative value.

# 三、可溶性盐分

• 用一定量的水从一定量土壤中经一定时间浸提出来的水溶性盐分； Water-soluble salts extracted from a certain amount of soil with a certain amount of water for a certain period of time

• 测定方法有重量法、比重计法、电导法、阴阳离子总和计算法等。The determination methods include gravimetric method, densimeter method, conductivity method, Anion-cation sum calculation, etc.

# 五、有机化合物测定

# (一) 六六六和滴滴涕 C₆H₆Cl₆ and DDT

六六六和滴滴涕属于高毒性、高生物活性的有机氯农药，在土壤中残留时间长，其半衰期为2-4年。C₆H₆Cl₆ and DDT belong to organochlorine pesticides with high toxicity and biological activity. They remain in soil for a long time and their half-life is 2-4 years.

# ⮚危害Harmfulness：

土壤被六六六和滴滴涕污染后，对土壤生物会产生直接毒害，并通过生物积累和食物链进入人体，危害人体健康。Soil contaminated by C₆H₆Cl₆ and DDT will cause direct toxicity to soil organisms and enter the human body through biological accumulation and food chain, endangering human health.

# ⮚测定方法Determination method：

广泛使用气相色谱法。其最低检出限质量分数为0.05~4.87ug/kg。Gas chromatography is widely used. The minimum detection limit mass fraction was 0.05-0.87 ug/kg.

土样中有机物萃取 色谱法测定 (电子捕获检测器—ECD检测器)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 79】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 79】

五、有机化合物测定

# Determination of Organic Compounds

# 1. 方法原理 principle

用丙酮-石油醚提取土壤样品中的六六六和滴滴涕，经硫酸净化处理后，用带电子捕获检测器的气相色谱仪测定。根据色谱峰进行两种物质异构体的定性分析；根据峰高(或峰面积)进行各组分的定量分析。

C₆H₆Cl₆ and DDT in soil samples were extracted with acetone-petroleum ether and purified with sulfuric acid, then determined by gas chromatography with electron capture detector. Qualitative analysis of isomers of two substances was carried out according to chromatographic peaks, and quantitative analysis of each component was carried out according to peak height (or peak area).

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 80】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 80】

# 2. 仪器及条件 Instruments and conditions

# 主要仪器 Instrument :

带电子捕获检测器的气相色谱仪；全玻璃进样器；长1.8～2.0m，内径2～3mm螺旋状硬质玻璃填充柱。

Gas chromatography with electron capture detector; full glass injector; spiral hard glass packed column with a length of 1.8 to 2.0 m and an inner diameter of 2 to 3 mm.

# 色谱条件 Chromatographic conditions：

- 固定液：1.5%OV-17(甲基硅酮) +1.95%QF-1(氟代烷基硅氧烷聚合物)；80～100目 Chromosorb W AW-DMCS担体；
- Fixation solution: 1.5% OV-17 (methyl silicone) + 1.95% QF-1 (fluoroalkylsiloxane polymer); 80-100 mesh Chromosorb W AW-DMCS support;
- 气化室温度：220℃； Gasification chamber temperature: 220℃
- 柱温：195℃； Column temperature: 195 ℃
- 载气(N₂)流速：40～70mL/min
- Carrier gas (N₂) flow rate: 40 ~ 70mL / min

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 81】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 81】

# 3. 测定要点 Measurement points

# 样品预处理 Sample pretreatment

- 准确称取20 g土样，先用石油醚-丙酮(1:1)于索氏提取器中提取，再用浓硫酸和无水硫酸钠净化。 Accurately weigh 20 g of soil sample, extract it in Soxhlet extractor with petroleum ether-acetone (1:1), and then purify it with concentrated sulfuric acid and anhydrous sodium sulfate.

# 定性和定量分析 Qualitative and quantitative analysis

- 用色谱纯α-六六六、β-六六六、γ-六六六、δ-六六六、p,p´-DDE、o,p´-DDT、p,p´- DDD、p,p´- DDT和异辛烷、石油醚配制标准工作溶液；分别吸取标液和样品试液进样，记录标液和样品色谱图，根据保留时间定性、峰高(或峰面积)定量。用外标法计算土壤样品中农药含量。
- Standard solution was prepared with chromatographically pure α-C₆H₆Cl₆，β-C₆H₆Cl₆，γ-C₆H₆Cl₆，δ-C₆H₆Cl₆，p,p´-DDE、o,p´-DDT、p,p´- DDD、p,p´-DDT，isooctane and petroleum ether. Standard solution and sample solution were sampled separately. Standard solution and sample chromatogram were recorded. Qualitative analysis was based on retention time, and peak height (or peak area) was used for quantitative analysis. The pesticide content in soil samples was calculated by external standard method.

---

> 【来源：Chapter 5 · Lecture 10 · Slide 82】

# (二) 苯并(a)芘的测定

# Determination of benzo(a)pyrene

# 测定方法 methods：

- 紫外分光光度法 UV spectrophotometry
- 荧光分光光度法 Fluorescence spectrophotometry
- 高效液相色谱法 High performance liquid chromatography

紫外分光光度法适于苯并(a)芘含量> 5μg/kg的土壤，

如苯并(a)芘含量&#x3C;5μg/kg，则用荧光分光光度法

Ultraviolet spectrophotometry is suitable for soils with a benzo(a)pyrene content of >5 μg/kg.

If the benzo(a)pyrene content is &#x3C; 5μg/kg, fluorescence spectrophotometry should be used.

# 无机元素

Inorganic elements：

- ◆  X射线荧光光谱仪（XRF）非破坏样品、直接测量
- ◆  X-ray fluorescence spectrometer, does not damage the samples, for direct measurement
- ◆  样品消解之后测定
- ◆  Determination after sample digestion:

# 样品组分：

Composition:

- ◆  X射线衍射仪（XRD）非破坏样品、直接测量
- ◆  X-ray diffractometer (XRD), does not damage the samples, for direct measurement

# 痕量样品含量及形态：同步辐射光源

# Trace sample content and morphology: synchrotron radiation source

# 同步辐射高分辨X射线衍射

大大提高分辨率，使靠近的衍射线得以分开，增加衍射线数量，因而增加了解出晶体结构的可能。

Synchrotron high-resolution X-ray diffraction: greatly increases the resolution, allowing the adjacent diffraction lines to be separated, increasing the number of diffraction lines, thus increasing the possibility of understanding the crystal structure.

# 衍射异常精细结构 (diffraction anomalous fine structure, DAFS)

衍射强度随入射X射线能量变化而引起的振荡，获得特定位置某种元素周围原子的局部精细结构。

Diffraction anomalous fine structure (DAFS): The oscillation caused by the change of the intensity of the incident X-ray energy to obtain the local fine structure of the atoms around a certain element at a specific position.

# 同步辐射XRF

微区及微量元素分析

Synchrotron Radiation XRF: Micro-area and trace element analysis

# 三维无损分析

同步辐射X射线荧光扫描和成像方法

3D non-destructive analysis: synchrotron radiation X-ray fluorescence scanning and imaging methods

# 7.1.1 声音和噪声 Sound and noise

# 一、声音的定义 Definition of sound

声音的本质是波动。受作用的空气发生振动，当振动频率在20～20000Hz时，作用于人的耳鼓膜而产生的感觉称为声音。 Sound is wave motion. When the frequency of air vibration is within the range of 20-20000z, the feeling produced by the human eardrum is called sound.

# 二、噪声及其主要危害 Noise and its main hazards

噪声：人们生活和工作所不需要的声音叫噪声。 Noise: The sound which is not needed in people's life and work is called noise.

主要危害：损伤听力、干扰人们的睡眠和工作，诱发疾病，干扰语言交流，强噪声还会影响设备正常运转和损环建筑结构。 The main influence: damage to hearing, interfere with people's sleep and work, diseases, interfere with language communication. Strong noise will also affect the normal operation of the equipment and damage to the building structure.

---
