# 7.1.2 声音的物理特性和量度

# Physical properties and measurement of sound

# 一、声音的发生、频率、波长和声速

# Generation, frequency, wavelength and speed of sound

# 1. 声音的发生 generation

当物体在空气中振动，使周围空气发生疏、密交替变化并向外传递，且这种振动频率在20～20000Hz之间，人耳可以感觉，称为可听声，简称声音。When the object vibrates in the air, the surrounding air is sparsely and densely changed and transmitted outwards. When the vibration frequency is between 20 and 20,000 Hz, human can feel it, which is called audible sound, referred to as sound.

次声——频率低于20Hz infrasound: frequency below 20Hz

超声——频率高于20000Hz ultrasound: frequency higher than 20000Hz

# 2. 频率 frequency

声源在一秒钟内振动的次数叫频率，记作f，单位为Hz。 The number of times the sound source vibrates in one second is called the frequency, which is denoted as f, and the unit is Hz.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 10】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 10】

# 一、声音的发生、频率、波长和声速

# 3. 波长 wavelength

沿声波传播方向，振动一个周期所传播的距离，或在波形上相位相同的相邻两点间的距离称为波长，用λ表示，单位为m。

The wavelength is the spatial period of a periodic wave—the distance over which the wave's shape repeats, expressed in λ and the unit is m.

# 4. 声速 sound speed

一秒时间内声波传播的距离叫声波速度，简称声速，记作c，单位为m/s。

The distance of sound wave propagation in one second is called sound wave speed, which is also called sound speed, and is recorded as c, and the unit is m/s.

# 频率、波长和声速三者的关系

The relationship between frequency, wavelength and sound speed :

| c | = | λ | f |
| - | - | - | - |

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 11】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 11】

# 二、声功率、声强和声压

# Sound Power, Sound Intensity and Sound Pressure

# 1.声功率 (W) Sound Power

声功率是指单位时间内，声波通过垂直于传播方向某指定面积的声能量。在噪声监测中，声功率是指声源总声功率，单位为W。Sound power refers to the sound energy of the sound wave passing through a specified area perpendicular to the direction of propagation per unit time. In noise monitoring, the sound power refers to the total sound power of the sound source, and the unit is W.

# 2.声强 (I) Sound Intensity

声强是指单位时间内，声波通过垂直于声波传播方向单位面积的声能量，单位为W/m²。 Sound intensity refers to the sound energy of per unit area when sound waves pass through the area which is perpendicular to the direction of sound wave propagation. The unit is W/m².

# 3.声压 (p) Sound Pressure

声压是由于声波的存在而引起的压力增值。声波是空气分子有指向、有节律的运动。声压单位为Pa。Sound pressure is a pressure increase due to the presence of sound waves. Sound waves are directional, regular movements of air molecules. The unit is Pa.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 12】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 12】

# 三、分贝、声功率级、声强级和声压级

# Decibel, sound power level, sound intensity level, sound pressure level

# 1. 分贝decibel

分贝是指两个相同的物理量(如A₁和A₀) 之比取以10为底的对数并乘以10(或20)。

Decibel refers to the division of two identical physical quantities (such as A1 and A0) and then take the base 10 logarithms, and then is multiplied by 10 (or 20).

分贝符号为“dB”，它是无量纲的，是噪声测量中很重要的参量。上式中A₀是基准量(或参考量)，A₁是被量度量。被量度量和基准量之比取对数，该对数值称为被量度量的“级”，亦即用对数标度时，所得到的是比值，它代表被量度量比基准量高出多少“级”。

Take "dB“ to denote decibel. A dimensionless and important parameter for noise. A₀ is a reference quantity, and A₁ is a measured quantity. This value is called the “level” of the measured quantity, which means the “levels” of the measured quantity higher than the reference quantity.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 13】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 13】

# 三、分贝、声功率级、声强级和声压级

# Decibel, sound power level, sound intensity level, sound pressure level

# 2. 声功率级 Sound power level

声功率级常用LW表示，定义为：

The sound power level is usually expressed as LW as below:

| LW = 10 lg W |   | W₀ |
| ------------ | - | -- |

式中： LW——声功率级 Sound power level, dB；

W——声功率 Sound power, W；

W₀——基准声功率 Reference sound power, 为10～12W。

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 14】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 14】

# 三、分贝、声功率级、声强级和声压级

# Decibel, sound power level, sound intensity level, sound pressure level

# 3. 声强级 Sound intensity level

声强级常用LI表示，定义为：

The sound intensity level is usually expressed as LI:

LI = 10 lg I

I₀

式中：LI——声强级 sound intensity level, dB；

I——声强 sound intensity, W/m²；

I₀——基准声强 reference sound intensity, 10～12W/m² in air

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 15】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 15】

# 三、分贝、声功率级、声强级和声压级

# Decibel, sound power level, sound intensity level, sound pressure level

# 4. 声压级 Sound pressure level

声压级常用Lₚ表示，定义为：

The Sound pressure level is usually expressed as Lp :

Lp = 10 lg p2 = 20 lg p

p2 p

0 0

式中： Lp——声压级 sound pressure level, dB；

p——声压 sound pressure, Pa；

p0——基准声压 reference sound pressure 。

在空气中规定p0为2×10-5 Pa，该值是正常青年人耳朵刚能听到的1000Hz纯音的声压值。

In the air, p0 is specified as 2×10-5 Pa, which is the sound pressure value of 1000 Hz pure tone that can be heard by normal young people.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 16】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 16】

# 四、噪声的叠加和相减

# Superposition and subtraction of noise

# 1. 噪声的叠加

# Superposition of noise

声能量是可以代数相加的，设两个声源的声功率分别为W₁和W₂，那么总声功率W总=W₁+W₂。而两个声源在某点的声强为 I₁和I₂时，叠加后的总声强I总=I₁+I₂

Sound can be added algebraically. If the sound power of two sources is W₁ and W₂ respectively, the total sound power W equals W₁+W₂. When the sound intensity of two sources at a certain point is I₁ and I₂, the total sound intensity of superposition is I总=I₁+I₂.

声压不能直接相加

Sound pressure cannot be added directly, so the total sound pressure level is:

= p2 + p2 = Lp1 + Lp2

Lp = 10 lg (p2 / p02) = 10 lg(10Lp1/10 + 10Lp2/10)

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 17】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 17】

# 3.0

# 1. 噪声的叠加 Superposition of noise

| ∆L p /dB |   |   |   |   |   |   |   |   |   |    |    |    |    |    |    |
| -------- | - | - | - | - | - | - | - | - | - | -- | -- | -- | -- | -- | -- |
| 1.5      |   |   |   |   |   |   |   |   |   |    |    |    |    |    |    |

图7.1.1  两噪声源的叠加曲线

Figure 7.1.1 Superimposed curve of two noise sources

[例] 两声源作用于某一点的声压级分别为Lₚ₁= 96 dB，Lₚ₂=93 dB， 由于Lₚ₁－Lₚ₂=3 dB，查曲线得ΔLₚ= 1.8 dB，因此Lₚ总＝96 dB +1.8 dB＝97.8 dB。

The sound pressure levels of two sound sources acting at a certain point are Lₚ₁= 96 dB and Lₚ₂=93 dB, respectively. Since Lₚ₁－Lₚ₂=3 dB, ΔLₚ= 1.8 dB is obtained from the curve, so Lₚ,total= 96 dB +1.8 dB = 97.8 dB.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 18】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 18】

# 1. 噪声的叠加 Superposition of noise

掌握了两个声源的叠加，就可以推广到多声源的叠加，只需逐次两两叠加即可，而与叠加次序无关。For the superposition of multiple sound sources, only need to superimpose these sound sources one by one, regardless of the superposition order.

例如，有8个声源作用于一点，声压级分别为 70、75、82、90、93、95、100dB，它们合成的总声压级可以任意次序查图7.1.1的曲线两两叠加而得。任选两种叠加次序如下：

| 叠加次序                 | 叠加次序二          |
| -------------------- | -------------- |
| 70 7075 82 909395100 | 70 70 75 82 90 |
| 73.0                 | 73.0           |
| 77.1                 | 77.1           |
| 83.5                 | 83.5           |
| 90.8                 |                |
| 95.1                 |                |
| 98.0                 |                |
| 102.1（总声压级）          | 94.8           |
|                      | 97.8           |
|                      | 102.1（总声压级）    |

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 19】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 19】

# 2. 噪声的相减 Subtraction of noise

[例]为测定某车间中一台机器的噪声大小，从声级计上测得声级为104 dB，当机器停止工作，测得背景噪声为100dB，求该机器噪声的实际大小。

To determine the noise level of a machine in a workshop, the sound level measured from the sound level meter is 104 dB. When the machine stops working, the background noise is 100 dB, calculate the actual noise of the machine.

| ΔL p /dB | 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6 | 7 | 9 | 10 |
| -------- | - | - | - | - | - | - | - | - | - | -- |

图7.1.2 背景噪声修正曲线

Figure 7.1.2 Background noise correction curve

解：由题可知104 dB是指机器噪声和背景噪声之和(Lₚ)，而背景噪声是100 dB (Lₚ₁)。Lₚ－Lₚ₁=4 dB，从图7.1.2中可查得相应ΔLₚ＝2.2 dB，因此该机器的实际噪声噪级Lₚ₂=Lₚ-ΔLₚ =101.8 dB。

From the title, 104 dB is the sum of machine noise and background noise (Lₚ), and the background noise is 100 dB (Lₚ₁). Lₚ-Lₚ₁ = 4 dB, the corresponding ΔLₚ = 2.2 dB can be found from Figure 7.1.2, so the actual noise noise level of the machine is Lₚ₂ = Lₚ - ΔLₚ = 101.8 dB.

> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 20】

# 五、噪声的物理量和主观听觉的关系

# 1、响度和响度级

# 1). 响度

响度是人耳判别声音由轻到响的强度等级概念，响度的单位叫“宋”（sone），1宋的定义为声压级为40dB，频率为1000Hz，且来自听者正前方的平面波形的强度。如果另一个声音听起来比这个大n倍，则声音的响度为n宋。

Loudness is the concept of the intensity level that the human ears discriminate the sound from soft to loud. The unit of loudness is called "sone", and the 1 song is defined as the strength of a plane waveform whose sound pressure level is 40dB, the frequency is 1000Hz, and is produced right in front of the listener. If another sound sounds is n times larger than this, the loudness of the sound is n sone.

# 2). 响度级

定义1000Hz纯音声压级的分贝值为响度级的数值，任何其他频率的声音，当调节1000Hz纯音的强度使之与这声音一样响时，则这1000Hz纯音的声压级分贝值就定为这一声音的响度级值。响度级的单位叫“方”（phon）。

Define the decibel value of the 1000Hz pure sound pressure level as the loudness level. For sound with other frequencies, we adjust the intensity of the 1000Hz pure sound to make it sound similarly, then the decibel value of the sound pressure level for this 1000Hz pure sound is determined as the loudness level of the measured sound. The unit of the loudness level is called "phon".

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 21】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 21】

# 2、计权声级 Weighted sound level

实际上声源所发生的声音几乎都包含很广的频率范围，为了能用仪器直接反应人的主观响度感觉的评价量，有关人员在噪声测量仪器——声级计中设计了一种特殊的滤波器，叫做计权网络，通过计权网络测得的声压级，并不再是可观的物理量声压级，而叫做计权声压级，简称声级。

Actually, the sounds generated by the sources always contain a wide range of frequencies. To directly respond the evaluation of the subjective loudness feeling of people by the instrument, people designed a special wave filter for the noise measuring instrument, which is called the weighting network. The sound pressure level measured by the weighting network is no longer a considerable physical sound pressure level, but is called as the weighted sound pressure level, referred to as the sound level.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 22】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 22】

# 2、计权声级Weighted sound level

- A计权声级是模拟人耳对 55 dB以下低强度噪声的频率特性。
- A-weighted sound level is the frequency characteristic of the simulated human ear for low-intensity noise below 55 dB.
- B计权声级是模拟55～85 dB的中等强度噪声的频率特性。
- B-weighted sound level is a frequency characteristic that simulates medium-intensity noise of 55 to 85 dB.
- C计权声级是模拟高强度噪声的频率特性。
- C-weighted sound level is the frequency characteristic of simulating high-intensity noise.
- D计权声级是对噪声参量的模拟，专用于飞机噪声的测量。
- D-weighted sound level is a simulation of the noise parameters and is dedicated to the measurement of aircraft noise.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 23】 -->

- 用一个相同时间内声能量与之相等的连续稳定的A声级来表示这段时间内噪声的声级大小The noise level is expressed by a continuous stable A sound level with the same sound energy in the same time.

# 7.1.4 噪声标准 Standards for noise

干扰睡眠和休息的噪声级阈值白天为50 dB，夜间为45 dB，我国提出环境噪声允许范围见表7.1.2。The noise level threshold for disturbing sleep and rest is 50 dB during the day and 45 dB during the night. The allowable range of environmental noise proposed in China is shown in Table 7.1.2.

| 脑力劳动（保证语言清晰度） | 60  | 40  | Metal work       | (Ensure language clarity) |             |
| 睡眠            | 50  | 30  | sleep            |                           |             |

# 7.1.4 噪声标准 Standards for noise

• 环境噪声标准制定的依据是环境基本噪声。各国大多参考ISO推荐的基数（如睡眠为30dB），根据不同时间、不同地区和室内噪声受室外噪声影响的修正值以及本国具体情况来制定。 Environmental noise standard is based on basic environmental noise. Most countries refer to the base recommended by ISO (30 dB for sleep), according to different time, different regions, revised values of indoor noise affected by outdoor noise and the specific situation of their own countries to formulate.

# 表7.1.3 一天不同时间对基数的修正 单位：dB

| 时间 time   | 修正值 Correction value |
| --------- | -------------------- |
| 白天day     | 0                    |
| 晚上evening | -5                   |
| 夜间night   | -10 \~ -15           |

# 7.2  振动及测量方法

# Vibration and its measurement method

• 物体围绕平衡位置往复运动称为振动。Vibration, periodic back-and-forth motion of the particles of an elastic body or medium, commonly resulting when almost any physical system is displaced from its equilibrium condition and allowed to respond to the forces that tend to restore equilibrium.

• 振动是噪声产生的原因。 Vibration is the cause of noise

• 机械设备产生的噪声有两种传播方式：一种是以空气为介质向外传播，称为空气声；另一种是声源直接激发固体构件振动，这种振动以弹性波的形式在建筑物基础、地板、墙壁中传播，并在传播过程中向外辐射噪声，称为固体声。There are two modes for noise propagation: one is to propagate outward through air, which is called air-borne sound; the other is that sound source directly excites the vibration of solid components. This kind of vibration propagates in the form of elastic waves in the building foundation, floor and wall, and radiates noise outward during the propagation process, which is called solid sound.

# 7.3 放射性和辐射监测

# 一、基础知识

# (一) 放射性和辐射

# 1. 放射性

- 是指一种不稳定的原子核(放射性物质)自发的衰变的现象。通常该过程伴随发出能导致电离的辐射(如α、β、γ等放射性)。
- Radioactivity, property exhibited by certain types of matter of emitting energy and subatomic particles spontaneously. It is, in essence, an attribute of individual atomic nuclei. (such as α、β、γ, etc.)
- 在衰变过程中，不稳定的原子核能自发地放出α、β、γ射线，使本身物理和化学性质发生变化的现象，称为“放射性”。
- Radioactivity refers to the particles which are emitted from nuclei as a result of nuclear instability. The most common types of radiation are called alpha, beta, and gamma radiation, but there are several other varieties of radioactive decay.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 32】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 32】

# 7.3 放射性和辐射监测

# Radioactivity and radiation monitoring

# 一、基础知识 Basic knowledge

# (一) 放射性和辐射 Radioactivity and radiation

# 2. 辐射 Radiation

• 是一种特殊的能量传递方式。通常可以分为两大类：即粒子辐射与电磁辐射。Radiation is the emission or transmission of energy in the form of waves or particles through space or through a material medium, including particle radiation and electromagnetic radiation.

• 粒子辐射 Particle radiation ：

• 包括α粒子、β粒子及各种核反应或放射性核素自然衰变过程中放出的高速带电荷粒子及中子等 α particles, β particles, and high-speed charged particles and neutrons emitted during the various nuclear reactions or the natural decay of radionuclides.

• 电磁辐射 Electromagnetic radiation：

• 包括可见光、红外线、紫外线、X射线及γ射线等 Visible light, infrared, ultraviolet, X-ray and γ rays, etc.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 33】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 33】

# 7.3 放射性和辐射监测

# （二）放射性核衰变

# Radioactive nuclear decay

# 1. 核衰变

不稳定的原子核能自发地有规律地改变其结构，从原子核内部放出电磁波（γ）或带有一定能量的粒子（α、β），降低其能级水平，转化为结构稳定的核。这种现象叫核蜕变或“放射性核衰变”。

Radioactive decay is the spontaneous breakdown of an atomic nucleus resulting in the release of energy and matter from the nucleus, releasing electromagnetic waves (γ) or particles with energy (α, β), reducing its energy level and transforming into a structurally stable nuclear.

决定放射性核素性质的基本要素是放射性衰变类型、放射性活度和半衰期。

The basic factors determining the properties of radionuclides are: the type of radioactive decay, the radioactive activity and half-life.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 34】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 34】

# （二）放射性核衰变 Radioactive nuclear decay

# 2. 放射性衰变的类别 Type of radioactive decay

# 1. α衰变α-decay

程。α衰变是不稳定重核（一般原子序数大于82）自发放出⁴He核（α粒子）的过

α  In α-decay, an unstable nucleus disintegrates into a lighter nucleus and an -particle (⁴He)

# 2. β衰变 β-decay

β衰变是放射性核素放射β粒子（即快速电子）的过程，它是原子核内质子和中子发生互变的结果。

β衰变可分为负β衰变、正β衰变和电子俘获三种类型。Β decay is a type of radioactive decay in which β particles are emitted from an atomic nucleus. Beta particles are fast-moving electrons, which are singly charged. They are lighter and ejected at a much higher speed than alpha particles.

# 3. γ衰变γ -decay

γ射线是原子核从较高能级跃迁到较低能级或者基态时所放射的电磁辐射。

Type of radioactivity in which some unstable atomic nuclei dissipate excess energy by a spontaneous electromagnetic process.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 35】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 35】

# （三）放射性活度和半衰期

# 1. 放射性活度（强度）

放射性活度系指单位时间内发生核衰变的数目。 The activity of a sample of radioactive matter is defined by the number of disintegrations taking place at its core per unit time.

# 2. 半衰期

当放射性的核素因衰变而减少到原来的一半时所需的时间称为半衰期（T1/2）。 The interval of time required for one-half of the atomic nuclei of a radioactive sample to decay, or, equivalently, the time interval required for the number of disintegrations per second of a radioactive material to decrease by one-half.

# （四）核反应

核反应：是指用快速粒子打击靶核而给出新核（核产物）和另一粒子的过程。 In nuclear physics, a nuclear reaction is a process in which two nuclei or nuclear particles collide, to produce different products than the initial particles.

They are terms used to characterize the effects and measurements produced by the interaction of radioactive particles with other substances.

- When gamma or X-ray is completely blocked in the air, the total electric quantity of charged particles (positive or negative) produced by air ionization of a volume element with a mass of dm

# 六、 放射性监测 Radioactivity monitoring

# 1、监测对象及内容 Monitoring objects and contents

# 1) 按照监测对象分Classified by monitoring object

| 1.现场监测 On-site monitoring         | 对放射性物质生产或应用单位内部工作区域的监测。 Monitoring of the internal working area of a radioactive material production or application units                                                                   |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 2.个人剂量监测 Personal dose monitoring | 对放射性专业工作人员或公众进行的内照射和外照射的剂量监测。 Dose monitoring of internal and external exposure to radioactive professional staff or the public                                                             |
| 3.环境监测 Environmental monitoring   | 对放射性生产和应用单位外部环境（包括空气、水体、土壤、生物、固体废物等）的监测。 Monitoring of the external environment of radioactive production and application units (including air, water, soil, biological, solid waste, etc.) |

# 六、 放射性监测 Radioactivity monitoring

# 1、监测对象及内容 Monitoring objects and contents

# 2) 按照测定的放射性核素分 Classification by the measured radionuclide

# 1. α放射性核素α radionuclide

3H、90Sr、89Sr、134Cs、137Cs、131I、60Co

# Classification by the measurement content of radionuclides

放射源强度，半衰期，射线种类及能量；环境和人体中放射性物质含量、放射性强度、空间照射量或电离辐射剂量。

Source intensity, half-life, type and energy of radiation; radioactive substances content, radioactivity intensity, space exposure or ionizing radiation dose in the environment and human body.

# 六、 放射性监测

# 2、放射性监测方法

# 定期监测

Regular monitoring：

- 采样、样品预处理、样品总放射性或放射性核素测定。Sampling, pretreatment, total radioactivity or radionuclide determination

# 连续监测

Continuous monitoring：

- 在现场安装放射性自动监测仪器，实现采样、预处理和测定自动化。Radioactive automatic monitoring instruments are installed on site to automate sampling, pre-processing and measurement.

# 基本步骤

The basic steps：

- 样品采集，样品前处理，选择适宜方法、仪器测定。Sampling, pretreatment, selection of appropriate methods, instrument determination.

# 六、 放射性监测 Radioactivity monitoring

# 2、放射性监测方法 Radioactive monitoring methods

# 2). 样品预处理 Sample pretreatment

预处理目的：将样品处理成适于测量的状态，将样品的欲测核素转变成适于测量的形态并进行浓集，以及去除干扰核素。The purpose of pretreatment is to treat the sample into the state which is suitable for measurement, to transform the desired nuclide into the form which is suitable for measurement, to concentrate and to remove interfering nuclides.

# 常用的样品预处理方法 sample pretreatment methods：

- 衰变法 Decay
- 共沉淀法 Coprecipitation
- 灰化法 Ashing
- 电化学法 Electrochemical

# 其他预处理方法 Other pretreatment methods：

- 有机溶剂溶解法 Organic solvent dissolution
- 蒸馏法 Distillation
- 溶剂萃取法 Solvent extraction
- 离子交换法 Ion exchange

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 49】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 49】

六、 放射性监测 Radioactivity monitoring

# 3. 环境中放射性监测 Radioactivity monitoring in the environment

# 1) 水样的总α放射性活度的测定 Total α radioactive activity of water

水体中常见的辐射α粒子的核素有²²⁶Ra、²²²Rn及其衰变产物。目前公认的水样总α放射性浓度是0.1Bq/L，当大于此值时，就应对放射α粒子的核素进行鉴定和测量，确定主要的放射性核素，判断水质污染情况。226Ra、²²²Rn and their decay products are common radiation alpha particles in water. At present, the recognized total α radioactivity concentration in water samples is 0.1Bq/L. When the value is greater than this, the radionuclides of α particles should be identified and measured, the main radionuclides should be determined, and the water pollution situation should be evaluated.

# 2) 水样的总β放射性活度测定 Total β radioactive activity of water

水样总β放射性活度测量步骤基本上与总α放射性活度测定相同，但检测器用低本底的盖革计数管，且以含⁴⁰K的化合物作标准源。 The procedure of measuring total β radioactivity in water samples is basically the same as that of total α radioactivity, and uses a Geiger counter with low background value and compounds containing ⁴⁰K as standard source.

---

<!-- 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 50】 -->
> 【来源：Chapter 6 · Lecture 11 · Slide 50】

# 六、 放射性监测 Radioactivity monitoring

# 3. 环境中放射性监测 Radioactivity monitoring in the environment

# 3) 土壤中总α、β放射性活度的测定 Total α and β radioactive activity in soil

# 4) 大气中氡的测定 Radon in the atmosphere

222Rn是226Ra的衰变产物，为一种放射性惰性气体。它与空气作用时，能使之电离，因而可用电离型探测器通过测量电离电流测定其浓度；也可用闪烁探测器记录由氡衰变时所放出的α粒子来计算其含量。

²²²Rn is the decay product of ²²⁶Ra, which is a kind of radioactive inert gas. When it acts with air, it can ionize it, so its concentration can be determined by measuring ionization current with an ionization detector, and its content can be calculated by recording α particles emitted by radon decay with a scintillation detector.

# 7.4 光污染监测 Light pollution monitoring

光污染定义 Definition

光污₃一般泛指影响自然环境，对人类正常生活、工作、休息和娱乐带来不利影响，损害人们观察物体的能力，引起人体不舒适感和损害人体健康的各种光的污染。

Light pollution that can affect the natural environment and people’s normal life, work, rest and entertainment, and influence people's ability to observe objects, make human feel discomfort and damage human health.

美丽的玻璃光，伤害眼睛的隐形杀手

# 7.4 光污染监测 Light pollution monitoring

光污染的物理量 Physical quantity of light pollution

能否看清一个物体，或能否辨别物体上的细微部分，都与物理表面的被照明程度有关。为了标明物体被照明程度，引进了照度的物理量。

Whether an object can be seen clearly, or whether the fine parts of the object can be distinguished, depends on the degree of illumination on the physical surface. In order to indicate the illumination degree of the object, the physical quantity of illumination is introduced.

照度：反映光强度的一种单位，其物理意义是照射到单位面积上的光通量，单位是勒(克斯 lux)，表示单位面积的光通量，流明(lm)为光通量的单位。

Illumination  A quantity that reflects the intensity of light. Its physical meaning is the luminous flux per unit area. The unit is Lux, which represents the luminous flux per unit area. Lumen (lm) is the unit of luminous flux.
